# sentence candidate comparison

선택 기준: 확정 뜻이 정확하고, 배정된 교과 개념과 단어가 비유·은유 없이 한 장면에서
직접 확인되며, 사실에 맞고 자연스러운 문장을 고른다.

- words: 1200
- authors: codex, antigravity, claude

## DAY 01 · set 1 · 일상생활 / 의식주생활

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY01-01 | chore | 집안일<br>a small job you must do at home | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>의식주생활 장면에서 '집안일'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The robot finished every chore before the family woke up. | The robot completed every chore before the family woke up. | Each family member helps with a daily chore after dinner. | Feeding the rabbit is my easiest chore every Saturday morning. |  |
| DAY01-02 | laundry | 세탁물<br>clothes that need washing or have just been washed | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>의식주생활 장면에서 '세탁물'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A friendly bear carried the laundry basket down the wooden stairs. | A helpful bear carried the laundry basket down the wooden stairs. | He puts the dirty laundry into the basket before washing. | Dad folds the clean laundry into three neat piles. |  |
| DAY01-03 | flavor | 맛<br>the taste that food or drink has | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>의식주생활 장면에서 '맛'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | This ice cream has a sweet strawberry flavor inside. | Today, this ice cream has a sweet strawberry flavor inside. | The sweet strawberry flavor makes the warm pie delicious. | The green tea ice cream has a soft, sweet flavor. |  |
| DAY01-04 | recipe | 조리법<br>steps that tell you how to cook a food | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>의식주생활 장면에서 '조리법'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A young cook follows a recipe to bake round cookies. | A cook checks each recipe step before mixing the batter. | Follow this simple recipe to bake warm bread in the kitchen. | The recipe tells us to mix flour, milk, and eggs. |  |
| DAY01-05 | beverage | 음료<br>a drink, such as juice, milk, or tea | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>의식주생활 장면에서 '음료'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The penguin poured a cold beverage into two tall glasses. | The young penguin poured a cold beverage into two tall glasses. | The waiter serves a cold beverage with lunch at the cafe. | Grandma served a warm beverage in my small blue cup. |  |
| DAY01-06 | operate | 작동시키다<br>to make a machine work | 과학 / 과학과 기술 (Science and Technology)<br>direct<br>문제를 해결할 장치를 설계하고 시제품의 조건과 한계를 시험한다 | Two rabbits operate a tiny train with shiny silver levers. | Students operate their prototype twice and record its limits. | Students operate the robotic arm to test its maximum lifting weight. | Students operate the new model crane to test its lifting limit. |  |
| DAY01-07 | edible | 먹을 수 있는<br>safe to eat | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>의식주생활 장면에서 '먹을 수 있는'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The chef showed which bright flowers are edible and sweet. | A young cook displayed which bright flowers are edible and sweet. | The bright yellow mushroom is completely edible and tastes sweet. | Only the soft inside part of this seed is edible. |  |
| DAY01-08 | shelf | 선반<br>a flat board on a wall for holding things | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>의식주생활 장면에서 '선반'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A sleepy cat naps on the top shelf above books. | One sleepy cat naps on the top shelf above books. | She places clean glass bowls neatly on the wooden shelf. | Two glass jars stand on the kitchen shelf beside the window. |  |
| DAY01-09 | quite | 꽤<br>very, but not completely | fallback<br>word_fallback<br>한 장면에서 '꽤'의 확정 뜻이 다른 뜻으로 오해되지 않게 직접 보여 준다 | The dragon's soup was quite hot, so everyone waited patiently. | Today, the dragon's soup was quite hot, so everyone waited patiently. | The tomato soup was quite hot, so we waited patiently. | This wool blanket feels quite heavy but still keeps us warm. |  |
| DAY01-10 | quiet | 조용한<br>making little or no noise | 국어 / 음운과 문자 체계 (Phonology and writing systems)<br>contextual<br>말소리의 크기와 들림 정도를 비교해 소리의 특징을 설명한다 | The library stayed quiet while the little owl read books. | Students compare a quiet voice with a loud voice. | A quiet sound has lower loudness than a loud shouted word. | A whisper is quiet, while a shout carries across the room. |  |

## DAY 01 · set 2 · 일상생활 / 의식주생활

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY01-11 | sweep | 쓸다<br>to clean a floor with a broom | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>의식주생활 장면에서 '쓸다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The elephant used its trunk to sweep the dusty porch. | The young elephant used its trunk to sweep the dirty porch. | We sweep the kitchen floor with a broom after cooking. | After dinner I sweep crumbs from the wooden floor. |  |
| DAY01-12 | glance | 흘끗 보다<br>to look at something very quickly | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>의식주생활 장면에서 '흘끗 보다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | She stopped to glance at the shiny toy in the window. | She stopped to glance at the gleaming toy in the window. | The boy can glance at the clock during his breakfast. | I glance at the wall clock before setting the table. |  |
| DAY01-13 | match | 어울리다<br>to look good together | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>의식주생활 장면에서 '어울리다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Those yellow boots match the color of the raincoat perfectly. | Today, those yellow boots match the color of the raincoat perfectly. | These blue curtains match the sofa in the living room. | These brown shoes match the warm winter coat very well. |  |
| DAY01-14 | furniture | 가구<br>chairs, tables, and beds inside a home | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>의식주생활 장면에서 '가구'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The mice carried tiny furniture into their cozy tree house. | The small mice carried tiny furniture into their comfortable tree house. | The workers moved wooden furniture into the new apartment room. | Movers carried light furniture into the empty living room today. |  |
| DAY01-15 | manual | 설명서<br>a book that explains how to use something | 국어 / 정보 전달 글쓰기 (Informative writing)<br>direct<br>독자가 절차나 사실을 이해하도록 정확한 정보를 순서 있게 쓴다 | The robot read a thick manual before building the shelf. | Before building the shelf, the robot read a thick manual. | The instruction manual clearly explains each step of assembling the chair. | The rice cooker manual lists each step in the correct order. |  |
| DAY01-16 | value | 가치<br>how much something is worth | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>의식주생활 장면에서 '가치'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The old coin has great value to the young collector. | Today, the old coin has great value to the young collector. | The antique wooden table has high market value today. | The value of this old wooden chair rose after careful repair. |  |
| DAY01-17 | pot | 냄비<br>a deep round container used for cooking | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>의식주생활 장면에서 '냄비'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A big pot of soup bubbled slowly on the stove. | A large pot of soup bubbled gently on the stove. | A large metal pot boils soup on the kitchen stove. | Steam rises from the pot of barley tea on the stove. |  |
| DAY01-18 | grocery | 식료품<br>food and drinks you buy at a store | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>의식주생활 장면에서 '식료품'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The fox filled a grocery bag with apples and bread. | The red fox filled a grocery bag with apples and bread. | Father buys fresh grocery items at the neighborhood supermarket. | Milk, eggs, and bread fill our grocery bag this evening. |  |
| DAY01-19 | striped | 줄무늬의<br>having lines of different colors | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>의식주생활 장면에서 '줄무늬의'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A striped tiger cub rolled happily across the soft grass. | A striped tiger cub rolled cheerfully across the soft grass. | The girl wears a striped apron while preparing the salad. | A striped towel hangs neatly beside the bathroom mirror. |  |
| DAY01-20 | mind | 마음<br>the part of a person that thinks and remembers | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>의식주생활 장면에서 '마음'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A student holds a puzzle while ideas fill his mind. | While ideas fill his mind, a student holds a puzzle. | A peaceful mind helps you make thoughtful decisions every day. | My mind still remembers the smell of grandmother's warm kitchen. |  |

## DAY 01 · set 3 · 일상생활 / 의식주생활

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY01-21 | receipt | 영수증<br>a small paper showing what you paid for | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>의식주생활 장면에서 '영수증'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The clerk handed a long receipt to the smiling shopper. | The clerk handed a long receipt to the happy shopper. | The cashier handed me a printed receipt for the shoes. | The store clerk handed me a receipt for two bread loaves. |  |
| DAY01-22 | bitter | 쓴<br>having a sharp taste that is not sweet | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>의식주생활 장면에서 '쓴'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The tiny bear frowned at the bitter green tea leaves. | The small bear frowned at the bitter green tea leaves. | This herbal tea tastes bitter without any added honey. | This dark chocolate tastes bitter, not sweet at all. |  |
| DAY01-23 | appearance | 외모<br>the way someone or something looks | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>의식주생활 장면에서 '외모'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The puppy changed its appearance with a bright party hat. | The young dog changed its appearance with a colorful party hat. | Her neat appearance made a positive impression at the event. | A new haircut changed the appearance of our friendly neighbor. |  |
| DAY01-24 | iron | 다리미<br>a hot tool that makes clothes flat and smooth | 과학 / 열전도 (Conduction)<br>contextual<br>뜨거운 다리미에서 천으로 열이 전도되어 주름이 펴지는 모습을 관찰한다 | A robot pressed the wrinkled shirt with a warm iron. | Heat from the iron moves into cloth and smooths wrinkles. | Heat moves from the hot iron to smooth the shirt. | Heat moves from the hot iron into the shirt, flattening wrinkles. |  |
| DAY01-25 | palm | 손바닥<br>the inside flat part of your hand | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>의식주생활 장면에서 '손바닥'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A tiny bird sat safely on the boy's open palm. | A small bird sat safely on the boy's open palm. | The chef held the round dough flat on his palm. | Warm rice sits in my palm before I shape it. |  |
| DAY01-26 | fancy | 화려한<br>very decorated and not simple | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>의식주생활 장면에서 '화려한'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The mouse wore a fancy hat covered in silver ribbons. | Today, the mouse wore a fancy hat covered in silver ribbons. | The dining room was decorated with fancy gold plates. | The dining table looks fancy with lace cloth and gold plates. |  |
| DAY01-27 | soak | 담그다<br>to leave something in water for a while | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>의식주생활 장면에서 '담그다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The chef let the beans soak inside a big bowl. | A young cook let the beans soak inside a large bowl. | You should soak the dried beans in warm water overnight. | Before washing, we soak the dirty socks in soapy water. |  |
| DAY01-28 | appeal | 마음을 끌다<br>to be liked or wanted by someone | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>의식주생활 장면에서 '마음을 끌다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Bright colors appeal to young children at the paint shop. | Colorful fruit bowls appeal to children choosing a healthy snack. | Fresh fruit desserts appeal to many people in summer. | Soft cotton pajamas appeal to shoppers who want comfortable sleep. |  |
| DAY01-29 | compare | 비교하다<br>to look at how things are same or different | 국어 / 설명과 논증 (Explanation and argument)<br>contextual<br>두 대상을 같은 기준으로 비교해 공통점과 차이점을 설명한다 | The twins compare two seashells to find the bigger one. | Students compare two maps and explain their shared symbols. | We compare two leaves to find their similarities and differences. | We compare two winter coats by warmth and name one difference. |  |
| DAY01-30 | compel | 강요하다<br>to make someone do something | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>의식주생활 장면에서 '강요하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The rules compel every player to move only one space. | Today, the rules compel every player to move only one space. | Strict parents should not compel their children to finish everything. | Heavy rain can compel us to eat lunch inside today. |  |

## DAY 01 · set 4 · 일상생활 / 의식주생활

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY01-31 | browse | 둘러보다<br>to look at many things slowly | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>의식주생활 장면에서 '둘러보다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The raccoon likes to browse the shelves of old books. | The raccoon often chooses to browse the shelves of old books. | Shoppers browse clothing racks to find warm winter coats. | Shoppers browse the market tables before choosing fresh vegetables. |  |
| DAY01-32 | appetite | 식욕<br>the feeling of wanting to eat | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>의식주생활 장면에서 '식욕'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The hungry puppy had a huge appetite after the walk. | After the walk, the hungry puppy had a huge appetite. | Walking outdoors before dinner gave him a strong appetite. | After the long walk, my appetite grew, so I ate quickly. |  |
| DAY01-33 | attach | 붙이다<br>to join one thing to another | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>의식주생활 장면에서 '붙이다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The child will attach paper wings to the toy rocket. | A student will attach paper wings to the toy rocket. | Please attach the address label firmly to the cardboard box. | I attach a small name tag to my lunch box. |  |
| DAY01-34 | shut | 닫다<br>to close something | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>의식주생활 장면에서 '닫다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Please shut the window before the cold evening air arrives. | Kindly shut the window before the cold evening air arrives. | Remember to shut the kitchen windows when cooking is finished. | Please shut the refrigerator door so cold air stays inside. |  |
| DAY01-35 | tender | 부드러운<br>soft and easy to cut or chew | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>의식주생활 장면에서 '부드러운'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The stew had tender carrots that melted in every bite. | Today, the stew had tender carrots that melted in every bite. | The roasted meat was tender and easy to chew. | Slow cooking made the potatoes tender enough for a spoon. |  |
| DAY01-36 | pour | 붓다<br>to make liquid flow out of a container | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>의식주생활 장면에서 '붓다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The rabbit began to pour warm milk into small cups. | The small rabbit started to pour warm milk into small cups. | Carefully pour hot tea into the ceramic mug. | The cook will pour warm broth into each small bowl. |  |
| DAY01-37 | lean | 기대다<br>to bend or rest against something | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>의식주생활 장면에서 '기대다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The tired hiker will lean against a tall pine tree. | The weary hiker will lean against a tall pine tree. | Do not lean your wet umbrella against the white wall. | Tired hikers lean against the wooden wall of the guesthouse. |  |
| DAY01-38 | temporary | 임시의<br>lasting only a short time | fallback<br>word_fallback<br>한 장면에서 '임시의'의 확정 뜻이 다른 뜻으로 오해되지 않게 직접 보여 준다 | The snow castle was a temporary home for the penguins. | Today, the snow castle was a temporary home for the penguins. | The workers built a temporary wooden bridge across the stream. | This temporary shelter will stand for only three rainy days. |  |
| DAY01-39 | cottage | 오두막<br>a small house in the countryside | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>의식주생활 장면에서 '오두막'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A tiny cottage stood beside a quiet blue mountain lake. | A small cottage stood beside a quiet blue mountain lake. | The quiet cottage stands near the edge of the forest. | A small stone cottage sits quietly among the green hills. |  |
| DAY01-40 | minute | 분<br>sixty seconds of time | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>의식주생활 장면에서 '분'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The egg timer rang after exactly one more quiet minute. | After exactly one more quiet minute, the egg timer rang. | Waiting for one minute allows the hot tea to cool. | We wait one more minute before taking the bread out. |  |

## DAY 02 · set 1 · 일상생활 / 학교생활

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY02-01 | active | 활발한<br>moving around a lot and full of energy | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>학교생활 장면에서 '활발한'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The active puppy ran across the field all morning. | Today, the active puppy ran across the field all morning. | The active students run across the soccer field during gym class. | Active first graders run and jump during the morning break. |  |
| DAY02-02 | excellent | 훌륭한<br>very, very good | fallback<br>word_fallback<br>한 장면에서 '훌륭한'의 확정 뜻이 다른 뜻으로 오해되지 않게 직접 보여 준다 | The young painter did an excellent job on the mural. | Today, the young painter did an excellent job on the mural. | Her excellent science presentation won first prize at the school fair. | The class chorus sounded excellent after four weeks of practice. |  |
| DAY02-03 | lecture | 강의<br>a talk that teaches people about one subject | 국어 / 발표 (Presentation)<br>direct<br>청중 앞에서 핵심 내용을 순서 있게 말하고 자료로 설명한다 | The owl gave a lecture about stars to young students. | The speaker gives a lecture using three pictures in order. | The guest teacher gave a fascinating lecture about ancient ocean fossils. | A guest scientist gave a lecture with slides about ocean animals. |  |
| DAY02-04 | academic | 학업의<br>about school study and learning | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>학교생활 장면에서 '학업의'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The robot won a prize for its academic work. | A robot won a prize for its academic work. | Students focus on their academic studies to prepare for high school. | My academic goal this year is reading thirty English books. |  |
| DAY02-05 | encourage | 격려하다<br>to give someone hope and courage to try | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>학교생활 장면에서 '격려하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The coach will encourage every runner before the big race. | A sports teacher will encourage every runner before the large race. | Teachers encourage young learners to ask curious questions during daily class. | Classmates clap to encourage the runner who finished last. |  |
| DAY02-06 | debate | 토론<br>a talk where two sides share different ideas | 국어 / 토론과 반박 (Debate and rebuttal)<br>direct<br>주장과 근거를 제시하고 상대 주장에 근거로 반박한다 | Two teams held a friendly debate about the best season. | Each debate team answers the other's claim with evidence. | The class held a lively debate about public library funding rules. | In the debate, one side answered the other side's reason with evidence. |  |
| DAY02-07 | president | 회장<br>the leader of a country or a group | 사회 / 사회적 지위와 사회적 역할 (Social Status and Social Role)<br>direct<br>한 사람이 가진 지위와 그에 기대되는 역할을 구별한다 | The class chose a rabbit as their new club president. | The club president leads meetings and listens to members. | The class president introduced the new student to everyone this morning. | The class president has one title but many duties, including leading meetings. |  |
| DAY02-08 | assignment | 과제<br>work that a teacher gives you to finish | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>학교생활 장면에서 '과제'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The students carried their science assignment into the bright classroom. | The students carried their science assignment into the colorful classroom. | The teacher collected our weekly math assignment at nine o'clock. | Our math assignment asks us to finish ten problems tonight. |  |
| DAY02-09 | principal | 교장<br>the leader of a school | 사회 / 사회적 지위와 사회적 역할 (Social Status and Social Role)<br>direct<br>한 사람이 가진 지위와 그에 기대되는 역할을 구별한다 | The friendly principal welcomed everyone at the school front gate. | The principal manages school rules and supports teachers. | The middle school principal welcomed all new students in the auditorium. | A school principal welcomes new students and signs official letters. |  |
| DAY02-10 | principle | 원칙<br>a rule you believe and always follow | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>학교생활 장면에서 '원칙'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Sharing fairly is an important principle in every team game. | Today, sharing fairly is an important principle in every team game. | Fairness is an essential moral principle in our school community. | Telling the truth is a principle our class follows daily. |  |

## DAY 02 · set 2 · 일상생활 / 학교생활

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY02-11 | graduate | 졸업하다<br>to finish school and get a certificate | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>학교생활 장면에서 '졸업하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The young bears will graduate from forest school this spring. | Today, the young bears will graduate from forest school this spring. | Senior students will graduate from middle school this coming February. | Sixth graders graduate in February and receive their certificates. |  |
| DAY02-12 | sense | 감각<br>a power like sight, hearing, taste, touch, or smell | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>학교생활 장면에서 '감각'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A dog uses its sense of smell to find treats. | One dog uses its sense of smell to find treats. | Humans use the sense of sight to observe bright colors clearly. | Blindfolded students used the sense of touch to name objects. |  |
| DAY02-13 | real | 진짜의<br>true and not pretend | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>학교생활 장면에서 '진짜의'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The magic flower looked fake but was completely real. | Today, the magic flower looked fake but was completely real. | The museum displayed a real fossil found in the local mountain. | That dinosaur bone in our classroom is a copy, not real. |  |
| DAY02-14 | annual | 연례의<br>happening one time every year | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>학교생활 장면에서 '연례의'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The village holds an annual kite festival every warm spring. | Today, the village holds an annual kite festival every warm spring. | Our school holds an annual sports festival every pleasant autumn. | Our school holds an annual sports day each October. |  |
| DAY02-15 | collect | 모으다<br>to gather many things and keep them together | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>학교생활 장면에서 '모으다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The squirrel likes to collect shiny acorns in one basket. | The squirrel often chooses to collect shiny acorns in one basket. | Students collect dried fallen leaves for their science classification project. | Volunteers collect used paper from each classroom on Fridays. |  |
| DAY02-16 | deadline | 마감 기한<br>the last day something must be finished | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>학교생활 장면에서 '마감 기한'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The team finished the robot model before the class deadline. | The club completed the robot model before the class deadline. | The final project deadline is Friday afternoon at five o'clock. | This book report deadline is Friday, so I started today. |  |
| DAY02-17 | confident | 자신 있는<br>sure that you can do something well | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>학교생활 장면에서 '자신 있는'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The small penguin felt confident before its first swimming lesson. | Before its first swimming lesson, the small penguin felt confident. | She felt confident before giving her speech to the whole class. | The singer felt confident about the solo after two rehearsals. |  |
| DAY02-18 | include | 포함하다<br>to have something as one of its parts | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>학교생활 장면에서 '포함하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Our lunch boxes include fruit, bread, and a small cheese. | Today, our lunch boxes include fruit, bread, and a small cheese. | Make sure to include your name on the test paper. | The field trip fee will include lunch and bus tickets. |  |
| DAY02-19 | shout | 외치다<br>to say something in a very loud voice | 국어 / 음운과 문자 체계 (Phonology and writing systems)<br>contextual<br>말소리의 크기와 들림 정도를 비교해 소리의 특징을 설명한다 | The children shout happily when the parade turns the corner. | Students shout once, then compare how far voices travel. | Children shout with joy when the final school bell rings. | Players shout across the field because normal talking cannot reach there. |  |
| DAY02-20 | subject | 과목<br>something you study at school, like math | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>학교생활 장면에서 '과목'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Science is the favorite subject of the curious young fox. | Science is the subject where students investigate plants and forces. | Science is my favorite school subject because we do fun experiments. | History is the subject I study right after lunch. |  |

## DAY 02 · set 3 · 일상생활 / 학교생활

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY02-21 | participate | 참여하다<br>to join others in doing an activity | 사회 / 민주주의와 시민성 (Democracy and Citizenship)<br>direct<br>시민이 자유와 평등을 지키며 공공 문제 결정에 참여한다 | Every student can participate in the fun school sports day. | Citizens participate by voting on a new park plan. | Many pupils participate actively in the after-school music club. | Students participate in the class vote about the field trip place. |  |
| DAY02-22 | auditorium | 강당<br>a big room with many seats for shows | 국어 / 발표 (Presentation)<br>contextual<br>강당의 청중을 고려해 발표 자료와 말의 크기를 조절한다 | The choir sang inside the large school auditorium last night. | The speaker adjusts her voice for listeners across the auditorium. | The choir practiced their song inside the spacious school auditorium. | Presenters speak louder in the auditorium than in a small classroom. |  |
| DAY02-23 | inform | 알리다<br>to tell someone something they need to know | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>학교생활 장면에서 '알리다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A bright sign will inform visitors about the new exhibit. | One bright sign will inform visitors about the new exhibit. | The notice will inform parents about the upcoming field trip schedule. | A morning notice will inform parents about tomorrow's early dismissal. |  |
| DAY02-24 | duty | 임무<br>something you must do as your job | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>학교생활 장면에서 '임무'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Watering the class plants is the duty of one helper. | Today, watering the class plants is the duty of one helper. | Cleaning the whiteboard is his daily duty after homeroom ends. | Watering the classroom plants is my duty this week. |  |
| DAY02-25 | evaluate | 평가하다<br>to look at something and decide how good it is | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>학교생활 장면에서 '평가하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The judges evaluate each paper airplane before choosing a winner. | Before choosing a winner, the judges evaluate each paper airplane. | Teachers evaluate student essays using clear criteria for grammar and ideas. | Teachers evaluate each poster using three points on a checklist. |  |
| DAY02-26 | minimum | 최소의<br>the smallest amount that is allowed | 수학 / 유리수의 대소 비교 (Comparing and Ordering Rational Numbers)<br>contextual<br>여러 수를 비교해 가장 작은 값과 가장 큰 값을 찾는다 | Each player needs a minimum score to join the next round. | Students compare four scores and circle the minimum value. | The test requires a minimum passing score of sixty points. | We circled the minimum value among five numbers on the board. |  |
| DAY02-27 | introduction | 소개, 도입<br>the act of telling people someone’s name or presenting something | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>학교생활 장면에서 '소개, 도입'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | She gave a brief introduction to Chopin's life and works. | She offered a brief introduction to Chopin's life and works. | The chapter begins with a brief introduction to modern geography. | Our club leader began with a short introduction of new members. |  |
| DAY02-28 | photograph | 사진<br>a picture made with a camera | 국어 / 복합양식 읽기 (Multimodal reading)<br>contextual<br>글과 사진·그림이 주는 정보를 함께 읽고 서로 보완하는지 확인한다 | A photograph of the smiling team hung above the door. | A photograph shows details that the article does not explain. | She took a clear photograph of the microscopic plant cell. | The report text and its photograph together explain how seeds sprout. |  |
| DAY02-29 | council | 의회<br>a group chosen to make plans together | 사회 / 대의 민주주의와 참여 민주주의 (Representative and Participatory Democracy)<br>direct<br>대표를 뽑는 방식과 시민이 직접 의견을 내는 방식을 비교한다 | The student council planned a garden beside the school playground. | Voters choose a council, then citizens speak at meetings. | The student council organized a recycling campaign across the campus. | Our student council members are elected, but anyone may send suggestions. |  |
| DAY02-30 | conceal | 숨기다<br>to hide something so nobody can see it | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>학교생활 장면에서 '숨기다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The clever fox tried to conceal a gift behind flowers. | Today, the clever fox tried to conceal a gift behind flowers. | You should not conceal your questions when studying difficult math topics. | Two friends conceal the birthday gift behind the classroom bookshelf. |  |

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY02-31 | session | 시간<br>a set time when people do one activity | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>학교생활 장면에서 '시간'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The morning painting session lasted almost one happy hour. | Today, the morning painting session lasted almost one happy hour. | Our group completed an intensive study session in the library. | A morning reading session lasts thirty minutes in our library. |  |
| DAY02-32 | strict | 엄격한<br>making people follow rules very carefully | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>학교생활 장면에서 '엄격한'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The strict but kind teacher checked every desk before lunch. | Before lunch, the strict but kind teacher checked every desk. | The strict librarian asked everyone to whisper in the reading room. | Our librarian is strict about returning books on the due date. |  |
| DAY02-33 | twist | 비틀다<br>to turn something around with your hands | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>학교생활 장면에서 '비틀다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | You can twist the cap to open the juice bottle. | Today, you can twist the cap to open the juice bottle. | Be careful not to twist the wire while connecting circuits. | Please twist the cap slowly to open the glue bottle. |  |
| DAY02-34 | spill | 쏟다<br>to let liquid fall out by accident | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>학교생활 장면에서 '쏟다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The clumsy robot let some water spill onto the floor. | Today, the clumsy robot let some water spill onto the floor. | Do not spill water near the computer keyboards in lab. | Be careful not to spill paint water on the desk. |  |
| DAY02-35 | union | 조합<br>a group that joins together for one goal | 사회 / 노동권 (Labor Rights)<br>contextual<br>노동자가 조합을 만들어 근로 조건과 안전 문제를 함께 협의한다 | The farmers formed a union to share tools and seeds. | Workers form a union to discuss fair pay and safety. | Workers formed a trade union to improve workplace safety standards. | Factory workers' union asked for safer ladders and shorter night hours. |  |
| DAY02-36 | maximum | 최대의<br>the largest amount that is allowed | 수학 / 유리수의 대소 비교 (Comparing and Ordering Rational Numbers)<br>contextual<br>여러 수를 비교해 가장 작은 값과 가장 큰 값을 찾는다 | The little cart carried its maximum load of round pumpkins. | Students compare the loads and identify the maximum weight. | The thermometer reached a maximum recorded temperature of thirty degrees. | Eight kilograms is the maximum weight this science scale allows. |  |
| DAY02-37 | semester | 학기<br>one of two long parts of a school year | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>학교생활 장면에서 '학기'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The new semester began with fresh notebooks and bright pencils. | Today, the new semester began with fresh notebooks and bright pencils. | We study Korean history during the first semester of school. | The second semester begins in March with new class schedules. |  |
| DAY02-38 | ceiling | 천장<br>the flat top surface inside a room | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>학교생활 장면에서 '천장'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Paper stars hung from the ceiling above the reading corner. | Today, paper stars hung from the ceiling above the reading corner. | Bright fluorescent lights hang from the high classroom ceiling. | Paper snowflakes hang from the ceiling above our reading corner. |  |
| DAY02-39 | stationery | 문구류<br>things for writing, like pens and paper | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>학교생활 장면에서 '문구류'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The shop sells colorful stationery beside the small art supplies. | The shop sells colorful stationery next to the small art supplies. | He bought new notebooks and pens at the local stationery store. | The school shop sells stationery such as pencils and notebooks. |  |
| DAY02-40 | term | 용어, 기간<br>a word or expression with a specific meaning | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>학교생활 장면에서 '용어, 기간'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The teacher explained an important scientific term during class. | The teacher defines each science term with a clear example. | The teacher explained the scientific term before the reading began. | Photosynthesis is a science term that means making food from light. |  |

## DAY 03 · set 1 · 일상생활 / 여가와 취미

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY03-01 | journey | 여행<br>a long trip from one place to another | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>여가와 취미 장면에서 '여행'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The little turtle began a long journey across the meadow. | The small turtle began a long journey across the meadow. | Our family began a long train journey to the seaside town. | Our train journey to the east coast took five hours. |  |
| DAY03-02 | theater | 극장<br>a place where people watch plays or movies | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>여가와 취미 장면에서 '극장'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The whole family watched a puppet show at the theater. | The entire family watched a puppet show at the theater. | We watched an exciting musical at the downtown city theater. | We watched a puppet play at the small neighborhood theater. |  |
| DAY03-03 | fee | 요금<br>money you pay to use or join something | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>여가와 취미 장면에서 '요금'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The zoo asked a small fee for the boat ride. | Today, the zoo asked a small fee for the boat ride. | Visitors must pay an entrance fee before entering the modern museum. | The swimming pool fee is cheaper for students on weekdays. |  |
| DAY03-04 | prepare | 준비하다<br>to get something ready before you use it | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>여가와 취미 장면에서 '준비하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The bakers prepare warm bread before the morning market opens. | Before the morning market opens, the bakers prepare warm bread. | The young hikers prepare their backpacks before climbing the green mountain. | Campers prepare their tents before the sun goes down. |  |
| DAY03-05 | surprised | 놀란<br>feeling something you did not expect | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>여가와 취미 장면에서 '놀란'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The kitten looked surprised when the balloon floated up high. | When the balloon floated up high, the kitten looked surprised. | He looked surprised when his friends gave him a birthday gift. | Everyone looked surprised when the magician found the hidden card. |  |
| DAY03-06 | receive | 받다<br>to get something that someone gives you | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>여가와 취미 장면에서 '받다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The tiny robot will receive a shiny medal this afternoon. | The small robot will receive a gleaming medal this afternoon. | Winners will receive a shiny medal at the end of competition. | Winners receive a small medal at the end of the race. |  |
| DAY03-07 | nervous | 긴장한<br>feeling worried about something you must do | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>여가와 취미 장면에서 '긴장한'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The young singer felt nervous before stepping onto the stage. | Before stepping onto the stage, the young singer felt nervous. | The young pianist felt nervous right before walking onto stage. | Before the first dance, the young dancer felt nervous backstage. |  |
| DAY03-08 | stage | 무대<br>the raised floor where people perform | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>여가와 취미 장면에서 '무대'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Dancers spun happily across the bright wooden stage tonight. | Dancers spun cheerfully across the colorful wooden stage tonight. | The actors bowed together on stage after the final scene ended. | Four drummers set their instruments on the wide wooden stage. |  |
| DAY03-09 | vacation | 휴가<br>time away from school or work for fun | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>여가와 취미 장면에서 '휴가'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The family spent their summer vacation beside a warm sea. | A family spent their summer vacation beside a warm sea. | Our family planned a restful summer vacation at the peaceful beach. | During summer vacation my family visited a quiet mountain village. |  |
| DAY03-10 | vocation | 천직<br>a job that you feel born to do | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>여가와 취미 장면에서 '천직'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Baking bread became the happy vocation of the cheerful bear. | Today, baking bread became the happy vocation of the cheerful bear. | She discovered her true vocation as a caring wildlife photographer. | Teaching children to swim became this coach's true vocation. |  |

## DAY 03 · set 2 · 일상생활 / 여가와 취미

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY03-11 | pack | 싸다<br>to put things inside a bag or box | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>여가와 취미 장면에서 '싸다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The campers pack warm socks into a big blue backpack. | The campers pack warm socks into a large blue backpack. | Please pack your swimsuit and towel for the swimming lesson. | I pack sunscreen and a towel into my beach bag. |  |
| DAY03-12 | host | 주최하다<br>to have people as guests at an event | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>여가와 취미 장면에서 '주최하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Our neighbors will host a garden party this sunny weekend. | Today, our neighbors will host a garden party this sunny weekend. | Our school will host the regional youth soccer tournament next weekend. | Our neighborhood will host a small kite festival this Sunday. |  |
| DAY03-13 | private | 사적인<br>only for one person, not for everyone | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>여가와 취미 장면에서 '사적인'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The koala keeps a private notebook inside its wooden desk. | Today, the koala keeps a private notebook inside its wooden desk. | He wrote his personal travel memories in a private notebook. | This private notebook holds drawings that nobody else reads. |  |
| DAY03-14 | craft | 공예<br>making things with your hands, like paper art | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>여가와 취미 장면에서 '공예'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The children made a paper craft with glue and ribbons. | Several children made a paper craft with glue and ribbons. | Students learned traditional paper craft during their weekend hobby class. | The paper craft class taught us to fold small cranes. |  |
| DAY03-15 | post | 우편으로 보내다<br>to send a letter through the mail | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>여가와 취미 장면에서 '우편으로 보내다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The rabbit will post a colorful card to its friend. | The small rabbit will post a colorful card to its friend. | I will post a postcard to my friend from Paris. | Tomorrow we will post this holiday card at the corner mailbox. |  |
| DAY03-16 | relieve | 덜어주다<br>to make a bad feeling smaller | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>여가와 취미 장면에서 '덜어주다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Warm tea can relieve the tired feeling after long walks. | Warm tea can relieve the weary feeling after long walks. | Listening to classical music helps relieve stress after a busy week. | A short walk can relieve the worry before a piano test. |  |
| DAY03-17 | practice | 연습하다<br>to do something again and again to improve | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>여가와 취미 장면에서 '연습하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The penguins practice their dance every evening near the ice. | The young penguins practice their dance every evening close to the ice. | Dancers practice their steps every afternoon in the dance studio. | Members practice the same dance step each Thursday evening. |  |
| DAY03-18 | souvenir | 기념품<br>something you keep to remember a trip | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>여가와 취미 장면에서 '기념품'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A tiny shell became the best souvenir from the beach. | A small shell became the best souvenir from the beach. | She bought a handmade wooden souvenir during her trip to Jeju. | A small shell souvenir reminds me of the summer beach trip. |  |
| DAY03-19 | decorate | 장식하다<br>to add pretty things to make something nicer | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>여가와 취미 장면에서 '장식하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The mice decorate their treehouse with leaves and small lanterns. | The small mice decorate their treehouse with leaves and small lanterns. | We decorate the living room with colorful paper lanterns for party. | We decorate the club room with paper flowers before the show. |  |
| DAY03-20 | book | 예약하다<br>to ask to keep a seat or room | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>여가와 취미 장면에서 '예약하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | You should book two seats for the evening puppet show. | Today, you should book two seats for the evening puppet show. | You can book airline tickets online several weeks before traveling. | Please book two seats for the Saturday afternoon movie. |  |

## DAY 03 · set 3 · 일상생활 / 여가와 취미

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY03-21 | reserve | 예약하다<br>to keep something for a person to use later | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>여가와 취미 장면에서 '예약하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The travelers reserve a small cabin near the quiet lake. | The travelers reserve a small cabin close to the quiet lake. | We should reserve a table at the restaurant for Saturday evening. | The library will reserve this comic book for me until Friday. |  |
| DAY03-22 | wrap | 포장하다<br>to cover something with paper or cloth | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>여가와 취미 장면에서 '포장하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The elephant helped wrap a gift in shiny gold paper. | The young elephant helped wrap a gift in shiny gold paper. | Use shiny paper to wrap the present for your friend. | We wrap the handmade gift with brown paper and string. |  |
| DAY03-23 | occasion | 특별한 때<br>a special time when something happens | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>여가와 취미 장면에서 '특별한 때'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A birthday is a happy occasion for the whole family. | A birthday is a happy occasion for the entire family. | The annual town festival is a special occasion for everyone. | A birthday is a happy occasion for sharing cake together. |  |
| DAY03-24 | allow | 허락하다<br>to let someone do something | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>여가와 취미 장면에서 '허락하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The gardener will allow the children to pick ripe berries. | A student gardener will allow the students to pick ripe berries. | Museum guards do not allow flash photography inside the gallery. | The camp leaders allow night walks only with an adult. |  |
| DAY03-25 | ensure | 보장하다<br>to make sure that something happens | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>여가와 취미 장면에서 '보장하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Strong ropes ensure the swing stays safe for every child. | Today, strong ropes ensure the swing stays safe for every child. | Wearing life jackets helps ensure your safety while riding boats. | Checking the ropes twice will ensure a safe climbing practice. |  |
| DAY03-26 | agent | 대리인<br>a person whose job is helping others plan | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>여가와 취미 장면에서 '대리인'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A friendly travel agent showed maps of the green islands. | A helpful travel agent displayed maps of the green islands. | The travel agent arranged our flight tickets and hotel rooms. | Our travel agent planned the train tickets and hotel rooms. |  |
| DAY03-27 | argue | 말다툼하다<br>to talk in a way that shows you disagree | 사회 / 사회 갈등과 차별 (Social Conflict and Discrimination)<br>contextual<br>서로 다른 의견으로 말다툼이 생긴 원인을 찾고 존중하는 해결책을 고른다 | The two squirrels argue about who found the biggest acorn. | Friends argue over rules, then choose a respectful solution. | The two friends argue over game rules and then apologize. | Two teammates argue about the game rule, then agree to reread it. |  |
| DAY03-28 | confirm | 확인하다<br>to say that something is true or certain | 국어 / 자료 신뢰성 (Source credibility)<br>contextual<br>자료의 작성자·날짜·근거를 확인하고 다른 출처와 대조한다 | The guide will confirm the boat leaves at ten. | Students confirm an article's date by checking another source. | Researchers confirm the source of the news article before sharing. | We confirm the hiking map's date by checking a second official website. |  |
| DAY03-29 | wonder | 궁금해하다<br>to want to know something | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>여가와 취미 장면에서 '궁금해하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The children wonder what lives inside the old hollow tree. | The children wonder what lives within the old hollow tree. | Children wonder how birds navigate across long distances in autumn. | Many visitors wonder how the huge sand castle stays standing. |  |
| DAY03-30 | wander | 거닐다<br>to walk slowly with no special plan | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>여가와 취미 장면에서 '거닐다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The deer likes to wander through the misty morning forest. | The deer often chooses to wander through the misty morning forest. | Tourists wander slowly through the historical streets of the old city. | Families wander slowly through the flower park on Sunday morning. |  |

## DAY 03 · set 4 · 일상생활 / 여가와 취미

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY03-31 | capital | 수도<br>the main city where a country is governed | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>여가와 취미 장면에서 '수도'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The train carried visitors toward the busy capital that morning. | The capital contains the main offices that govern the country. | Seoul is the bustling capital city of South Korea today. | Seoul is the capital of Korea, so many tourists visit it. |  |
| DAY03-32 | absolute | 완전한<br>complete and total | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>여가와 취미 장면에서 '완전한'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The snowy field lay in absolute silence before dawn. | Before dawn, the snowy field lay in absolute silence. | The quiet forest was in absolute silence under falling snow. | The night campsite was in absolute darkness until we lit lanterns. |  |
| DAY03-33 | anticipate | 기대하다<br>to expect something and get ready for it | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>여가와 취미 장면에서 '기대하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The puppies anticipate treats whenever the cupboard door opens. | The young dogs anticipate treats whenever the cupboard door opens. | Children anticipate the opening of the new amusement park eagerly. | Fans anticipate the concert and buy tickets three months early. |  |
| DAY03-34 | leisure | 여가<br>free time when you can relax | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>여가와 취미 장면에서 '여가'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Fishing by the lake is his favorite leisure activity. | Today, fishing by the lake is his favorite leisure activity. | Reading exciting mystery novels is my favorite form of leisure. | Weekend leisure hours are best for growing tomatoes on our balcony. |  |
| DAY03-35 | undergo | 겪다<br>to go through a change or event | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>여가와 취미 장면에서 '겪다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The caterpillar will undergo an amazing change inside its cocoon. | Today, the caterpillar will undergo an amazing change inside its cocoon. | Athletes undergo rigorous daily training before entering the championship games. | Old bicycles undergo repairs before the club's spring riding trip. |  |
| DAY03-36 | spare | 여분의<br>extra and kept for later use | fallback<br>word_fallback<br>한 장면에서 '여분의'의 확정 뜻이 다른 뜻으로 오해되지 않게 직접 보여 준다 | The cyclist carried a spare wheel inside the small basket. | The cyclist carried a spare wheel within the small basket. | Always keep a spare bicycle tire in your repair kit. | I keep a spare battery in my camera bag. |  |
| DAY03-37 | aquarium | 수족관<br>a glass tank where fish live | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>여가와 취미 장면에서 '수족관'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Bright orange fish swam slowly inside the giant round aquarium. | Bright orange fish swam gently within the giant round aquarium. | We saw colorful tropical fish swimming inside the giant aquarium. | Small striped fish swim behind the thick glass of the aquarium. |  |
| DAY03-38 | applaud | 박수치다<br>to clap your hands to show you liked something | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>여가와 취미 장면에서 '박수치다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The audience will applaud loudly when the curtain rises again. | When the curtain rises again, the audience will applaud loudly. | The excited audience began to applaud loudly after the performance. | Parents applaud loudly when the school band finishes its song. |  |
| DAY03-39 | insurance | 보험<br>money you pay so you get help later | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>여가와 취미 장면에서 '보험'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The farmer bought insurance to protect the barn from storms. | Today, the farmer bought insurance to protect the barn from storms. | Travelers buy travel insurance before departing on overseas trips abroad. | Travel insurance paid for the doctor visit during our trip. |  |
| DAY03-40 | return | 돌아가다<br>to go back to a place | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>여가와 취미 장면에서 '돌아가다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The birds return to the warm valley every early spring. | Today, the birds return to the warm valley every early spring. | Campers return to their cozy tents when the sun sets. | After the picnic, we return to the parking lot together. |  |

## DAY 04 · set 1 · 일상생활 / 일과 직업

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY04-01 | career | 직업<br>the work a person does for many years | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>일과 직업 장면에서 '직업'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The bear dreams of a career baking cakes and pies. | The brown bear dreams of a career baking cakes and pies. | She chose a rewarding career in environmental protection and research. | A firefighter's career often lasts more than twenty years. |  |
| DAY04-02 | skill | 기술<br>something you can do well after practice | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>일과 직업 장면에서 '기술'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Juggling three balls is a skill the clown learned quickly. | Years of careful practice gave the carpenter this useful skill. | The young carpenter practices daily to improve his woodworking skill. | Cutting hair neatly is a skill this barber practiced for years. |  |
| DAY04-03 | task | 일<br>a piece of work you must do | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>일과 직업 장면에서 '일'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The little robot finished each task on the busy morning. | The small robot completed each task on the busy morning. | Sorting these files by date is his primary daily task. | My first task at the bakery is weighing the flour. |  |
| DAY04-04 | tend | 돌보다<br>to take care of something | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>일과 직업 장면에서 '돌보다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The gardener will tend the roses every quiet spring morning. | A student gardener will tend the roses every quiet spring morning. | Gardeners tend the delicate flower beds every morning in spring. | Farmers tend young rice plants from spring until late summer. |  |
| DAY04-05 | position | 자리<br>the place where someone or something is | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>일과 직업 장면에서 '자리'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The goalkeeper stood in her position beside the wooden net. | The goalkeeper stood in her position next to the wooden net. | He applied for a managerial position at the design company. | The goalkeeper stayed in position near the left goal post. |  |
| DAY04-06 | hire | 고용하다<br>to pay someone to work for you | 사회 / 노동권 (Labor Rights)<br>direct<br>임금·안전·단결권 등 일터의 기본 권리를 사례에서 확인한다 | The bakery will hire two helpers for the busy weekend. | The company will hire workers with fair pay and safe hours. | The bakery plans to hire two friendly assistants for summer. | Restaurant owners hire two cooks and pay the legal wage. |  |
| DAY04-07 | quit | 그만두다<br>to stop doing something | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>일과 직업 장면에서 '그만두다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The tired hamster decided to quit running on its wheel. | The weary hamster decided to quit running on its wheel. | He decided to quit his evening job to attend university. | Our neighbor will quit driving trucks and open a flower shop. |  |
| DAY04-08 | proud | 자랑스러운<br>feeling happy about something good you did | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>일과 직업 장면에서 '자랑스러운'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The young painter felt proud of her bright finished picture. | The young painter felt proud of her bright completed picture. | The proud baker displayed his freshly baked artisan loaves proudly. | The young baker felt proud of the first perfect loaf. |  |
| DAY04-09 | labor | 노동<br>hard work done with your body | 사회 / 노동권 (Labor Rights)<br>direct<br>임금·안전·단결권 등 일터의 기본 권리를 사례에서 확인한다 | Building the stone bridge took months of careful labor. | Workers discuss fair wages and safe labor conditions together. | Factory workers provide essential manual labor to assemble new automobiles. | Heavy labor at the port needs safety helmets and rest breaks. |  |
| DAY04-10 | label | 라벨<br>a small tag that tells what something is | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>일과 직업 장면에서 '라벨'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A colorful label was stuck on every jam jar. | One colorful label was stuck on every jam jar. | Read the nutrition label on the bottle before drinking juice. | Each jar has a label showing the fruit inside it. |  |

## DAY 04 · set 2 · 일상생활 / 일과 직업

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY04-11 | familiar | 익숙한<br>known well because you have seen it often | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>일과 직업 장면에서 '익숙한'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The path home felt familiar even in the evening fog. | Today, the path home felt familiar even in the evening fog. | The new employee soon became familiar with office computer software. | This delivery route feels familiar after three months of work. |  |
| DAY04-12 | spread | 펼치다<br>to open something out flat and wide | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>일과 직업 장면에서 '펼치다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The bear will spread a blanket under the tall oak. | The brown bear will spread a blanket under the tall oak. | The architects spread large building blueprints across the wooden table. | Workers spread a large blue sheet across the wet floor. |  |
| DAY04-13 | divide | 나누다<br>to split something into smaller parts | 수학 / 정수와 유리수의 사칙연산 (Operations with Integers and Rational Numbers)<br>direct<br>정수나 분수를 더하고 빼고 곱하고 나누어 값을 구한다 | The friends divide the pizza into eight equal slices. | Students divide twelve counters into three equal groups. | We divide the classroom into four equal teams for games. | We divide twelve apples equally among four workers, giving three each. |  |
| DAY04-14 | retire | 은퇴하다<br>to stop working because you are older | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>일과 직업 장면에서 '은퇴하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The old ship captain plans to retire beside the sea. | The old ship captain plans to retire next to the sea. | Senior professors usually retire from university teaching at age sixty-five. | Our school cook will retire next spring after thirty years. |  |
| DAY04-15 | vision | 시력<br>the power to see things clearly | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>일과 직업 장면에서 '시력'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | An owl has sharp vision even on the darkest night. | Today, an owl has sharp vision even on the darkest night. | The eye doctor checked her vision with a wall chart. | Good vision helps the fruit picker spot ripe berries far away. |  |
| DAY04-16 | obstacle | 장애물<br>something in the way that blocks you | 국어 / 서사 갈등 (Narrative conflict)<br>direct<br>등장인물의 목표와 장애물·갈등이 사건을 어떻게 움직이는지 정리한다 | A fallen log became an obstacle on the forest trail. | A fallen tree becomes the obstacle blocking the hero's goal. | Hard work helped the athlete overcome every difficult training obstacle. | In the story, a locked gate is the hero's main obstacle. |  |
| DAY04-17 | contrary | 반대되는<br>completely different or opposite | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>일과 직업 장면에서 '반대되는'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The two arrows pointed in contrary directions on the map. | Today, the two arrows pointed in contrary directions on the map. | The experimental results showed evidence contrary to our initial guess. | The new plan is contrary to the old delivery schedule. |  |
| DAY04-18 | arrange | 정리하다<br>to put things in a neat order | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>일과 직업 장면에서 '정리하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The florist will arrange yellow tulips inside a glass vase. | Today, the florist will arrange yellow tulips inside a glass vase. | Librarians arrange returned books alphabetically on the tall metal shelves. | Clerks arrange the shoes by size on the front table. |  |
| DAY04-19 | attempt | 시도하다<br>to try to do something | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>일과 직업 장면에서 '시도하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The young climber will attempt the easy wall today. | Now, the young climber will attempt the easy wall today. | Do not attempt dangerous stunts without proper safety equipment on. | Young bakers attempt the braided bread for the first time. |  |
| DAY04-20 | board | 탑승하다<br>to get onto a ship train bus or plane | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>일과 직업 장면에서 '탑승하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Passengers board the train while carrying small travel bags. | Passengers board the bus after the driver opens its doors. | Passengers must board the morning flight twenty minutes before departure. | Passengers board the morning ferry with heavy fishing boxes. |  |

## DAY 04 · set 3 · 일상생활 / 일과 직업

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY04-21 | presentation | 발표<br>a talk that shows your work to others | 국어 / 발표 (Presentation)<br>direct<br>청중 앞에서 핵심 내용을 순서 있게 말하고 자료로 설명한다 | The panda gave a short presentation about bamboo forests today. | Her presentation explains three bamboo facts in a clear order. | Her business presentation included helpful charts about company sales growth. | Our group presentation explains each step with a simple chart. |  |
| DAY04-22 | accomplish | 이루다<br>to finish something you were trying to do | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>일과 직업 장면에서 '이루다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The team can accomplish the puzzle before the sun sets. | The club can accomplish the puzzle before the sun sets. | Dedicated team members worked hard together to accomplish their project. | The team will accomplish the roof repair before the rain starts. |  |
| DAY04-23 | convince | 설득하다<br>to make someone believe or agree with you | 국어 / 토론과 반박 (Debate and rebuttal)<br>direct<br>주장과 근거를 제시하고 상대 주장에 근거로 반박한다 | The clever crow tried to convince its friends to share. | Students use facts to convince classmates to protect the garden. | The salesman used clear facts to convince the doubtful customer. | Clear cost numbers convince the manager that the new plan saves money. |  |
| DAY04-24 | audience | 관객<br>the people who watch or listen to something | 국어 / 발표 (Presentation)<br>contextual<br>강당의 청중을 고려해 발표 자료와 말의 크기를 조절한다 | A cheerful audience filled every seat inside the small hall. | The speaker adjusts picture size and volume for the audience. | The theater audience listened attentively throughout the entire dramatic performance. | The speaker used bigger pictures so the far audience could see. |  |
| DAY04-25 | wage | 임금<br>money paid to someone for their work | 사회 / 노동권 (Labor Rights)<br>direct<br>임금·안전·단결권 등 일터의 기본 권리를 사례에서 확인한다 | The young helper saved every coin from the weekly wage. | Workers compare each wage and ask the union for fairness. | Employees receive an hourly wage for their work each week. | Part-time workers receive a fair wage for every hour worked. |  |
| DAY04-26 | previous | 이전의<br>coming before this one | fallback<br>word_fallback<br>한 장면에서 '이전의'의 확정 뜻이 다른 뜻으로 오해되지 않게 직접 보여 준다 | The previous drawing hung beside the newest colorful one. | The previous drawing hung next to the newest colorful one. | His previous work experience in marketing helped him get hired. | This month's order is larger than the previous one. |  |
| DAY04-27 | spend | 쓰다<br>to use money or time on something | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>일과 직업 장면에서 '쓰다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The rabbits spend every sunny afternoon painting in the garden. | The small rabbits spend every sunny afternoon painting in the garden. | Companies spend money to improve product quality and employee training. | Repair workers spend two hours fixing the broken door hinge. |  |
| DAY04-28 | distinguish | 구별하다<br>to see how two things are different | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>일과 직업 장면에서 '구별하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Can you distinguish the twin puppies by their tiny collars? | Today, can you distinguish the twin puppies by their tiny collars? | A trained jeweler can distinguish real diamonds from synthetic gems. | Bakers distinguish rye flour from wheat flour by its darker color. |  |
| DAY04-29 | former | 이전의<br>belonging to an earlier time | fallback<br>word_fallback<br>한 장면에서 '이전의'의 확정 뜻이 다른 뜻으로 오해되지 않게 직접 보여 준다 | The former champion smiled kindly at the new young winner. | Today, the former champion smiled kindly at the new young winner. | The former company president now advises young startup technology founders. | Our former coach now trains swimmers at another pool. |  |
| DAY04-30 | formal | 격식을 차린<br>very neat and following polite rules | 국어 / 맞춤법과 언어 변이 (Orthography and language variation)<br>contextual<br>상황과 상대에 따라 격식 있는 말투와 편안한 말투를 구별한다 | Everyone wore formal clothes to the grand evening dinner. | She uses formal language when presenting her report to adults. | He wore a formal dark suit for the job interview. | New workers use formal words at interviews and casual words with friends. |  |

| DAY04-35 | enthusiasm | 열정<br>a strong happy feeling about something you like | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>일과 직업 장면에서 '열정'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The puppy greeted the mail carrier with pure enthusiasm. | The young dog greeted the mail carrier with pure enthusiasm. | The young scientist showed great enthusiasm for her ocean research. | The young guide showed real enthusiasm while explaining the old temple. |  |
| DAY04-36 | desirable | 바람직한<br>wanted by many people because it is good | fallback<br>word_fallback<br>한 장면에서 '바람직한'의 확정 뜻이 다른 뜻으로 오해되지 않게 직접 보여 준다 | A sunny window seat is the most desirable spot here. | One sunny window seat is the most desirable spot here. | Punctuality is a desirable quality for all new workplace employees. | Clean air makes this town a desirable place to live. |  |
| DAY04-37 | dismiss | 해산시키다<br>to tell people they may leave | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>일과 직업 장면에서 '해산시키다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The teacher will dismiss the class after the bell rings. | An instructor will dismiss the class after the bell rings. | The supervisor will dismiss the workers after safety inspection finishes. | Our coach will dismiss the team after the final stretching. |  |
| DAY04-38 | competent | 유능한<br>able to do something well enough | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>일과 직업 장면에서 '유능한'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The competent little robot repaired the clock in minutes. | Today, the competent little robot repaired the clock in minutes. | The hospital hired a competent surgeon with ten years experience. | A competent guide answered every question about the mountain path. |  |
| DAY04-39 | memorize | 암기하다<br>to learn something so you remember it perfectly | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>일과 직업 장면에서 '암기하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The parrot tried to memorize a cheerful morning song. | Today, the parrot tried to memorize a cheerful morning song. | Actors must memorize their script lines before the opening night. | Waiters memorize the daily menu before the restaurant opens. |  |
| DAY04-40 | fix | 고치다<br>to repair something that is broken | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>일과 직업 장면에서 '고치다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The mechanic will fix the wobbly wheel on the wagon. | Today, the mechanic will fix the wobbly wheel on the wagon. | The skilled mechanic used wrench tools to fix the engine. | A mechanic will fix the bicycle chain in ten minutes. |  |

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |

| DAY05-02 | regular | 규칙적인<br>happening again and again at the same times | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>운동과 건강 장면에서 '규칙적인'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The rabbit takes a regular morning run around the field. | The small rabbit takes a regular morning run around the field. | Doctors recommend regular aerobic exercise for maintaining good cardiovascular health. | A regular bedtime helps my body wake up easily. |  |
| DAY05-03 | stretch | 스트레칭하다<br>to make your body long and loose | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>운동과 건강 장면에서 '스트레칭하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The cat likes to stretch its legs after a nap. | The house cat often chooses to stretch its legs after a nap. | Runners stretch their leg muscles carefully before starting the race. | Players stretch their arms and legs before the soccer match. |  |
| DAY05-04 | reduce | 줄이다<br>to make something smaller in size or amount | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>운동과 건강 장면에서 '줄이다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The chef will reduce the sugar in the fruit pie. | A young cook will reduce the sugar in the fruit pie. | Eating fresh vegetables can reduce health risks over a lifetime. | The coach asked us to reduce sitting time by ten minutes. |  |
| DAY05-05 | opponent | 상대<br>the person you play against in a game | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>운동과 건강 장면에서 '상대'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The friendly opponent shook hands after the chess match ended. | The helpful opponent shook hands after the chess match ended. | The tennis player shook hands warmly with her skillful opponent. | My opponent served the ball across the net very quickly. |  |
| DAY05-06 | recommend | 추천하다<br>to tell someone that something is good | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>운동과 건강 장면에서 '추천하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The coach will recommend soft shoes for the long walk. | A sports teacher will recommend soft shoes for the long walk. | Nutrition experts recommend drinking plenty of fresh water every day. | Doctors recommend enough sleep and daily walking for growing students. |  |
| DAY05-07 | warn | 경고하다<br>to tell someone about a possible problem | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>운동과 건강 장면에서 '경고하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A yellow sign will warn hikers about the slippery path. | One yellow sign will warn hikers about the slippery path. | Lifeguards warn swimmers about strong ocean currents near the pier. | Our coach will warn us when the gym floor is wet. |  |
| DAY05-08 | cause | 원인<br>the reason why something happens | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>운동과 건강 장면에서 '원인'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Too little sleep was the cause of the sleepy yawns. | Today, too little sleep was the cause of the sleepy yawns. | Lack of sleep is often the main cause of headaches. | The cause of my sore knee was too much running. |  |
| DAY05-09 | lose | 잃다<br>to no longer have something you had | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>운동과 건강 장면에서 '잃다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Do not lose the small key to the garden gate. | Today, do not lose the small key to the garden gate. | Athletes try hard not to lose their focus during matches. | Runners lose water through sweat, so they drink often. |  |
| DAY05-10 | loose | 헐거운<br>not tight and able to move around | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>운동과 건강 장면에서 '헐거운'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The puppy wore a loose collar around its soft neck. | The young dog wore a loose collar around its soft neck. | Wear comfortable loose clothing when practicing yoga in the morning. | These running shoes feel loose, so I tighten the laces. |  |

## DAY 05 · set 2 · 일상생활 / 운동과 건강

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY05-11 | rather | 꽤<br>more than a little; quite | fallback<br>word_fallback<br>한 장면에서 '꽤'의 확정 뜻이 다른 뜻으로 오해되지 않게 직접 보여 준다 | The soup tasted rather salty for the hungry little fox. | Today, the soup tasted rather salty for the hungry little fox. | The morning gym session was rather difficult for beginning runners. | This morning walk felt rather long for the youngest beginners. |  |
| DAY05-12 | straight | 곧은<br>going one way without any bends | 수학 / 점, 선, 면, 각 (Basic Geometric Objects)<br>direct<br>점·선·면·각을 그리고 서로 평행하거나 수직인지 확인한다 | The road stayed straight across the wide golden wheat field. | Students draw a straight line beside two parallel lines. | Draw a straight line connecting points A and B precisely. | The white court lines stay straight and meet at right angles. |  |
| DAY05-13 | meanwhile | 그동안<br>at the same time as something else | fallback<br>word_fallback<br>한 장면에서 '그동안'의 확정 뜻이 다른 뜻으로 오해되지 않게 직접 보여 준다 | The bread baked; meanwhile, the soup simmered on the stove. | Today, the bread baked; meanwhile, the soup simmered on the stove. | He rested quietly; meanwhile, his teammate continued practicing free throws. | One team stretches; meanwhile, the other team runs slow laps. |  |
| DAY05-14 | continuous | 계속되는<br>going on without stopping | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>운동과 건강 장면에서 '계속되는'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A continuous stream of water fell from the mossy rocks. | One continuous stream of water fell from the mossy rocks. | Jogging requires continuous rhythmic breathing to maintain a steady pace. | Swimming needs continuous arm movement from start to finish. |  |
| DAY05-15 | ordinary | 평범한<br>normal and not special | fallback<br>word_fallback<br>한 장면에서 '평범한'의 확정 뜻이 다른 뜻으로 오해되지 않게 직접 보여 준다 | It was an ordinary morning until the balloons appeared. | Today, it was an ordinary morning until the balloons appeared. | Taking ordinary daily walks improves physical fitness for people everywhere. | An ordinary cotton shirt works fine for this easy jog. |  |
| DAY05-16 | relationship | 관계<br>the way two people or things connect | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>운동과 건강 장면에서 '관계'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The gentle relationship between the dog and cat grew stronger. | Today, the gentle relationship between the dog and cat grew stronger. | A positive relationship between diet and energy levels is proven. | This chart shows the relationship between rest days and faster times. |  |
| DAY05-17 | throat | 목구멍<br>the part inside your neck for swallowing | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>운동과 건강 장면에서 '목구멍'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Warm honey tea soothed the singing bird's sore throat. | Today, warm honey tea soothed the singing bird's sore throat. | Warm honey tea soothed his dry throat after soccer practice. | Cold water cooled my throat after the long race. |  |
| DAY05-18 | encounter | 마주치다<br>to meet someone or something by chance | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>운동과 건강 장면에서 '마주치다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Hikers sometimes encounter friendly deer near the quiet mountain stream. | Hikers sometimes encounter friendly deer close to the quiet mountain stream. | Hikers may encounter sudden weather changes on high mountain trails. | Morning joggers encounter a friendly dog near the river path. |  |
| DAY05-19 | disturb | 방해하다<br>to bother someone who is busy or resting | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>운동과 건강 장면에서 '방해하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Please do not disturb the sleeping bear in the cave. | Kindly do not disturb the sleeping bear in the cave. | Loud noise can disturb your natural sleep cycle at night. | Loud music can disturb runners who count their breathing. |  |
| DAY05-20 | lead | 이끌다<br>to go first and show the way | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>운동과 건강 장면에서 '이끌다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The oldest goose will lead the flock toward warm lakes. | Today, the oldest goose will lead the flock toward warm lakes. | Experienced coaches lead their team through rigorous daily warm-up routines. | Our captain will lead the warm-up jog around the field. |  |

## DAY 05 · set 3 · 일상생활 / 운동과 건강

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY05-21 | capable | 유능한<br>able to do something well | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>운동과 건강 장면에서 '유능한'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The capable young sailor tied every knot without any help. | Today, the capable young sailor tied every knot without any help. | A capable personal trainer designs customized exercise routines for clients. | After six months, this swimmer is capable of longer distances. |  |
| DAY05-22 | proper | 적절한<br>right and correct for the situation | fallback<br>word_fallback<br>한 장면에서 '적절한'의 확정 뜻이 다른 뜻으로 오해되지 않게 직접 보여 준다 | Wearing proper boots keeps your feet dry on muddy paths. | Today, wearing proper boots keeps your feet dry on muddy paths. | Wearing proper running shoes prevents foot strain on hard pavement. | Wearing proper shoes protects your feet during long hikes. |  |
| DAY05-23 | frequent | 잦은<br>happening very often | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>운동과 건강 장면에서 '잦은'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Frequent rain kept the garden green all through the summer. | Today, frequent rain kept the garden green all through the summer. | Drink water during frequent rest breaks while hiking on hot days. | Frequent short breaks keep our team fresh during practice. |  |
| DAY05-24 | symptom | 증상<br>a sign that shows you are not well | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>운동과 건강 장면에서 '증상'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A runny nose is one common symptom of a cold. | One runny nose is one common symptom of a cold. | A high fever is a common symptom of seasonal influenza. | A runny nose is one symptom of a common cold. |  |
| DAY05-25 | monitor | 관찰하다<br>to watch something carefully over time | 과학 / 과학적 탐구 (Scientific Inquiry)<br>contextual<br>현상을 일정 시간 관찰하며 변화를 감지하고 자료로 기록한다 | Scientists monitor the baby turtles crossing the warm sandy beach. | Scientists monitor turtle crossings and record the count each morning. | Doctors monitor heart rate changes during the treadmill stress test. | We monitor our heart rate every minute and record the numbers. |  |
| DAY05-26 | consume | 소비하다<br>to eat, drink, or use something up | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>운동과 건강 장면에서 '소비하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Growing puppies consume a lot of water every single day. | Today, growing puppies consume a lot of water every single day. | Athletes consume carbohydrates to supply energy before a marathon competition. | Long distance runners consume fruit and water after the race. |  |
| DAY05-27 | struggle | 애쓰다<br>to try very hard at something difficult | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>운동과 건강 장면에서 '애쓰다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The tiny ant will struggle to carry the heavy crumb. | The small ant will struggle to carry the heavy crumb. | Beginning swimmers struggle to stay afloat without proper kicking technique. | Beginners struggle to hold the plank for one full minute. |  |
| DAY05-28 | strengthen | 강화하다<br>to make something stronger | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>운동과 건강 장면에서 '강화하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Daily swimming will strengthen the young otter's growing legs. | Today, daily swimming will strengthen the young otter's growing legs. | Push-up exercises help strengthen chest and shoulder muscle groups effectively. | Climbing the rope helps strengthen the arms and shoulders. |  |
| DAY05-29 | slide | 미끄러지다<br>to move smoothly across a surface | 과학 / 마찰력 (Friction)<br>contextual<br>표면의 거칠기와 마찰이 미끄러지는 움직임에 주는 영향을 비교한다 | Penguins love to slide down the smooth icy hill. | Blocks slide farther on smooth wood because friction is lower. | Smooth wooden blocks slide easily across the low-friction plastic ramp. | Socks slide farther on smooth wood than on rough carpet. |  |
| DAY05-30 | slice | 자르다<br>to cut something into thin flat pieces | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>운동과 건강 장면에서 '자르다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The chef will slice the melon into ten sweet pieces. | A young cook will slice the melon into ten sweet pieces. | Use a sharp knife to slice fresh apples for snack. | We slice apples into thin pieces for the team snack. |  |

## DAY 05 · set 4 · 일상생활 / 운동과 건강

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY05-31 | sufficient | 충분한<br>enough for what you need | fallback<br>word_fallback<br>한 장면에서 '충분한'의 확정 뜻이 다른 뜻으로 오해되지 않게 직접 보여 준다 | One basket held sufficient apples for the whole picnic group. | Today, one basket held sufficient apples for the whole picnic group. | Eight hours of sleep provides sufficient rest for active teenagers. | One water bottle is sufficient for this thirty minute walk. |  |
| DAY05-32 | fulfill | 이루다<br>to make something you hoped for come true | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>운동과 건강 장면에서 '이루다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The little bird will fulfill its dream of flying high. | The small bird will fulfill its dream of flying high. | Dedicated training helped him fulfill his dream of running marathons. | Finishing the race helped our club fulfill its winter goal. |  |
| DAY05-33 | suggest | 제안하다<br>to give someone an idea to think about | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>운동과 건강 장면에서 '제안하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The guide will suggest an easier path up the hill. | Today, the guide will suggest an easier path up the hill. | Fitness trainers suggest drinking green tea after workouts for recovery. | Teammates suggest a shorter warm-up before the cold morning game. |  |
| DAY05-34 | fatigue | 피로<br>a very tired feeling after hard work | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>운동과 건강 장면에서 '피로'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Deep fatigue made the hiker rest under a shady tree. | Today, deep fatigue made the hiker rest under a shady tree. | Drinking water and resting helps relieve muscle fatigue after exercise. | Deep breathing and rest slowly removed the fatigue in my legs. |  |
| DAY05-35 | intake | 섭취량<br>the amount of food or drink you take | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>운동과 건강 장면에서 '섭취량'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The coach checked the water intake of every young runner. | A sports teacher checked the water intake of every young runner. | Doctors measure daily sodium intake to protect normal blood pressure. | The nurse wrote down my daily water intake for one week. |  |
| DAY05-36 | pace | 속도<br>how fast or slow you move | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>운동과 건강 장면에서 '속도'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The turtle kept a slow, steady pace along the path. | The young turtle kept a slow, steady pace along the path. | Runners maintain a steady pace throughout the long distance race. | Our runner kept a steady pace during the last two laps. |  |
| DAY05-37 | disorder | 무질서<br>a messy state with nothing in order | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>운동과 건강 장면에서 '무질서'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The playroom fell into cheerful disorder after the busy afternoon. | After the busy afternoon, the playroom fell into cheerful disorder. | Messy equipment created temporary disorder on the gymnasium floor today. | The gym locker room was in disorder after the noisy match. |  |
| DAY05-38 | flexible | 유연한<br>able to bend easily without breaking | 과학 / 탄성력 (Elastic Force)<br>direct<br>용수철이나 고무를 늘인 뒤 원래 모양으로 돌아오는 힘을 관찰한다 | A flexible young dancer bent smoothly like a soft ribbon. | A flexible rubber band returns to its shape after stretching. | The flexible rubber band returns quickly to its original shape. | This flexible rubber band stretches, then returns to its first shape. |  |
| DAY05-39 | supplement | 보충제<br>extra food or vitamins that help your body | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>운동과 건강 장면에서 '보충제'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The vet added a healthy supplement to the puppy's meal. | Today, the vet added a healthy supplement to the puppy's meal. | Taking a daily vitamin supplement supports overall immune system health. | A vitamin supplement sits beside the cereal bowl each morning. |  |
