| DAY05-40 | physical | 신체의<br>about your body, not your mind | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>운동과 건강 장면에서 '신체의'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Running and jumping are fun physical games for young children. | Running and jumping are fun physical games for school children. | Regular sports activities improve physical health and boost mental focus. | Daily physical activity keeps our bones and heart healthy. |  |

## DAY 06 · set 1 · 심리&관계 / 성격과 심리

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY06-01 | relax | 쉬다<br>to rest and feel calm and comfortable | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>성격과 심리 장면에서 '쉬다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The koala loves to relax on a soft mossy branch. | Today, the koala loves to relax on a soft mossy branch. | Students like to relax on the grass after gym class. | After the long test, we relax on the grass together. |  |
| DAY06-02 | grateful | 감사하는<br>feeling thankful for something good | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>성격과 심리 장면에서 '감사하는'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The rescued kitten looked grateful for the warm milk bowl. | Today, the rescued kitten looked grateful for the warm milk bowl. | She was grateful for the helpful guidance from her teacher. | The lost hiker felt grateful for the warm blanket offered. |  |
| DAY06-03 | complain | 불평하다<br>to say you are unhappy about something | 국어 / 대화의 원리 (Dialogue principles)<br>direct<br>대화 차례와 상황에 맞게 인사·요청·응답을 주고받는다 | The seagulls complain loudly when the snack basket is empty. | A student waits his turn to complain about the broken chair. | Polite customers do not complain about small delays in service. | When neighbors complain politely, we listen and answer with a calm request. |  |
| DAY06-04 | delighted | 아주 기뻐하는<br>very happy and pleased | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>성격과 심리 장면에서 '아주 기뻐하는'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The children felt delighted when the puppy learned a trick. | When the puppy learned a trick, the children felt delighted. | The delighted girl smiled when receiving her new art supplies. | Grandpa looked delighted when the whole family arrived for dinner. |  |
| DAY06-05 | express | 표현하다<br>to show your ideas or feelings to others | 국어 / 정서 표현 글쓰기 (Expressive writing)<br>contextual<br>구체적인 사건과 감각 표현을 써서 자신의 느낌을 드러낸다 | Painters express happy feelings with bright yellow and orange colors. | A student uses clear details to express joy about spring. | Students express their feelings by writing descriptive sentences in personal journals. | She writes about the salty sea wind to express her joy. |  |
| DAY06-06 | reward | 보상<br>something nice you get for doing well | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>성격과 심리 장면에서 '보상'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A crunchy carrot was the reward for the clever pony. | One crunchy carrot was the reward for the clever pony. | Receiving a shiny trophy was a great reward for effort. | A sticker chart gives a small reward for finishing homework. |  |
| DAY06-07 | frustrated | 좌절한<br>upset because something is not working | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>성격과 심리 장면에서 '좌절한'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The robot felt frustrated when the puzzle piece would not fit. | When the puzzle piece would not fit, the robot felt frustrated. | He felt frustrated when the math equation remained unsolved today. | That puzzle piece would not fit, so I felt frustrated. |  |
| DAY06-08 | recognize | 알아보다<br>to know someone because you have seen them before | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>성격과 심리 장면에서 '알아보다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The duckling could recognize its mother among the tall reeds. | Today, the duckling could recognize its mother among the tall reeds. | I could easily recognize my friend in the crowded auditorium. | We recognize our old classmate by the same curly hair. |  |
| DAY06-09 | temper | 성질<br>how calm or angry a person usually feels | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>성격과 심리 장면에서 '성질'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The gentle dragon kept a calm temper during the loud storm. | Today, the gentle dragon kept a calm temper during the loud storm. | He tried hard to control his quick temper during debate. | The quiet farmer has a calm temper, even during busy days. |  |
| DAY06-10 | temple | 사원<br>a special building where people pray | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>성격과 심리 장면에서 '사원'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | An old stone temple stood quietly among the misty mountains. | Today, an old stone temple stood quietly among the misty mountains. | The monks live peacefully in the quiet ancient mountain temple. | Visitors bow quietly inside the old wooden mountain temple. |  |

## DAY 06 · set 2 · 심리&관계 / 성격과 심리

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY06-11 | object | 물건<br>a thing you can see and touch | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>성격과 심리 장면에서 '물건'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A shiny round object rolled under the wooden kitchen table. | The student picks up each object and describes its texture. | Several committee members object to the proposed new library rule. | The smallest object in the box is a wooden button. |  |
| DAY06-12 | disappointed | 실망한<br>sad because something was not as good as hoped | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>성격과 심리 장면에서 '실망한'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The bunny looked disappointed when the picnic ended so early. | When the picnic ended so early, the bunny looked disappointed. | The disappointed runner vowed to train harder for next race. | Fans looked disappointed when the concert ended after two songs. |  |
| DAY06-13 | anxious | 불안한<br>worried about something that might happen | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>성격과 심리 장면에서 '불안한'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The kitten grew anxious before its first visit outdoors. | Before its first visit outdoors, the kitten grew anxious. | She felt anxious before giving her first major class speech. | Waiting for test results made my brother anxious all morning. |  |
| DAY06-14 | insist | 고집하다<br>to say something firmly again and again | 국어 / 대화의 원리 (Dialogue principles)<br>direct<br>대화 차례와 상황에 맞게 인사·요청·응답을 주고받는다 | The little goat will insist on climbing the tallest rock. | She will insist politely, then listen to her partner's answer. | Scientists insist on checking experimental data before publishing their findings. | Some speakers insist loudly, but good talkers wait for their turn. |  |
| DAY06-15 | sudden | 갑작스러운<br>happening quickly without any warning | fallback<br>word_fallback<br>한 장면에서 '갑작스러운'의 확정 뜻이 다른 뜻으로 오해되지 않게 직접 보여 준다 | A sudden breeze lifted every paper napkin off the table. | One sudden breeze lifted every paper napkin off the table. | A sudden loud thunderstorm interrupted our outdoor picnic in park. | A sudden rain shower stopped our picnic in one minute. |  |
| DAY06-16 | immediate | 즉각적인<br>happening right away with no waiting | fallback<br>word_fallback<br>한 장면에서 '즉각적인'의 확정 뜻이 다른 뜻으로 오해되지 않게 직접 보여 준다 | The helper gave immediate care to the thirsty little plant. | The helper offered immediate care to the thirsty little plant. | The injured player required immediate first aid on the field. | Pressing this button brings an immediate answer from the front desk. |  |
| DAY06-17 | appreciate | 감사하다<br>to be thankful for something good | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>성격과 심리 장면에서 '감사하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The villagers appreciate the baker who shares warm bread daily. | Local residents appreciate the baker who shares warm bread daily. | We appreciate the hard work of our school cafeteria staff. | We appreciate the volunteers who carried our heavy boxes upstairs. |  |
| DAY06-18 | pale | 창백한<br>having a light color, not bright | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>성격과 심리 장면에서 '창백한'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The pale blue sky slowly turned gold at sunset. | The pale blue sky gently turned gold at sunset. | His face looked pale after running up the steep hill. | The morning sky turned a pale pink above the hills. |  |
| DAY06-19 | purpose | 목적<br>the reason you do something | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>성격과 심리 장면에서 '목적'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The purpose of the tall fence is protecting young plants. | Today, the purpose of the tall fence is protecting young plants. | The main purpose of this meeting is to plan sports. | Our purpose today is choosing next month's reading book. |  |
| DAY06-20 | race | 경주<br>a contest to see who is fastest | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>성격과 심리 장면에서 '경주'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The turtle and hare began a friendly race downhill. | Two cyclists start a race to see who finishes fastest. | The young athletes ran fast to win the hundred-meter race. | Two turtles finished the slow race side by side. |  |

## DAY 06 · set 3 · 심리&관계 / 성격과 심리

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY06-21 | discouraged | 낙담한<br>feeling that you cannot succeed | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>성격과 심리 장면에서 '낙담한'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The young penguin felt discouraged after missing the first jump. | After missing the first jump, the young penguin felt discouraged. | Do not feel discouraged by one low score on quiz. | After three missed shots, the young player felt discouraged. |  |
| DAY06-22 | stare | 응시하다<br>to look at something for a long time | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>성격과 심리 장면에서 '응시하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The owl will stare at the moon all night long. | The small owl will stare at the moon all night long. | Curious children stare at the strange sea creature in tank. | Children stare at the slow goldfish for several quiet minutes. |  |
| DAY06-23 | burst | 터지다<br>to break open suddenly | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>성격과 심리 장면에서 '터지다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The balloon may burst if you squeeze it too hard. | Today, the balloon may burst if you squeeze it too hard. | The colorful party balloon will burst if you touch pins. | The balloon will burst if you keep blowing air inside. |  |
| DAY06-24 | miserable | 비참한<br>feeling very sad and uncomfortable | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>성격과 심리 장면에서 '비참한'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The soggy scarecrow looked miserable in the long gray rain. | Today, the soggy scarecrow looked miserable in the long gray rain. | Cold rain made the long waiting line feel miserable outside. | Wet socks and cold wind made the campers feel miserable. |  |
| DAY06-25 | embarrassed | 당황한<br>feeling shy after doing something silly | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>성격과 심리 장면에서 '당황한'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The clown felt embarrassed after tripping over his own shoes. | After tripping over his own shoes, the clown felt embarrassed. | He felt embarrassed when he dropped his metal lunch tray. | I felt embarrassed after calling my teacher by the wrong name. |  |
| DAY06-26 | hardly | 거의 않다<br>almost not at all | fallback<br>word_fallback<br>한 장면에서 '거의 않다'의 확정 뜻이 다른 뜻으로 오해되지 않게 직접 보여 준다 | The tiny mouse could hardly reach the top shelf. | The small mouse could hardly reach the top shelf. | The whispering student could hardly be heard across the room. | The shy puppy hardly moved when strangers walked past. |  |
| DAY06-27 | panic | 당황<br>a sudden strong feeling of worry | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>성격과 심리 장면에서 '당황'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A small panic spread when the picnic basket went missing. | When the picnic basket went missing, a small panic spread. | The loud fire siren caused momentary panic in the hallway. | A small panic spread when the lights blinked during practice. |  |
| DAY06-28 | correspond | 일치하다, 편지를 주고받다<br>to match something or communicate by writing letters | 국어 / 대화의 원리 (Dialogue principles)<br>contextual<br>편지를 주고받을 때 목적과 상대에 맞게 내용을 이어서 응답한다 | The numbers on the map correspond to our list of landmarks. | The pen pals correspond by answering each other's questions in letters. | The experimental results correspond closely with our theoretical computer model. | Two pen pals correspond monthly and answer each other's questions. |  |
| DAY06-29 | cruel | 잔인한<br>not kind to others at all | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>성격과 심리 장면에서 '잔인한'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The story dragon was never cruel, only very shy. | The cruel game character refuses to share any supplies. | Bullying other students is a cruel and unacceptable school behavior. | Leaving a puppy alone in rain would be cruel. |  |
| DAY06-30 | crucial | 결정적인<br>very important for something to work | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>성격과 심리 장면에서 '결정적인'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Water is a crucial part of every growing green plant. | Today, water is a crucial part of every growing green plant. | Adequate sleep is crucial for healthy physical growth in teenagers. | Careful measuring is crucial when you bake a birthday cake. |  |

## DAY 06 · set 4 · 심리&관계 / 성격과 심리

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY06-31 | isolated | 고립된<br>far away from other people or things | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>성격과 심리 장면에서 '고립된'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | One isolated island sat alone in the wide calm sea. | Today, one isolated island sat alone in the wide calm sea. | The small lighthouse stands on an isolated rocky ocean island. | That isolated island cabin stands far from any other house. |  |
| DAY06-32 | awkward | 어색한, 서투른<br>feeling embarrassed or not comfortable with others | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>성격과 심리 장면에서 '어색한, 서투른'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | There was an awkward silence before someone told a joke. | Before someone told a joke, there was an awkward silence. | There was an awkward silence before the speaker began talking. | Our first club meeting felt awkward until everyone shared names. |  |
| DAY06-33 | sympathy | 동정<br>a kind feeling for someone who is sad | 국어 / 공감적 경청 (Active listening)<br>contextual<br>힘든 일을 말한 상대의 감정을 확인하고 동정을 담아 적절히 응답한다 | The bear showed sympathy by sharing berries with the fox. | A listener shows sympathy by naming her friend's sadness. | She offered warm words of sympathy to her sad friend. | My friend showed sympathy by repeating my worry and offering help. |  |
| DAY06-34 | terrifying | 무서운<br>making someone feel very afraid | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>성격과 심리 장면에서 '무서운'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The paper monster mask was not really terrifying at all. | The terrifying mask is only cardboard, so children stay calm. | The sudden dark storm created a terrifying sound of thunder. | The tiny mouse thought the vacuum sound was terrifying. |  |
| DAY06-35 | optimistic | 낙관적인<br>believing good things will happen | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>성격과 심리 장면에서 '낙관적인'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The optimistic farmer smiled at the tiny green seedlings. | The optimistic farmer smiled at the small green seedlings. | The team remained optimistic about winning the final championship game. | Even after losing once, our team stayed optimistic about tomorrow. |  |
| DAY06-36 | sincere | 진실한<br>honest and truly meaning what you say | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>성격과 심리 장면에서 '진실한'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A sincere thank you note made the librarian smile brightly. | One sincere thank you note made the librarian smile brightly. | He wrote a sincere apology letter to his offended classmate. | A sincere apology helped the two friends play together again. |  |
| DAY06-37 | likely | 가능성 있는<br>probably going to happen | fallback<br>word_fallback<br>한 장면에서 '가능성 있는'의 확정 뜻이 다른 뜻으로 오해되지 않게 직접 보여 준다 | Rain is likely before the clouds pass over the valley. | Before the clouds pass over the valley, rain is likely. | Dark gray clouds make rain likely in the late afternoon. | Thick gray clouds mean rain is likely this afternoon. |  |
| DAY06-38 | endure | 견디다<br>to keep going through something hard | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>성격과 심리 장면에서 '견디다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Desert cactuses endure many hot days without any rain. | Today, desert cactuses endure many hot days without any rain. | Desert plants can endure extreme heat and lack of rain. | Desert camels endure long days without drinking any water. |  |
| DAY06-39 | cognitive | 인지의<br>about thinking, learning, and remembering | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>성격과 심리 장면에서 '인지의'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Puzzle games build cognitive skills in young curious minds. | Today, puzzle games build cognitive skills in young curious minds. | Reading books regularly promotes healthy cognitive development in young children. | Memory games train cognitive skills such as remembering and comparing. |  |
| DAY06-40 | reflect | 반사하다<br>to send back light or an image | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>성격과 심리 장면에서 '반사하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Still lake water can reflect the tall snowy mountain peaks. | Today, still lake water can reflect the tall snowy mountain peaks. | Smooth mirror surfaces reflect bright sunlight into the dark room. | Still lake water can reflect the mountain and the clouds. |  |

## DAY 07 · set 1 · 심리&관계 / 인간관계

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY07-01 | similar | 비슷한<br>almost the same but not exactly | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>인간관계 장면에서 '비슷한'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The two kittens have similar stripes on their soft backs. | Today, the two kittens have similar stripes on their soft backs. | The two triangles have similar angles and proportional side lengths. | The twins wear similar jackets but choose different shoes. |  |
| DAY07-02 | situation | 상황<br>everything that is happening at one time | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>인간관계 장면에서 '상황'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The rainy situation changed all the plans for the picnic. | Today, the rainy situation changed all the plans for the picnic. | The referee assessed the game situation before making a call. | In this situation, two friends must share one small umbrella. |  |
| DAY07-03 | related | 관련된<br>connected to something else | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>인간관계 장면에서 '관련된'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | These two puzzles are related because they share one picture. | Today, these two puzzles are related because they share one picture. | The two scientific experiments study related questions about plant nutrition. | My question is related to yesterday's group project plan. |  |
| DAY07-04 | suppose | 추측하다<br>to think that something is probably true | 국어 / 추론 (Inference)<br>direct<br>글에 나온 단서와 배경지식을 연결해 드러나지 않은 뜻을 추론한다 | I suppose the missing sock is under the wooden bed. | Readers suppose the door opened because muddy prints lead outside. | Scientists suppose that ancient Mars once had liquid surface water. | From the muddy shoes, we suppose our friend walked home. |  |
| DAY07-05 | social | 사회적인<br>about spending time with other people | 사회 / 사회화와 사회적 정체성 (Socialization and Social Identity)<br>contextual<br>또래와 함께하는 사회적 활동에서 규범과 역할을 배우는 모습을 찾는다 | Meerkats are social animals that live in busy family groups. | Students learn social rules by taking turns during group work. | Schools help young children develop healthy social skills and empathy. | At social club meetings, new members learn the group's turn-taking rules. |  |
| DAY07-06 | unique | 독특한<br>the only one like it anywhere | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>인간관계 장면에서 '독특한'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Every snowflake has a unique shape falling from the sky. | Today, every snowflake has a unique shape falling from the sky. | Each traditional festival has a unique customary dance and costume. | Each fingerprint pattern is unique, so nobody else shares it. |  |
| DAY07-07 | prefer | 선호하다<br>to like one thing more than another | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>인간관계 장면에서 '선호하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Some penguins prefer cold water to warm sunny beaches. | Today, some penguins prefer cold water to warm sunny beaches. | Some students prefer studying in the quiet library after school. | Some classmates prefer quiet reading over noisy playground games. |  |
| DAY07-08 | remain | 남다<br>to stay in the same place or state | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>인간관계 장면에서 '남다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Three apples remain inside the basket after the long picnic. | Three apples remain within the basket after the long picnic. | A few yellow autumn leaves remain on the tall maple. | Two students remain in the classroom to finish the poster. |  |
| DAY07-09 | distract | 산만하게 하다<br>to take someone's attention away from something | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>인간관계 장면에서 '산만하게 하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A buzzing bee can distract the artist from her painting. | One buzzing bee can distract the artist from her painting. | Loud street traffic can distract students from reading their textbooks. | Whispering friends can distract someone who is solving problems. |  |
| DAY07-10 | district | 구역<br>one part of a city or country | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>인간관계 장면에서 '구역'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The quiet garden district has flowers along every narrow street. | Today, the quiet garden district has flowers along every narrow street. | Our residential district opened a new park for young children. | Our school district holds one friendly sports meeting each spring. |  |

## DAY 07 · set 2 · 심리&관계 / 인간관계

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY07-11 | apologize | 사과하다<br>to say you are sorry for something | 국어 / 대화의 원리 (Dialogue principles)<br>direct<br>대화 차례와 상황에 맞게 인사·요청·응답을 주고받는다 | The puppy seemed to apologize after knocking the cup over. | The student will apologize, and her partner accepts the response. | He chose to apologize sincerely for breaking his classmate's pencil. | We apologize for interrupting, then wait until our friend finishes. |  |
| DAY07-12 | refer | 참조하다<br>to look at something for information | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>인간관계 장면에서 '참조하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Sailors refer to the stars when clouds cover the moon. | When clouds cover the moon, sailors refer to the stars. | Please refer to page twenty in the manual for instructions. | Please refer to the club calendar before choosing a meeting day. |  |
| DAY07-13 | colleague | 동료<br>a person who works with you | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>인간관계 장면에서 '동료'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The baker greeted a colleague at the early morning market. | Today, the baker greeted a colleague at the early morning market. | She worked with a trusted colleague to finish the report. | My mother's colleague brought warm soup when our family moved. |  |
| DAY07-14 | rely | 의지하다<br>to trust someone to help you | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>인간관계 장면에서 '의지하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Young ducklings rely on their mother near the wide pond. | Young ducklings rely on their mother close to the wide pond. | Blind pedestrians rely on trained guide dogs to cross streets. | Younger children rely on the crossing guard every school morning. |  |
| DAY07-15 | mistake | 실수<br>something done in the wrong way | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>인간관계 장면에서 '실수'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | One small mistake turned the blue paint into bright green. | Today, one small mistake turned the blue paint into bright green. | Making an honest mistake helps students learn better math methods. | Calling the wrong number was an honest mistake, not rudeness. |  |
| DAY07-16 | request | 요청<br>when you politely ask for something | 국어 / 대화의 원리 (Dialogue principles)<br>direct<br>대화 차례와 상황에 맞게 인사·요청·응답을 주고받는다 | The polite request for extra crayons made the teacher smile. | His polite request receives a clear answer from the teacher. | Citizens request additional street lighting from the local town council. | A polite request for help usually gets a friendly answer back. |  |
| DAY07-17 | assume | 가정하다<br>to think something is true without checking | 국어 / 추론 (Inference)<br>direct<br>글에 나온 단서와 배경지식을 연결해 드러나지 않은 뜻을 추론한다 | Do not assume the box is empty before looking inside. | Before looking inside, do not assume the box is empty. | Do not assume the experiment outcome before recording your measurements. | Do not assume the answer; check the writer's real evidence first. |  |
| DAY07-18 | refuse | 거절하다<br>to say no to something | 국어 / 대화의 원리 (Dialogue principles)<br>direct<br>대화 차례와 상황에 맞게 인사·요청·응답을 주고받는다 | The stubborn goat will refuse to cross the wet bridge. | She can refuse politely and explain her reason during dialogue. | Fair athletes refuse to use unfair advantages in sports games. | You may refuse politely and explain why the plan feels difficult. |  |
| DAY07-19 | reply | 대답하다<br>to answer someone who asked something | 국어 / 공감적 경청 (Active listening)<br>direct<br>상대의 말을 듣고 고개를 끄덕이거나 뜻을 확인해 공감을 드러낸다 | The parrot will reply whenever someone says hello to it. | The listener will reply after checking the speaker's meaning. | The polite student will reply promptly to the teacher's question. | I nod first, then reply that I understand my friend's worry. |  |
| DAY07-20 | degree | 도(단위), 정도, 학위<br>a unit for measuring temperature or angles | 수학 / 내각과 외각 (Interior and Exterior Angles)<br>direct<br>다각형 꼭짓점의 내각과 이어진 외각의 크기를 재어 관계를 확인한다 | The thermometer showed twenty degree Celsius on a warm morning. | Students record the degree measure of each angle with a protractor. | The boiling water reached one hundred degree temperature on thermometer. | Turning the polygon's side one degree changes its interior and exterior angles. |  |

## DAY 07 · set 3 · 심리&관계 / 인간관계

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY07-21 | capacity | 수용력<br>how much something can hold inside | 수학 / 겉넓이와 부피 (Surface Area and Volume)<br>direct<br>상자나 원기둥의 겉넓이와 내부 부피를 단위로 계산한다 | The small jug has a capacity of exactly one liter. | Students calculate the box's capacity using equal unit cubes. | The new sports stadium has a seating capacity of five thousand. | This box has a capacity of eight small cubes inside. |  |
| DAY07-22 | passion | 열정<br>a very strong love for something | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>인간관계 장면에서 '열정'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Painting sunsets is the young artist's greatest passion. | Today, painting sunsets is the young artist's greatest passion. | The young musician showed great passion during her violin performance. | A passion for folk drumming brought many neighbors together weekly. |  |
| DAY07-23 | surround | 둘러싸다<br>to be all around something | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>인간관계 장면에서 '둘러싸다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Tall green trees surround the small pond in the park. | Today, tall green trees surround the small pond in the park. | Tall green mountains surround the quiet valley village on sides. | Cheering classmates surround the runner who crossed the finish line. |  |
| DAY07-24 | indeed | 정말로<br>really and truly | fallback<br>word_fallback<br>한 장면에서 '정말로'의 확정 뜻이 다른 뜻으로 오해되지 않게 직접 보여 준다 | The cake was indeed the sweetest one at the fair. | Today, the cake was indeed the sweetest one at the fair. | The historical museum tour was indeed educational for all students. | This old photograph is indeed my grandmother's first school picture. |  |
| DAY07-25 | imitate | 모방하다<br>to copy the way someone acts or sounds | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>인간관계 장면에서 '모방하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Baby monkeys imitate the way older monkeys peel bananas. | Today, baby monkeys imitate the way older monkeys peel bananas. | Young birds imitate their parents to learn proper singing calls. | Young parrots imitate the whistles they hear from older birds. |  |
| DAY07-26 | bond | 유대<br>a strong feeling that joins two friends | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>인간관계 장면에서 '유대'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A warm bond grew between the horse and the girl. | One warm bond grew between the horse and the girl. | Shared volunteer work formed a strong bond among team members. | A strong bond grew between the two hiking partners. |  |
| DAY07-27 | obtain | 얻다<br>to get something you wanted | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>인간관계 장면에서 '얻다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The explorer hoped to obtain a map of the island. | Today, the explorer hoped to obtain a map of the island. | Students obtain experimental data by measuring liquid volume with cylinders. | We obtain library cards after filling in one simple form. |  |
| DAY07-28 | precious | 귀중한<br>very valuable and important to you | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>인간관계 장면에서 '귀중한'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A precious blue stone shone inside the old wooden box. | A precious blue stone shone within the old wooden box. | Clean drinking water is a precious resource in dry climates. | That handmade bracelet from my sister is precious to me. |  |
| DAY07-29 | contribute | 기여하다<br>to give something to help a group | 사회 / 지속 가능한 발전과 국제 협력 (Sustainable Development and International Cooperation)<br>contextual<br>공동 문제를 해결하려고 사람이나 집단이 자원과 역할을 나누어 협력한다 | Every student can contribute one book to the class library. | Two towns contribute workers and tools to repair the bridge. | Many citizens contribute money to build a neighborhood community center. | Three villages contribute workers and tools to rebuild the shared bridge. |  |
| DAY07-30 | distribute | 나눠주다<br>to give things out to many people | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>인간관계 장면에서 '나눠주다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Helpers distribute warm blankets to everyone at the camp. | Today, helpers distribute warm blankets to everyone at the camp. | Volunteers distribute warm meals to families in the shelter daily. | Volunteers distribute warm gloves to everyone waiting at the shelter. |  |

## DAY 07 · set 4 · 심리&관계 / 인간관계

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY07-31 | reject | 거절하다<br>to not accept something offered to you | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>인간관계 장면에서 '거절하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The picky cat will reject any food without fresh fish. | Today, the picky cat will reject any food without fresh fish. | The editor had to reject the article because of inaccuracies. | The judges reject any drawing that arrives after the deadline. |  |
| DAY07-32 | gaze | 응시하다<br>to look at something for a long time | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>인간관계 장면에서 '응시하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Sailors gaze at the wide ocean from the tall deck. | Today, sailors gaze at the wide ocean from the tall deck. | Astronomers gaze at distant glowing stars through the optical telescope. | Two friends gaze at the night sky and count stars. |  |
| DAY07-33 | cherish | 소중히 여기다<br>to love and care for something deeply | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>인간관계 장면에서 '소중히 여기다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The bear will cherish the drawing from its little friend. | The brown bear will cherish the drawing from its little friend. | We cherish old family photographs preserved in our history album. | We cherish the letters our grandfather wrote during long travels. |  |
| DAY07-34 | hospitality | 환대<br>kind and warm care given to guests | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>인간관계 장면에서 '환대'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The village showed great hospitality to the tired traveling musicians. | The village displayed great hospitality to the weary traveling musicians. | The village elders welcomed the travelers with warm local hospitality. | The village showed warm hospitality by sharing tea with travelers. |  |
| DAY07-35 | uncover | 발견하다<br>to take a cover off and find something | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>인간관계 장면에서 '발견하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The archaeologists will uncover an old pot beneath the sand. | Today, the archaeologists will uncover an old pot beneath the sand. | Archaeologists uncover ancient clay pottery buried deep beneath the sand. | We uncover an old family photo under the dusty cloth. |  |
| DAY07-36 | casual | 편안한<br>relaxed and not formal at all | 국어 / 맞춤법과 언어 변이 (Orthography and language variation)<br>contextual<br>상황과 상대에 따라 격식 있는 말투와 편안한 말투를 구별한다 | Everyone wore casual clothes to the friendly backyard party. | Friends use casual language while chatting after the formal meeting. | He wore a comfortable casual shirt to the weekend picnic. | With close friends we use casual words, but teachers hear polite ones. |  |
| DAY07-37 | mutual | 서로의<br>shared equally by two people | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>인간관계 장면에서 '서로의'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Their mutual love of drawing made them fast friends. | Today, their mutual love of drawing made them fast friends. | A lasting friendship requires mutual respect between two caring partners. | Their mutual respect helped the two teams share one field. |  |
| DAY07-38 | farewell | 작별<br>words you say when someone is leaving | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>인간관계 장면에서 '작별'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The crew waved a cheerful farewell from the sailing ship. | Today, the crew waved a cheerful farewell from the sailing ship. | The class organized a farewell party for their departing teacher. | Classmates waved and said a cheerful farewell at the station. |  |
| DAY07-39 | owe | 빚지다<br>to need to give money back to someone | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>인간관계 장면에서 '빚지다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | I owe my neighbor two coins for the fresh eggs. | Today, I owe my neighbor two coins for the fresh eggs. | I owe a sincere debt of gratitude to my parents. | I owe my cousin two thousand won for the bus ticket. |  |
| DAY07-40 | treat | 대하다<br>to behave toward someone in a particular way | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>인간관계 장면에서 '대하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The children treat the new student with kindness. | Classmates treat the new student with patience and respect. | Doctors treat injured patients with gentle care in emergency rooms. | Good teammates treat every player with the same fair respect. |  |

## DAY 08 · set 1 · 심리&관계 / 말과 언어

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY08-01 | comment | 의견<br>something you say about a person or thing | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>말과 언어 장면에서 '의견'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The teacher wrote a kind comment beside the colorful drawing. | An instructor wrote a kind comment next to the colorful drawing. | She shares a helpful comment during the group book discussion. | The teacher wrote a short comment beside my drawing. |  |
| DAY08-02 | popular | 인기 있는<br>liked by many people | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>말과 언어 장면에서 '인기 있는'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Strawberry ice cream is the most popular flavor here. | Today, strawberry ice cream is the most popular flavor here. | Soccer is a popular sport among middle school students everywhere. | This folk song is popular with students in our school. |  |
| DAY08-03 | develop | 개발하다<br>to make something grow better over time | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>말과 언어 장면에서 '개발하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The inventors will develop a quieter engine for the boat. | Today, the inventors will develop a quieter engine for the boat. | Regular reading practice helps students develop a rich vocabulary repertoire. | Reading aloud daily helps children develop clearer speaking habits. |  |
| DAY08-04 | moment | 순간<br>a very short piece of time | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>말과 언어 장면에서 '순간'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | For one bright moment the fireflies lit the whole meadow. | Today, for one bright moment the fireflies lit the whole meadow. | The winner enjoyed a proud moment on the award stage. | For one short moment, the whole room became completely silent. |  |
| DAY08-05 | public | 공공의<br>open for everyone to use | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>말과 언어 장면에서 '공공의'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The public garden welcomes families every warm summer afternoon. | Today, the public garden welcomes families every warm summer afternoon. | The town opened a new public library for all residents. | Anyone may borrow books from this public reading room. |  |
| DAY08-06 | promise | 약속하다<br>to say you will surely do something | 국어 / 대화의 원리 (Dialogue principles)<br>direct<br>대화 차례와 상황에 맞게 인사·요청·응답을 주고받는다 | The two friends promise to meet under the oak tree. | Friends promise to share tasks and confirm their plan aloud. | He kept his sincere promise to return the borrowed book. | I promise to answer your message before dinner tonight. |  |
| DAY08-07 | select | 고르다<br>to choose one thing from many | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>말과 언어 장면에서 '고르다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | You may select one puppy from the playful little group. | Today, you may select one puppy from the playful little group. | Please select the correct mathematical equation from the four choices. | Speakers select simple words so younger listeners understand easily. |  |
| DAY08-08 | pleasure | 즐거움<br>a happy feeling you get from something | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>말과 언어 장면에서 '즐거움'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Reading in the hammock brings the fox great pleasure daily. | Today, reading in the hammock brings the fox great pleasure daily. | Reading fascinating adventure stories brings great pleasure to young readers. | Telling funny stories gives my grandmother great pleasure every evening. |  |
| DAY08-09 | involve | 포함하다<br>to include someone or something as a part | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>말과 언어 장면에서 '포함하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The games involve every child in the friendly summer camp. | The games involve every child in the helpful summer camp. | Science experiments involve careful observation and accurate measurement of data. | Our word game will involve every student in the class. |  |
| DAY08-10 | evolve | 진화하다<br>to change slowly into something new | 과학 / 변이 (Variation)<br>contextual<br>세대를 거치며 유리한 변이가 이어져 생물이 진화하는 과정을 설명한다 | Birds evolve over long ages into many colorful new shapes. | Bird populations evolve as helpful traits continue across generations. | Species evolve over many generations to adapt to changing environments. | Birds with stronger beaks survive, so beak shapes evolve over generations. |  |

## DAY 08 · set 2 · 심리&관계 / 말과 언어

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY08-11 | range | 범위<br>the space between the smallest and biggest | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>말과 언어 장면에서 '범위'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The store sells hats in a wide range of sizes. | Today, the store sells hats in a wide range of sizes. | The temperature data shows a wide range across different seasons. | The range between the shortest and tallest plant is ten centimeters. |  |
| DAY08-12 | stuff | 물건<br>things of many kinds together | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>말과 언어 장면에서 '물건'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The attic was full of dusty old stuff and boxes. | The attic was full of dirty old stuff and boxes. | Please pack your art stuff into the wooden storage box. | My drawer holds art stuff such as crayons and stickers. |  |
| DAY08-13 | journal | 일기<br>a book where you write about your days | 국어 / 정서 표현 글쓰기 (Expressive writing)<br>direct<br>하루의 구체적인 사건과 그때의 감정을 일기에 쓴다 | The explorer kept a journal inside a leather travel bag. | Her journal describes the rainy trip and her worried feelings. | She writes daily reflections in her personal reading journal notebook. | In my journal I wrote about the lost kitten and my worry. |  |
| DAY08-14 | describe | 묘사하다<br>to use words to tell what something is like | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>말과 언어 장면에서 '묘사하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Can you describe the strange bird you saw this morning? | Today, can you describe the strange bird you saw this morning? | Students use sensory adjectives to describe their favorite outdoor park. | Please describe the bird's color, size, and song for us. |  |
| DAY08-15 | otherwise | 그렇지 않으면<br>if that does not happen | fallback<br>word_fallback<br>한 장면에서 '그렇지 않으면'의 확정 뜻이 다른 뜻으로 오해되지 않게 직접 보여 준다 | Water the plants now; otherwise, they will droop tomorrow. | Today, water the plants now; otherwise, they will droop tomorrow. | Wear a warm coat, otherwise you might catch a cold. | Speak slowly; otherwise the listeners will miss important words. |  |
| DAY08-16 | share | 나누다<br>to give part of something to others | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>말과 언어 장면에서 '나누다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The bear will share honey cake with all the cubs. | The brown bear will share honey cake with all the cubs. | The siblings share their storybooks during quiet afternoon reading time. | Group members share their notes before writing the final report. |  |
| DAY08-17 | jealous | 질투하는<br>unhappy because someone has what you want | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>말과 언어 장면에서 '질투하는'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The parrot felt jealous of the puppy's shiny new toy. | Today, the parrot felt jealous of the puppy's shiny new toy. | He felt jealous when his brother received a new bicycle. | The younger brother felt jealous of the new bicycle. |  |
| DAY08-18 | translate | 번역하다<br>to change words into another language | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>말과 언어 장면에서 '번역하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A friendly robot can translate every message for the visitors. | A helpful robot can translate every message for the visitors. | Can you translate this traditional Korean proverb into natural English? | My cousin can translate Korean picture books into simple English. |  |
| DAY08-19 | nod | 고개를 끄덕이다<br>to move your head down to say yes | 국어 / 공감적 경청 (Active listening)<br>direct<br>상대의 말을 듣고 고개를 끄덕이거나 뜻을 확인해 공감을 드러낸다 | The shy turtle will nod whenever it agrees with you. | Listeners nod and restate the speaker's concern before answering. | Listeners nod their heads to show agreement with the speaker. | Listeners nod slowly to show they follow the speaker's story. |  |
| DAY08-20 | mean | 의미하다<br>to have a certain meaning | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>말과 언어 장면에서 '의미하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Green lights mean the little train can move ahead now. | Green lights mean the small train can move ahead now. | Red traffic lights mean that all moving vehicles must stop. | These two words mean almost the same thing in Korean. |  |

## DAY 08 · set 3 · 심리&관계 / 말과 언어

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY08-21 | conclude | 결론짓다<br>to decide something after thinking carefully | 국어 / 추론 (Inference)<br>contextual<br>글에 나온 단서와 배경지식을 연결해 타당한 결론을 내린다 | The young detective will conclude that the cat took it. | Readers conclude the cat escaped after finding paw prints outside. | Scientists conclude their research report with verified evidence and data. | The story's last hint lets readers conclude who found the key. |  |
| DAY08-22 | intend | 의도하다<br>to plan to do something | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>말과 언어 장면에서 '의도하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The squirrels intend to build a nest before winter comes. | Before winter comes, the squirrels intend to build a nest. | I intend to finish my science project before the weekend. | We intend to write thank-you cards for the school cooks. |  |
| DAY08-23 | worth | 가치 있는<br>good enough for the time or money | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>말과 언어 장면에서 '가치 있는'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The long climb was worth the beautiful mountain view. | Today, the long climb was worth the beautiful mountain view. | This rare historical coin is worth five hundred thousand won. | A careful rewrite is worth the extra twenty minutes. |  |
| DAY08-24 | outcome | 결과<br>what finally happens at the end | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>말과 언어 장면에서 '결과'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The outcome of the friendly game made everybody smile happily. | The outcome of the helpful game made everybody smile cheerfully. | The election outcome was announced on the evening news broadcast. | The final outcome of our class vote was a tie. |  |
| DAY08-25 | honor | 명예<br>great respect given for doing something good | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>말과 언어 장면에서 '명예'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Winning the kindness badge was a real honor for her. | Today, winning the kindness badge was a real honor for her. | Winning the fair play award was a great team honor. | Reading the school poem aloud was a great honor. |  |
| DAY08-26 | internal | 내부의<br>on the inside of something | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>말과 언어 장면에서 '내부의'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The clock's internal gears turned smoothly behind the glass door. | Today, the clock's internal gears turned smoothly behind the glass door. | The human heart is an essential internal organ for circulation. | This clock's internal gears turn quietly behind the glass cover. |  |
| DAY08-27 | verbal | 말의<br>using spoken words | 국어 / 대화의 원리 (Dialogue principles)<br>direct<br>대화 차례와 상황에 맞게 인사·요청·응답을 주고받는다 | The coach gave verbal directions during the swimming lesson today. | The coach gives verbal directions, then the swimmer repeats them. | The teacher gave clear verbal instructions before the science experiment. | A quick verbal answer works better than silence during discussions. |  |
| DAY08-28 | besides | 게다가<br>also, in addition to that | fallback<br>word_fallback<br>한 장면에서 '게다가'의 확정 뜻이 다른 뜻으로 오해되지 않게 직접 보여 준다 | The soup smelled great; besides, it was warm and healthy. | Today, the soup smelled great; besides, it was warm and healthy. | Besides playing basketball, he enjoys practicing classical guitar on weekends. | The poem rhymes well; besides, it uses very simple words. |  |
| DAY08-29 | cure | 치료하다<br>to make a sick person well again | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>말과 언어 장면에서 '치료하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Warm soup helped cure the little dragon's sniffly nose. | Warm soup helped cure the small dragon's sniffly nose. | Medical scientists search for an effective cure for viral diseases. | Warm rest and medicine will cure this common cold. |  |
| DAY08-30 | core | 핵심<br>the middle part of something | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>말과 언어 장면에서 '핵심'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The apple core sat neatly on the small white plate. | Today, the apple core sat neatly on the small white plate. | The heavy iron core lies at the center of Earth. | Cutting the apple showed the core with five brown seeds. |  |

## DAY 08 · set 4 · 심리&관계 / 말과 언어

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY08-31 | strike | 치다<br>to hit something, like a drum | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>말과 언어 장면에서 '치다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The drummer will strike the big drum one more time. | The drummer will strike the large drum one more time. | The clock began to strike twelve times at bright noon. | Drummers strike the round drum head with soft wooden sticks. |  |
| DAY08-32 | channel | 수로<br>a narrow path where water flows | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>말과 언어 장면에서 '수로'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A small channel carried river water into the rice field. | One small channel carried river water into the rice field. | The ship navigated safely through the narrow deep water channel. | Rain water flows through the narrow channel beside the rice field. |  |
| DAY08-33 | gratitude | 감사<br>a warm thankful feeling inside you | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>말과 언어 장면에서 '감사'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The villagers showed gratitude with a basket of fresh fruit. | Local residents displayed gratitude with a basket of fresh fruit. | She wrote a thank-you letter to express her sincere gratitude. | The students bowed and spoke words of gratitude to the volunteers. |  |
| DAY08-34 | metaphor | 은유<br>words that call one thing another thing | 국어 / 운율·비유·상징 (Poetic devices)<br>direct<br>시에서 반복되는 소리와 비유·상징이 만드는 느낌을 찾는다 | Calling the sun a golden coin is a metaphor. | Calling the sun a yellow coin is a metaphor. | The poet used a subtle metaphor in the second stanza. | This poem uses one metaphor that calls the moon a lamp. |  |
| DAY08-35 | interpret | 해석하다<br>to explain what something means | 국어 / 해석과 상호텍스트성 (Interpretation and intertextuality)<br>direct<br>작품의 구절을 근거로 뜻을 해석하고 다른 이야기와 연결한다 | The guide will interpret the old cave paintings for visitors. | Readers interpret the hero's choice using clues from the story. | Students interpret the meaning of the historical text using evidence. | Students interpret the last line and connect it to another folk tale. |  |
| DAY08-36 | insult | 모욕<br>an unkind thing said to someone | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>말과 언어 장면에서 '모욕'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The knight ignored the goblin's silly insult and kept walking. | Today, the knight ignored the goblin's silly insult and kept walking. | Using a hurtful insult damages trust among close school friends. | Calling someone's drawing ugly is an insult, not honest advice. |  |
| DAY08-37 | weird | 이상한<br>very strange and unusual | fallback<br>word_fallback<br>한 장면에서 '이상한'의 확정 뜻이 다른 뜻으로 오해되지 않게 직접 보여 준다 | A weird purple mushroom grew beside the old stone wall. | A weird purple mushroom grew next to the old stone wall. | The strange nocturnal bird made a weird whistling sound tonight. | This purple cloud shape looked weird above the evening hills. |  |
| DAY08-38 | random | 무작위의<br>chosen without any plan or order | 과학 / 기체 입자 모형 (Particle Model of Gases)<br>contextual<br>기체 입자가 일정한 길 없이 무작위 방향으로 움직이는 모형을 관찰한다 | The robot picked a random card from the shuffled deck. | Gas particles move in random directions and strike the container. | Gas particles move in a random direction inside the container. | Gas particles move in random directions inside the closed bottle. |  |
| DAY08-39 | remark | 발언<br>a short thing that someone says | 국어 / 대화의 원리 (Dialogue principles)<br>direct<br>대화 차례와 상황에 맞게 인사·요청·응답을 주고받는다 | One kind remark made the shy hedgehog feel much braver. | Her respectful remark answers the speaker without interrupting. | His polite remark opened the discussion in a friendly way. | One kind remark from the listener kept our conversation going. |  |
| DAY08-40 | sentence | 문장<br>a group of words that tells one idea | 국어 / 문장 구조 (Sentence structure)<br>direct<br>낱말과 구절을 주어와 서술어 관계가 분명한 문장으로 배열한다 | The parrot repeated the whole sentence without missing one word. | Students build a sentence with a clear subject and verb. | Write a complete English sentence with a subject and verb. | We arranged the words so this sentence has a clear subject. |  |

## DAY 09 · set 1 · 심리&관계 / 도덕과 윤리

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY09-01 | just | 공정한<br>fair and right for everyone | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>도덕과 윤리 장면에서 '공정한'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A just referee treats both teams the same way. | One just referee treats both teams the same way. | A just referee treats both competing teams equally without favoritism. | A just referee gives both teams the same warning. |  |
| DAY09-02 | moral | 도덕적인<br>about doing what is right and good | 사회 / 법, 도덕과 법치주의 (Law, Morality, and the Rule of Law)<br>direct<br>법적 의무와 도덕적 의무를 구별하고 모든 사람에게 같은 절차를 적용한다 | Returning the lost coin was a moral choice. | The student makes a moral choice and returns the wallet. | Citizens respect both formal laws and shared moral values. | Sharing her lunch was a moral choice, not a school rule. |  |
| DAY09-03 | peer | 또래<br>someone who is the same age as you | 사회 / 사회화와 사회적 정체성 (Socialization and Social Identity)<br>direct<br>가족·학교·친구 관계에서 규범과 정체성을 배우는 모습을 찾는다 | Each peer helped a classmate finish the group project. | Each peer follows the group's rule and shares one task. | Students learn teamwork by working with each peer in class. | My peer group taught me how to greet older neighbors politely. |  |
| DAY09-04 | achieve | 이루다<br>to reach a goal after working hard | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>도덕과 윤리 장면에서 '이루다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The young skater will achieve a smooth spin today. | Now, the young skater will achieve a smooth spin today. | Hard work helps honest people achieve their important life goals. | Honest practice helped our team achieve the reading goal together. |  |
| DAY09-05 | path | 길<br>a narrow way for walking | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>도덕과 윤리 장면에서 '길'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A stone path led through the quiet green garden. | One stone path led through the quiet green garden. | Walk along the designated path to protect the fragile flowers. | We chose the shorter path so nobody walked through gardens. |  |
| DAY09-06 | necessary | 필요한<br>needed and very important to have | fallback<br>word_fallback<br>한 장면에서 '필요한'의 확정 뜻이 다른 뜻으로 오해되지 않게 직접 보여 준다 | Water is necessary for every plant in the school garden. | Today, water is necessary for every plant in the school garden. | Clean drinking water is necessary for all living creatures everywhere. | Two signatures are necessary before the club borrows the camera. |  |
| DAY09-07 | selfish | 이기적인<br>thinking only about yourself, not others | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>도덕과 윤리 장면에서 '이기적인'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The selfish dragon puppet kept all the cookies to itself. | Today, the selfish dragon puppet kept all the cookies to itself. | A selfish person refuses to share shared toys with others. | Taking all the crayons was selfish, so I returned some. |  |
| DAY09-08 | penalty | 벌칙<br>something you lose for breaking a rule | 사회 / 법, 도덕과 법치주의 (Law, Morality, and the Rule of Law)<br>direct<br>법적 의무와 도덕적 의무를 구별하고 모든 사람에게 같은 절차를 적용한다 | The player moved back three spaces as a game penalty. | The same rule gives each player an equal penalty. | The referee gave a penalty for breaking the game rule. | The same penalty applies to every player who breaks this rule. |  |
| DAY09-09 | adopt | 입양하다<br>to take a pet or child into your family | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>도덕과 윤리 장면에서 '입양하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The family will adopt a gentle dog this weekend. | A family will adopt a gentle dog this weekend. | The caring family decided to adopt a rescue puppy today. | Our family will adopt a shelter dog next Saturday morning. |  |
| DAY09-10 | adapt | 적응하다<br>to change so you fit a new place | 사회 / 문화와 문화 변동 (Culture and Cultural Change)<br>contextual<br>새로운 지역이나 환경에 맞추어 생활 방식이 달라지는 사례를 비교한다 | Desert foxes adapt well to very hot sandy places. | Families adapt local recipes after moving to a new country. | Immigrants adapt to new customs while preserving their native traditions. | New neighbors adapt their meals to the vegetables grown here. |  |

## DAY 09 · set 2 · 심리&관계 / 도덕과 윤리

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY09-11 | pause | 잠시 멈추다<br>to stop for a short time | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>도덕과 윤리 장면에서 '잠시 멈추다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The hikers will pause beside the cool mountain stream. | The hikers will pause next to the cool mountain stream. | Please pause the video while the teacher answers a question. | Speakers pause briefly so listeners can think about the question. |  |
| DAY09-12 | incentive | 동기<br>something that makes you want to try | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>도덕과 윤리 장면에서 '동기'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A shiny sticker became a fun incentive for reading books. | One shiny sticker became a fun incentive for reading books. | The prize offered a strong incentive for students to read. | A shared class picnic became our incentive for cleaning together. |  |
| DAY09-13 | sacrifice | 희생하다<br>to give up something for someone else | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>도덕과 윤리 장면에서 '희생하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The bear will sacrifice its warm seat for a friend. | The brown bear will sacrifice its warm seat for a friend. | Brave firefighters sacrifice their comfort to protect the community members. | Older students sacrifice their break time to help younger readers. |  |
| DAY09-14 | generous | 너그러운<br>happy to give a lot to others | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>도덕과 윤리 장면에서 '너그러운'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The generous baker gave extra rolls to every child. | The generous baker offered extra rolls to every child. | The generous merchant donated warm coats to the local shelter. | Our neighbor is generous and shares fresh vegetables every week. |  |
| DAY09-15 | appropriate | 적절한<br>right for the time or place | fallback<br>word_fallback<br>한 장면에서 '적절한'의 확정 뜻이 다른 뜻으로 오해되지 않게 직접 보여 준다 | Warm boots are appropriate for a walk in deep snow. | Today, warm boots are appropriate for a walk in deep snow. | Wearing safety goggles is appropriate during science chemistry lab experiments. | Quiet voices are appropriate inside the hospital waiting room. |  |
| DAY09-16 | chase | 쫓다<br>to run after someone or something | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>도덕과 윤리 장면에서 '쫓다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Two puppies happily chase a rolling red ball outside. | Two puppies cheerfully chase a rolling red ball outside. | Playful puppies chase a rolling tennis ball across the lawn. | Two puppies chase one another around the wide grassy yard. |  |
| DAY09-17 | deserve | 받을 만하다<br>to have earned something by what you did | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>도덕과 윤리 장면에서 '받을 만하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The helpful robots deserve a bright medal for their work. | The helpful robots deserve a colorful medal for their work. | Honest students deserve praise for doing their own homework tasks. | Students who cleaned the park deserve the thank-you letter. |  |
| DAY09-18 | pretend | 척하다<br>to act like something is real for fun | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>도덕과 윤리 장면에서 '척하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The kittens pretend the cardboard box is a fast rocket. | Today, the kittens pretend the cardboard box is a fast rocket. | Do not pretend to know an answer when you do. | Children pretend the cardboard box is a small sailing boat. |  |
| DAY09-19 | neglect | 소홀히 하다<br>to forget to take care of something | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>도덕과 윤리 장면에서 '소홀히 하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Do not neglect the plants during the long summer trip. | Today, do not neglect the plants during the long summer trip. | Pet owners should not neglect their daily duties of feeding. | If we neglect the class plants, their leaves turn yellow. |  |
| DAY09-20 | lie | 거짓말<br>something said that is not true | 사회 / 법, 도덕과 법치주의 (Law, Morality, and the Rule of Law)<br>direct<br>규칙을 지키는 행동과 거짓되거나 불공정한 행동을 도덕 기준으로 구별한다 | A small lie in the story made everyone laugh together. | A lie breaks trust, although no written law was broken. | Telling a deliberate lie destroys trust between two close friends. | Telling a lie about the score is unfair, not just impolite. |  |

## DAY 09 · set 3 · 심리&관계 / 도덕과 윤리

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY09-21 | devote | 바치다<br>to give all your time to something | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>도덕과 윤리 장면에서 '바치다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The gardener will devote each morning to the rose beds. | A student gardener will devote each morning to the rose beds. | Volunteers devote their free time to cleaning the polluted beach. | Some volunteers devote every Sunday to cleaning the river bank. |  |
| DAY09-22 | admit | 인정하다<br>to say that something true is your fault | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>도덕과 윤리 장면에서 '인정하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The puppy will admit that it ate the last biscuit. | The young dog will admit that it ate the last biscuit. | It takes courage to admit your mistakes to your parents. | I admit that I broke the flowerpot while running inside. |  |
| DAY09-23 | gender | 성별<br>a person's identity as a girl, a boy, or other | 사회 / 사회화와 사회적 정체성 (Socialization and Social Identity)<br>direct<br>가족·학교·친구 관계에서 규범과 정체성을 배우는 모습을 찾는다 | The sports club welcomes every gender to join the team. | Students discuss how family and friends shape gender roles. | Students of every gender have equal opportunities in school sports. | Our club welcomes members of any gender for every activity. |  |
| DAY09-24 | permit | 허락하다<br>to let someone do something | 사회 / 기본권 제한과 권리 구제 (Limitations and Remedies for Rights)<br>contextual<br>행동을 허용하거나 금지하는 규칙이 권리를 필요한 만큼만 제한하는지 따진다 | The librarian will permit quiet reading in the garden corner. | The rule may permit speech unless it harms another student. | School rules permit students to use tablets for educational research. | Library rules permit talking in one room but not the reading hall. |  |
| DAY09-25 | spouse | 배우자<br>the person someone is married to | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>도덕과 윤리 장면에서 '배우자'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The farmer and her spouse planted apple trees together. | Today, the farmer and her spouse planted apple trees together. | He supported his spouse during her difficult medical school studies. | The letter was addressed to my grandmother and her spouse. |  |
| DAY09-26 | ultimate | 궁극적인<br>the last and greatest one | fallback<br>word_fallback<br>한 장면에서 '궁극적인'의 확정 뜻이 다른 뜻으로 오해되지 않게 직접 보여 준다 | Reaching the sunny peak was the ultimate goal today. | Now, reaching the sunny peak was the ultimate goal today. | Achieving world peace is the ultimate goal of international cooperation. | Our ultimate goal is finishing the whole mountain trail safely. |  |
| DAY09-27 | cheat | 속이다<br>to break rules to win unfairly | 사회 / 법, 도덕과 법치주의 (Law, Morality, and the Rule of Law)<br>direct<br>규칙을 지키는 행동과 거짓되거나 불공정한 행동을 도덕 기준으로 구별한다 | Nobody wants to cheat during the friendly board game. | Nobody wants to cheat during the helpful board game. | Students must not cheat on exams by copying others' answers. | Players who cheat during the board game must return their cards. |  |
| DAY09-28 | extraordinary | 놀라운<br>much more special than usual | fallback<br>word_fallback<br>한 장면에서 '놀라운'의 확정 뜻이 다른 뜻으로 오해되지 않게 직접 보여 준다 | The tiny bird made an extraordinary nest from bright threads. | The small bird made an extraordinary nest from bright threads. | The young musician has an extraordinary talent for playing violin. | That sunflower reached an extraordinary height above the garden fence. |  |
| DAY09-29 | ethnic | 민족의<br>about a group with shared customs | 사회 / 문화경관과 문화 다양성 (Cultural Landscapes and Cultural Diversity)<br>direct<br>지역의 건축·의복·음식·종교가 만든 문화경관의 다양성을 비교한다 | The festival showed ethnic dances from many faraway lands. | The festival displayed ethnic dances from many faraway lands. | The city festival celebrates different ethnic foods and traditional dances. | Each ethnic neighborhood shows different food, clothes, and roof shapes. |  |
| DAY09-30 | ethical | 윤리적인<br>doing what is honest and right | 사회 / 법, 도덕과 법치주의 (Law, Morality, and the Rule of Law)<br>direct<br>규칙을 지키는 행동과 거짓되거나 불공정한 행동을 도덕 기준으로 구별한다 | The ethical shopkeeper always returned the extra change politely. | The ethical player returns extra points awarded by mistake. | Doctors follow strict ethical guidelines to protect patient privacy rights. | Everyone agreed that returning the lost wallet was ethical behavior. |  |

## DAY 09 · set 4 · 심리&관계 / 도덕과 윤리

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY09-31 | fundamental | 근본적인<br>the most basic and important part | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>도덕과 윤리 장면에서 '근본적인'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Kindness is a fundamental rule in this friendly classroom. | Today, kindness is a fundamental rule in this friendly classroom. | Equal rights are a fundamental principle of modern democratic society. | Honesty is a fundamental rule in every team we join. |  |
| DAY09-32 | modify | 수정하다<br>to change a small part of something | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>도덕과 윤리 장면에서 '수정하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The inventor will modify the wheels on the little cart. | The inventor will modify the wheels on the small cart. | The committee voted to modify the outdated club safety rules. | We modify one club rule so younger members can join. |  |
| DAY09-33 | instinct | 본능<br>something an animal knows without learning it | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>도덕과 윤리 장면에서 '본능'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Baby turtles follow instinct as they crawl toward the sea. | Today, baby turtles follow instinct as they crawl toward the sea. | Birds follow natural instinct when building cozy nests in spring. | Baby turtles follow instinct and crawl toward the bright sea. |  |
| DAY09-34 | volunteer | 자원봉사자<br>someone who helps others without getting paid | 사회 / 지속 가능한 발전과 국제 협력 (Sustainable Development and International Cooperation)<br>contextual<br>공동 문제를 해결하려고 사람이나 집단이 자원과 역할을 나누어 협력한다 | A kind volunteer handed out water at the busy race. | One kind volunteer handed out water at the busy race. | Each volunteer helped distribute food packages to families in need. | Each volunteer takes one task, so the village cleanup finishes quickly. |  |
| DAY09-35 | conscious | 의식하는<br>awake and knowing what is happening | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>도덕과 윤리 장면에서 '의식하는'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The sleepy owl stayed conscious until the sun came up. | The sleepy owl remained conscious until the sun came up. | She became conscious of her posture while sitting at desk. | The resting hiker was conscious and knew where the group waited. |  |
| DAY09-36 | tempt | 유혹하다<br>to make someone want something very much | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>도덕과 윤리 장면에서 '유혹하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Warm cookies tempt the hungry raccoon near the open window. | Warm cookies tempt the hungry raccoon close to the open window. | Sweet treats might tempt children to spoil their healthy appetite. | Warm cookies tempt the children waiting beside the kitchen door. |  |
| DAY09-37 | humble | 겸손한<br>not proud of yourself in a loud way | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>도덕과 윤리 장면에서 '겸손한'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The humble champion thanked every teammate after the game. | After the game, the humble champion thanked every teammate. | The humble scientist gave credit to his entire research team. | The winning runner stayed humble and thanked every teammate quietly. |  |
| DAY09-38 | ashamed | 부끄러워하는<br>feeling bad about something you did | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>도덕과 윤리 장면에서 '부끄러워하는'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The kitten felt ashamed after spilling the flour everywhere. | After spilling the flour everywhere, the kitten felt ashamed. | He felt ashamed after breaking his promise to his friend. | I felt ashamed after forgetting my friend's birthday party. |  |
| DAY09-39 | prejudice | 편견<br>an unfair idea about people you do not know | 사회 / 고정관념과 지역 인식 (Stereotypes and Regional Perspectives)<br>direct<br>지역 사람들에 대한 고정관념을 실제 자료와 다양한 관점으로 점검한다 | The story taught that prejudice makes friendship much harder. | Students compare real data with prejudice about another region. | Education helps reduce unfair prejudice against different cultural minority groups. | Real data about the island removed our prejudice about its people. |  |
| DAY09-40 | order | 순서<br>the way things are arranged one after another | 수학 / 유리수의 대소 비교 (Comparing and Ordering Rational Numbers)<br>direct<br>여러 수를 같은 기준으로 비교해 작은 수부터 순서대로 놓는다 | The books sat in neat order along the wooden shelf. | Students place fractions in order from smallest to largest. | Arrange these rational numbers in ascending order from least value. | We placed the five fractions in order from smallest to largest. |  |

## DAY 10 · set 1 · 심리&관계 / 문제와 해결책

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY10-01 | welfare | 복지<br>the health and happiness of people | 사회 / 인권과 헌법상 기본권 (Human Rights and Constitutional Rights)<br>contextual<br>건강·교육·기초 생활을 보장하는 사회적 권리와 복지의 관계를 확인한다 | The town cares about the welfare of every young child. | Free school meals protect children's health and welfare. | Government programs provide social welfare support for citizens in need. | Free school lunches protect the welfare of every child here. |  |
| DAY10-02 | victim | 피해자<br>someone or something harmed by a bad event | 사회 / 사회 갈등과 차별 (Social Conflict and Discrimination)<br>direct<br>집단 간 갈등과 차별의 원인을 찾고 평등한 해결 방안을 비교한다 | The little sapling was a victim of the strong storm. | Classmates support the victim and reject unfair group treatment. | The legal counselor helped the innocent victim of consumer fraud. | Our class heard the victim of unfair teasing and fixed the rule. |  |
| DAY10-03 | population | 인구<br>the number of people living in a place | 사회 / 인구 구조와 인구 피라미드 (Population Structure and Population Pyramids)<br>direct<br>연령별 인구 자료를 인구 피라미드로 나타내 구조를 읽는다 | The penguin population grew larger on the icy island. | A population graph compares the town's children and older adults. | A pyramid chart shows the age structure of the national population. | The town population chart shows more grandparents than young children. |  |
| DAY10-04 | neighbor | 이웃<br>a person who lives near your home | 사회 / 지방 자치와 시민 참여 (Local Self-Government and Civic Participation)<br>direct<br>주민이 지역 문제를 지방 의회나 담당 기관에 제안하고 결과를 확인한다 | Our kind neighbor shares fresh vegetables over the low fence. | A neighbor asks the local council to repair the sidewalk. | The friendly neighbor helped shovel snow from our front walkway. | One neighbor asked the district office to repair the dark streetlight. |  |
| DAY10-05 | ignore | 무시하다<br>to not pay attention to something | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>문제와 해결책 장면에서 '무시하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The busy squirrel will ignore the noisy chattering birds. | Today, the busy squirrel will ignore the noisy chattering birds. | Drivers should never ignore red stop signs at busy intersections. | Please do not ignore the small crack in this wall. |  |
| DAY10-06 | donate | 기부하다<br>to give something to help other people | 사회 / 지속 가능한 발전과 국제 협력 (Sustainable Development and International Cooperation)<br>contextual<br>공동 문제를 해결하는 단체에 필요한 자원과 도움을 제공한다 | Many families donate warm coats to the winter clothing drive. | Today, many families donate warm coats to the winter clothing drive. | Citizens donate warm blankets to help disaster victims rebuild lives. | Families donate warm coats to the group helping village elders. |  |
| DAY10-07 | serious | 심각한<br>important and needing careful thought | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>문제와 해결책 장면에서 '심각한'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The serious young chess player studied the board carefully. | Today, the serious young chess player studied the board carefully. | Water pollution is a serious problem that requires urgent action. | The water leak became serious, so the class called maintenance. |  |
| DAY10-08 | fund | 기금<br>money saved for a special purpose | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>문제와 해결책 장면에서 '기금'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The class started a fund for new library books. | Today, the class started a fund for new library books. | The charity raised a special fund to build clean wells. | Our school keeps a small fund for repairing broken instruments. |  |
| DAY10-09 | personal | 개인의<br>belonging to only one person | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>문제와 해결책 장면에서 '개인의'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Each camper kept a personal cup on the wooden shelf. | Today, each camper kept a personal cup on the wooden shelf. | Keep your personal password secret to protect your online accounts. | This locker holds my personal notebook and nobody else's. |  |
| DAY10-10 | personnel | 직원들<br>all the people who work somewhere | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>문제와 해결책 장면에서 '직원들'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The zoo personnel fed the animals early every single morning. | Today, the zoo personnel fed the animals early every single morning. | Emergency personnel arrived quickly at the scene of the accident. | Hospital personnel meet every morning to plan the day's work. |  |

## DAY 10 · set 2 · 심리&관계 / 문제와 해결책

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY10-11 | half | 절반<br>one of two equal parts | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>문제와 해결책 장면에서 '절반'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The rabbit ate half of the sweet orange carrot. | The small rabbit ate half of the sweet orange carrot. | She cut the round apple into half to share equally. | We cut the sandwich and shared one half each. |  |
| DAY10-12 | concern | 걱정<br>a worried feeling about something | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>문제와 해결책 장면에서 '걱정'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Her main concern was the tiny bird in the rain. | Her main concern was the small bird in the rain. | Air quality is a growing concern for modern city residents. | My biggest concern is the broken lock on the gate. |  |
| DAY10-13 | rescue | 구조하다<br>to save someone from a difficult place | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>문제와 해결책 장면에서 '구조하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Firefighters will rescue the kitten from the tall oak tree. | Today, firefighters will rescue the kitten from the tall oak tree. | Firefighters rushed to rescue the trapped cat from tall tree. | Firefighters rescue a small kitten from the tall pine tree. |  |
| DAY10-14 | critic | 비평가<br>someone who says what is good or bad | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>문제와 해결책 장면에서 '비평가'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The food critic tasted every soup at the village fair. | Today, the food critic tasted every soup at the village fair. | The film critic wrote a balanced review of the movie. | A book critic explained why the ending felt too quick. |  |
| DAY10-15 | charity | 자선<br>a group that helps people who need help | 사회 / 지속 가능한 발전과 국제 협력 (Sustainable Development and International Cooperation)<br>contextual<br>공동 문제를 해결하는 단체에 필요한 자원과 도움을 제공한다 | The charity sent warm blankets to the cold mountain village. | Today, the charity sent warm blankets to the cold mountain village. | The local charity provides hot meals to homeless people daily. | This charity collects rice and blankets for families after storms. |  |
| DAY10-16 | rage | 분노<br>a very strong angry feeling | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>문제와 해결책 장면에서 '분노'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The storybook giant's rage melted when someone offered cake. | When someone offered cake, the storybook giant's rage melted. | He controlled his rage by taking several slow deep breaths. | Deep breathing helped the rage disappear before anyone spoke. |  |
| DAY10-17 | afford | 여유가 되다<br>to have enough money to buy something | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>문제와 해결책 장면에서 '여유가 되다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The mouse could afford one small cheese with its coins. | Today, the mouse could afford one small cheese with its coins. | The family could afford to buy a small second-hand car. | Our club cannot afford two new tents this year. |  |
| DAY10-18 | unite | 연합하다<br>to join together as one group | 사회 / 지속 가능한 발전과 국제 협력 (Sustainable Development and International Cooperation)<br>contextual<br>공동 문제를 해결하려고 사람이나 집단이 자원과 역할을 나누어 협력한다 | The villages unite to build a bridge across the stream. | Nearby towns unite their workers and tools to clean the river. | Nations unite their efforts to fight against global climate change. | Three classes unite to clean the school garden every spring. |  |
| DAY10-19 | orphan | 고아<br>a young animal or child with no parents | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>문제와 해결책 장면에서 '고아'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The kind farmer raised an orphan lamb with warm milk. | Today, the kind farmer raised an orphan lamb with warm milk. | The animal shelter took care of the lonely puppy orphan. | The zoo keepers feed an orphan deer with warm milk. |  |
| DAY10-20 | bear | 참다<br>to keep going through something hard | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>문제와 해결책 장면에서 '참다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Camels can bear the long hot walk across sand. | Today, camels can bear the long hot walk across sand. | He learned to bear the cold winter temperatures with patience. | Runners bear the cold wind until they reach the finish. |  |

## DAY 10 · set 3 · 심리&관계 / 문제와 해결책

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY10-21 | abandon | 버리다<br>to leave something behind and not return | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>문제와 해결책 장면에서 '버리다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The pirates will abandon the old ship on the beach. | Today, the pirates will abandon the old ship on the beach. | Do not abandon your pets when you move to cities. | Please never abandon a pet beside the river path. |  |
| DAY10-22 | crash | 부딪치다<br>to hit something hard by accident | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>문제와 해결책 장면에서 '부딪치다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The toy car might crash into the soft cushion wall. | Today, the toy car might crash into the soft cushion wall. | Two bumper cars crash gently into each other during ride. | The toy car will crash into the wall without brakes. |  |
| DAY10-23 | promote | 승진시키다<br>to give someone a higher position | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>문제와 해결책 장면에서 '승진시키다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The manager will promote the skilled helper to team leader. | Today, the manager will promote the skilled helper to team leader. | The manager decided to promote the hardworking cashier to supervisor. | The bakery owner will promote the careful baker to manager. |  |
| DAY10-24 | decade | 십년<br>a time of ten whole years | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>문제와 해결책 장면에서 '십년'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The oak tree grew tall over one long decade. | Today, the oak tree grew tall over one long decade. | Our town has changed significantly over the past single decade. | This old bridge has carried buses for one whole decade. |  |
| DAY10-25 | establish | 설립하다<br>to start something that will last | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>문제와 해결책 장면에서 '설립하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The friends will establish a small garden club this spring. | Two classmates will establish a small garden club this spring. | The government helped establish a new national park for wildlife. | Students will establish a repair club for broken school chairs. |  |
| DAY10-26 | burden | 짐<br>a heavy load that is hard to carry | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>문제와 해결책 장면에서 '짐'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The donkey carried a burden of soft woolly blankets. | Today, the donkey carried a burden of soft woolly blankets. | Carrying the heavy backpack was a difficult burden for him. | That heavy water burden slowed the donkey on the hill. |  |
| DAY10-27 | assist | 돕다<br>to help someone do something | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>문제와 해결책 장면에서 '돕다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A robot will assist the chef during the busy lunch. | One robot will assist the chef during the busy lunch. | Volunteers assist elderly citizens with their heavy weekly grocery bags. | Older students assist beginners who cannot tie climbing knots. |  |
| DAY10-28 | resolve | 해결하다<br>to find an answer to a problem | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>문제와 해결책 장면에서 '해결하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The friends will resolve the puzzle before dinner time. | Two classmates will resolve the puzzle before dinner time. | Diplomats worked together to resolve the border dispute peacefully today. | We resolve the seating problem by drawing numbers from a box. |  |
| DAY10-29 | detect | 감지하다<br>to notice something that is hard to see | 과학 / 과학적 탐구 (Scientific Inquiry)<br>contextual<br>현상을 일정 시간 관찰하며 변화를 감지하고 자료로 기록한다 | A dog can detect a hidden treat under the blanket. | Students detect leaf changes and record them each morning. | Sensitive smoke alarms detect tiny smoke particles in the kitchen. | We detect a small temperature change after watching the thermometer hourly. |  |
| DAY10-30 | defeat | 패배시키다<br>to win against someone in a game | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>문제와 해결책 장면에서 '패배시키다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The blue team may defeat the red team in chess. | Today, the blue team may defeat the red team in chess. | Our soccer team worked hard to defeat our strongest rivals. | Our board game team can defeat the champions with careful moves. |  |

## DAY 10 · set 4 · 심리&관계 / 문제와 해결책

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
