| DAY10-31 | capture | 포착하다<br>to catch a picture or a moment | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>문제와 해결책 장면에서 '포착하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The camera can capture the bright rainbow above the hills. | The camera can capture the colorful rainbow above the hills. | The photographer managed to capture the sunset over ocean horizon. | The camera can capture the exact moment the seed sprouts. |  |
| DAY10-32 | perspective | 관점<br>the way someone sees or thinks about something | 국어 / 시점과 관점 (Point of view)<br>direct<br>같은 사건을 서로 다른 인물의 관점에서 보고 차이를 설명한다 | From the treetop perspective the village looked very tiny. | Each character's perspective explains the same event differently. | We must consider this social issue from another person's perspective. | From the younger sister's perspective, the same argument looks different. |  |
| DAY10-33 | desperate | 절박한<br>needing something very badly right now | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>문제와 해결책 장면에서 '절박한'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The thirsty plants were desperate for a long gentle rain. | Today, the thirsty plants were desperate for a long gentle rain. | The lost traveler made a desperate search for fresh water. | Farmers were desperate for rain after two very dry weeks. |  |
| DAY10-34 | apparent | 분명한<br>easy to see or understand | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>문제와 해결책 장면에서 '분명한'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | It was apparent that the puppy loved the squeaky toy. | Today, it was apparent that the puppy loved the squeaky toy. | The reason for the machine's failure was apparent to engineers. | The reason for the flood became apparent after checking the drain. |  |
| DAY10-35 | contradict | 반박하다<br>to say the opposite of what someone said | 국어 / 토론과 반박 (Debate and rebuttal)<br>direct<br>주장과 근거를 제시하고 상대 주장에 근거로 반박한다 | The map seemed to contradict the old sailor's simple directions. | Students contradict a weak claim by presenting stronger evidence. | Debaters use verified statistics to contradict their opponent's false claims. | New measurements contradict the claim that the pond is shrinking. |  |
| DAY10-36 | aggressive | 공격적인<br>acting in a strong pushy way | 사회 / 사회 갈등과 차별 (Social Conflict and Discrimination)<br>direct<br>집단 간 갈등과 차별의 원인을 찾고 평등한 해결 방안을 비교한다 | The game robot uses an aggressive but fair playing style. | Students replace aggressive shouting with a fair discussion. | Aggressive behavior in school hallways violates our student code rules. | Aggressive words made the group argument worse, so we set turns. |  |
| DAY10-37 | companion | 동반자<br>a friend who goes places with you | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>문제와 해결책 장면에서 '동반자'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A loyal dog became the shepherd's best mountain companion. | One loyal dog became the shepherd's best mountain companion. | Her loyal dog is a wonderful companion on morning walks. | My hiking companion carried the map during the whole trail. |  |
| DAY10-38 | compromise | 타협<br>an answer where each side gives up something | 국어 / 토의 (Discussion)<br>direct<br>여러 사람이 문제의 대안을 말하고 공통 결론이나 타협점을 찾는다 | The friends found a fair compromise about the last cookie. | Two classmates found a fair compromise about the last cookie. | Both sides reached a sensible compromise after a lengthy meeting. | Our compromise gives the field to soccer players every other day. |  |
| DAY10-39 | commitment | 헌신<br>a promise to keep doing something | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>문제와 해결책 장면에서 '헌신'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Daily practice showed the young violinist's strong commitment. | Daily practice displayed the young violinist's strong commitment. | Finishing this project requires strong commitment from all team members. | This club's commitment to feeding stray cats continued through winter. |  |
| DAY10-40 | stock | 재고<br>goods that a shop keeps ready to sell | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>문제와 해결책 장면에서 '재고'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The shop had plenty of stock before the busy holiday. | The clerk checks the store's stock before ordering more rice. | The bookstore keeps a large stock of popular science books. | The shop's stock of notebooks ran low before the new semester. |  |

## DAY 11 · set 1 · 문화&예술 / 예술과 문학

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY11-01 | publish | 출판하다<br>to print a book so people can read it | 국어 / 정보 전달 글쓰기 (Informative writing)<br>direct<br>독자가 절차나 사실을 이해하도록 정확한 정보를 순서 있게 쓴다 | The young writer will publish her first picture book. | Writers check facts and order steps before they publish a guide. | The local press will publish a new informative science handbook. | Our club will publish a short guide explaining each printing step. |  |
| DAY11-02 | literature | 문학<br>stories and poems that people write | 국어 / 사회적 독서 (Social reading)<br>contextual<br>같은 글을 읽은 친구들이 근거가 되는 구절을 들며 해석을 나눈다 | Old fairy tales are a beloved part of literature. | Friends discuss literature and support interpretations with lines from the story. | Friends read classic literature together and discuss key lines from book. | In literature class we quote lines and compare our different readings. |  |
| DAY11-03 | fiction | 소설<br>stories about things that did not happen | 국어 / 서사 갈등 (Narrative conflict)<br>contextual<br>이야기 속 인물의 목표와 장애물이 사건의 갈등을 어떻게 만드는지 찾는다 | The talking bear tale is pure fiction, not real history. | In this fiction, a storm blocks the sailor's goal. | The exciting historical fiction describes a young hero's difficult journey. | This fiction shows a hero blocked by a locked garden gate. |  |
| DAY11-04 | author | 저자<br>the person who wrote a book | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>예술과 문학 장면에서 '저자'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The author signed books for children at the library. | Today, the author signed books for children at the library. | The popular children's book author visited our local school library. | The author signed copies of the new picture book yesterday. |  |
| DAY11-05 | symbol | 상징<br>a shape that stands for an idea | 국어 / 운율·비유·상징 (Poetic devices)<br>direct<br>시에서 반복되는 소리와 비유·상징이 만드는 느낌을 찾는다 | A dove is often a symbol of peace and calm. | One dove is often a symbol of peace and calm. | In traditional poetry, the white dove is a peace symbol. | In this poem the white bird is a symbol of peace. |  |
| DAY11-06 | plot | 줄거리<br>the story of what happens in a book | 국어 / 서사 갈등 (Narrative conflict)<br>direct<br>등장인물의 목표와 장애물·갈등이 사건을 어떻게 움직이는지 정리한다 | The plot of the pirate story surprised every reader. | The plot changes when a locked door blocks the hero. | The mystery novel has an exciting plot with surprising twists. | We wrote the plot showing how the lost map caused trouble. |  |
| DAY11-07 | collaborate | 협력하다<br>to work together on the same project | 국어 / 토의 (Discussion)<br>direct<br>여러 사람이 문제의 대안을 말하고 공통 결론이나 타협점을 찾는다 | Two artists collaborate on one huge colorful wall painting. | Group members collaborate by comparing ideas and choosing one plan. | Students collaborate in small groups to solve complex math problems. | Three writers collaborate and agree on one ending for the play. |  |
| DAY11-08 | statue | 조각상<br>a stone or metal figure of someone | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>예술과 문학 장면에서 '조각상'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A stone statue of a lion guarded the museum steps. | One stone statue of a lion guarded the museum steps. | The bronze statue stands in the center of the park. | A stone statue of an old scholar stands beside the gate. |  |
| DAY11-09 | state | 상태<br>the condition something is in | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>예술과 문학 장면에서 '상태'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The old book was in perfect state after many years. | The restorer checks the old painting's state before cleaning it. | Ice transitions to a liquid state when temperature rises above zero. | Workers checked the state of every wooden frame before the show. |  |
| DAY11-10 | status | 지위<br>the position someone has in a group | 사회 / 사회적 지위와 사회적 역할 (Social Status and Social Role)<br>direct<br>한 사람이 가진 지위와 그에 기대되는 역할을 구별한다 | The queen bee holds the highest status in the hive. | Her status is class president, so she leads the meeting. | In ancient times, royal status brought special privileges and responsibilities. | Winning the prize changed the young painter's status inside the club. |  |

## DAY 11 · set 2 · 문화&예술 / 예술과 문학

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY11-11 | fit | 맞다<br>to be the right size for something | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>예술과 문학 장면에서 '맞다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | These small boots fit the little penguin just right. | These small boots fit the small penguin just right. | These comfortable running shoes fit my feet perfectly for sports. | This costume does not fit the tallest actor in our play. |  |
| DAY11-12 | movement | 움직임<br>the act of changing place or position | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>예술과 문학 장면에서 '움직임'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A soft movement of the curtain showed the gentle breeze. | A soft movement of the curtain displayed the gentle breeze. | The slow graceful movement of the dancer impressed the audience. | One slow arm movement started the whole dance on stage. |  |
| DAY11-13 | brilliant | 훌륭한<br>very clever or very bright | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>예술과 문학 장면에서 '훌륭한'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The inventor had a brilliant idea about folding wings. | Today, the inventor had a brilliant idea about folding wings. | The young scientist proposed a brilliant solution to save water. | That brilliant stage design used only paper and colored lights. |  |
| DAY11-14 | prior | 이전의<br>coming before something else | fallback<br>word_fallback<br>한 장면에서 '이전의'의 확정 뜻이 다른 뜻으로 오해되지 않게 직접 보여 준다 | The prior drawing was smaller than this new one. | Today, the prior drawing was smaller than this new one. | She had no prior experience before joining the school band. | The prior painting was smaller than this new one. |  |
| DAY11-15 | outline | 개요<br>a short plan showing the main ideas | 국어 / 요약 (Summary)<br>direct<br>긴 글에서 핵심 내용만 골라 짧은 개요와 결론으로 정리한다 | The student arranges picture cards into a story outline. | Students outline only the article's main ideas and conclusion. | Write a brief outline of the story before drafting paragraphs. | We wrote a short outline with the story's three main events. |  |
| DAY11-16 | sort | 분류하다<br>to put things into groups by type | 과학 / 생물 분류 (Biological Classification)<br>contextual<br>생물의 공통 특징을 기준으로 여러 무리를 나누어 분류한다 | The children sort colorful buttons into four small bowls. | Students sort animals by feathers, fur, or scales. | Biologists sort various living organisms into distinct kingdoms and families. | Students sort the leaf pictures by vein shape and edge type. |  |
| DAY11-17 | portrait | 초상화<br>a picture of a person's face | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>예술과 문학 장면에서 '초상화'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A painted portrait of the kind farmer hung upstairs. | One painted portrait of the kind farmer hung upstairs. | The famous artist painted a lifelike oil portrait of queen. | The museum hung a portrait of a smiling old farmer. |  |
| DAY11-18 | tale | 이야기<br>a story that someone tells you | 국어 / 서사 갈등 (Narrative conflict)<br>contextual<br>이야기 속 인물의 목표와 장애물이 사건의 갈등을 어떻게 만드는지 찾는다 | The old sailor told a tale about a friendly whale. | The tale follows a sailor seeking home through a storm. | Grandmother told an ancient folk tale by the warm fireplace. | In this tale, a fox wants grapes hanging too high. |  |
| DAY11-19 | add | 더하다<br>to put more of something in | 수학 / 정수와 유리수의 사칙연산 (Operations with Integers and Rational Numbers)<br>direct<br>정수나 분수를 더하고 빼고 곱하고 나누어 값을 구한다 | Please add two spoons of honey to the warm milk. | Students add one half and one quarter using fraction strips. | You must add positive integers to calculate the total sum. | When we add one third and one third, we get two thirds. |  |
| DAY11-20 | volume | 음량<br>how loud a sound is | 국어 / 음운과 문자 체계 (Phonology and writing systems)<br>contextual<br>말소리의 크기와 들림 정도를 비교해 소리의 특징을 설명한다 | Turn the volume down before the baby bird sleeps. | The speaker lowers the microphone volume until everyone hears clearly. | The speaker adjusts the microphone volume so the audience hears clearly. | Lower the radio volume until the singer's soft words become clear. |  |

## DAY 11 · set 3 · 문화&예술 / 예술과 문학

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY11-21 | wisdom | 지혜<br>knowing the right thing to do | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>예술과 문학 장면에서 '지혜'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The old tortoise shared wisdom with the young forest animals. | Today, the old tortoise shared wisdom with the young forest animals. | Old proverbs contain valuable wisdom passed down through many generations. | The old storyteller's wisdom helped the village choose a fair plan. |  |
| DAY11-22 | impressed | 감명받은<br>feeling that something is very good | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>예술과 문학 장면에서 '감명받은'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Everyone felt impressed by the tall sandcastle on the beach. | Today, everyone felt impressed by the tall sandcastle on the beach. | The art teacher was impressed by her creative watercolor painting. | Visitors looked impressed by the huge paper crane sculpture. |  |
| DAY11-23 | emphasize | 강조하다<br>to show that something is very important | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>예술과 문학 장면에서 '강조하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The coach will emphasize teamwork before the friendly relay race. | A sports teacher will emphasize teamwork before the helpful relay race. | Good writers emphasize main ideas using strong examples and evidence. | Painters emphasize the main figure by using the brightest color. |  |
| DAY11-24 | bold | 대담한<br>brave and not afraid to try | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>예술과 문학 장면에서 '대담한'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A bold little mouse stepped onto the wide open field. | One bold little mouse stepped onto the wide open field. | The explorer made a bold decision to cross the desert. | One bold student sang alone before the whole audience. |  |
| DAY11-25 | admire | 존경하다<br>to look at something with great respect | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>예술과 문학 장면에서 '존경하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The students admire the giant painting in the quiet hall. | Several students admire the giant painting in the quiet hall. | Young athletes admire the Olympic champion for her sportsmanship. | Young dancers admire the teacher who trained for thirty years. |  |
| DAY11-26 | vivid | 생생한<br>very bright and easy to picture | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>예술과 문학 장면에서 '생생한'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The parrot had vivid green and orange feathers everywhere. | Today, the parrot had vivid green and orange feathers everywhere. | The author used vivid sensory details to describe the forest. | The novel's vivid description shows red apples and wet grass. |  |
| DAY11-27 | multiple | 다수의<br>many, more than one | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>예술과 문학 장면에서 '다수의'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Multiple rainbows appeared above the wet green valley today. | Now, multiple rainbows appeared above the wet green valley today. | The test question had multiple possible answers to consider carefully. | This play has multiple endings, and each group chooses one. |  |
| DAY11-28 | profound | 깊은<br>very deep and full of meaning | fallback<br>word_fallback<br>한 장면에서 '깊은'의 확정 뜻이 다른 뜻으로 오해되지 않게 직접 보여 준다 | The quiet forest gave the hikers a profound calm feeling. | The quiet forest offered the hikers a profound calm feeling. | The ancient philosopher shared profound thoughts on virtue and justice. | The last line left a profound feeling in every reader. |  |
| DAY11-29 | terrible | 끔찍한<br>very bad or unpleasant | fallback<br>word_fallback<br>한 장면에서 '끔찍한'의 확정 뜻이 다른 뜻으로 오해되지 않게 직접 보여 준다 | The burnt toast tasted terrible, so everyone laughed together. | Today, the burnt toast tasted terrible, so everyone laughed together. | The sudden severe storm caused terrible damage to coastal houses. | Those burnt cookies tasted terrible, so nobody finished them. |  |
| DAY11-30 | terrific | 훌륭한<br>very good and wonderful | fallback<br>word_fallback<br>한 장면에서 '훌륭한'의 확정 뜻이 다른 뜻으로 오해되지 않게 직접 보여 준다 | The puppet show was terrific from beginning to end. | Today, the puppet show was terrific from beginning to end. | The school choir gave a terrific musical performance tonight in auditorium. | Our school band played a terrific song at the festival. |  |

## DAY 11 · set 4 · 문화&예술 / 예술과 문학

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY11-31 | calculate | 계산하다<br>to work out an answer with numbers | 수학 / 정수와 유리수의 사칙연산 (Operations with Integers and Rational Numbers)<br>direct<br>정수나 분수를 더하고 빼고 곱하고 나누어 값을 구한다 | The robot can calculate the seeds needed for each row. | Students calculate one half plus one quarter using fraction pieces. | Students calculate the final sum by adding positive and negative integers. | We calculate three times four and write twelve on the poster. |  |
| DAY11-32 | opportunity | 기회<br>a good chance to do something | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>예술과 문학 장면에서 '기회'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Rainy days give a great opportunity for indoor board games. | Today, rainy days give a great opportunity for indoor board games. | Summer camp offers a wonderful opportunity to learn outdoor survival. | The contest gives every student one opportunity to read aloud. |  |
| DAY11-33 | poetry | 시<br>writing with short lines and pretty sounds | 국어 / 운율·비유·상징 (Poetic devices)<br>direct<br>시에서 반복되는 소리와 비유·상징이 만드는 느낌을 찾는다 | The class wrote poetry about falling autumn leaves today. | Students read poetry and notice repeated sounds and symbols. | Students read classical lyrical poetry aloud to enjoy rhythmic sounds. | This poetry repeats soft s sounds in every second line. |  |
| DAY11-34 | recall | 기억해내다<br>to remember something from before | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>예술과 문학 장면에서 '기억해내다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Can you recall the name of that tiny island? | Today, can you recall the name of that tiny island? | He tried to recall the phone number from memory quickly. | Older readers recall the ending of this famous folk tale. |  |
| DAY11-35 | inferior | 열등한<br>not as good as something else | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>예술과 문학 장면에서 '열등한'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The paper kite was inferior to the strong silk one. | Today, the paper kite was inferior to the strong silk one. | Cheap plastic tools are often inferior in durability to steel. | Thin drawing paper is inferior to the thick painting paper. |  |
| DAY11-36 | spirit | 정신<br>the feeling or mood inside a person | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>예술과 문학 장면에서 '정신'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The team kept a cheerful spirit through the long game. | The club kept a cheerful spirit through the long game. | The athletic team displayed great team spirit throughout the match. | The singers kept a cheerful spirit through the long rehearsal. |  |
| DAY11-37 | fade | 희미해지다<br>to slowly become weaker or lighter | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>예술과 문학 장면에서 '희미해지다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The bright poster colors fade after many sunny days. | The colorful poster colors fade after many sunny days. | Bright clothing colors gradually fade after many wash cycles in water. | Sunlight made the old poster colors fade over three summers. |  |
| DAY11-38 | generation | 세대<br>all the people born around the same time | 사회 / 인구 구조와 인구 피라미드 (Population Structure and Population Pyramids)<br>direct<br>연령별 인구 자료를 인구 피라미드로 나타내 구조를 읽는다 | Each generation of birds returns to the same green cliff. | The chart compares each generation by age group. | A population pyramid displays each generation within the national census. | Students counted each generation and drew the village age pyramid. |  |
| DAY11-39 | masterpiece | 걸작<br>the very best work someone has made | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>예술과 문학 장면에서 '걸작'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The painter called this bright mural her greatest masterpiece. | Today, the painter called this bright mural her greatest masterpiece. | The museum displayed a timeless artistic masterpiece from the Renaissance. | This painting is the artist's masterpiece after forty years of work. |  |
| DAY11-40 | figure | 알아내다<br>to understand or find out | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>예술과 문학 장면에서 '알아내다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | I cannot figure out how to solve this difficult math problem. | Today, I cannot figure out how to solve this difficult math problem. | Can you figure out the correct answer to this puzzle? | Readers figure out the ending from three small clues. |  |

## DAY 12 · set 1 · 문화&예술 / 미디어와 음악

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY12-01 | perform | 공연하다<br>to sing, act, or play for other people | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>미디어와 음악 장면에서 '공연하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The young dancers perform on stage every autumn evening. | Today, the young dancers perform on stage every autumn evening. | The school orchestra will perform classical music in the auditorium. | Six students perform a short folk song on Friday afternoon. |  |
| DAY12-02 | feature | 특징<br>an important part of something | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>미디어와 음악 장면에서 '특징'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The tall glass roof is the best feature here. | Today, the tall glass roof is the best feature here. | A prominent feature of this camera is its optical zoom. | The loudest feature of this drum is its deep sound. |  |
| DAY12-03 | genre | 장르<br>a type of story, music, or art | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>미디어와 음악 장면에서 '장르'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Adventure is her favorite genre of storybook to read. | Today, adventure is her favorite genre of storybook to read. | Science fiction is a popular literary genre among young readers. | Folk music is my favorite genre for quiet evening listening. |  |
| DAY12-04 | instrument | 악기<br>a thing you play to make music | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>미디어와 음악 장면에서 '악기'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The violin is a lovely instrument with four strings. | Today, the violin is a lovely instrument with four strings. | The violin is a classical string instrument played with bow. | My sister plays a small wooden instrument with six strings. |  |
| DAY12-05 | broadcast | 방송하다<br>to send sound or pictures to many people | 국어 / 대중매체와 개인 방송 (Mass and creator media)<br>direct<br>방송과 개인 제작 매체가 정보를 고르고 전달하는 방식을 비교한다 | The radio will broadcast the concert across the whole valley. | The station will broadcast edited news to thousands of listeners. | Local television stations broadcast weather warnings during severe winter storms. | Radio stations broadcast the weather, while video makers choose personal stories. |  |
| DAY12-06 | artwork | 예술 작품<br>something made as art, like a painting | 국어 / 복합양식 매체 제작 (Multimodal media production)<br>direct<br>대본·영상·소리 자료를 역할에 맞게 묶어 하나의 매체 자료를 만든다 | Colorful artwork covered every wall of the small gallery. | Students combine narration, music, and artwork in one video. | Students created digital artwork combining photos and recorded voice narrations. | Our artwork joins painted scenes with recorded drum sounds. |  |
| DAY12-07 | suit | 어울리다<br>to look good on someone | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>미디어와 음악 장면에서 '어울리다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Bright yellow scarves suit the cheerful little snow bear. | These soft blue shoes suit the dancer's silver costume. | These bright yellow frames suit your round face shape nicely. | Bright yellow scarves suit the dancers in this stage light. |  |
| DAY12-08 | inspire | 영감을 주다<br>to give someone a good idea or hope | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>미디어와 음악 장면에서 '영감을 주다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Ocean waves inspire the young painter every single morning. | Today, ocean waves inspire the young painter every single morning. | Inspiring real stories inspire young students to pursue their dreams. | The old song can inspire young writers to try new lyrics. |  |
| DAY12-09 | belief | 믿음<br>something you think is true | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>미디어와 음악 장면에서 '믿음'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Her strong belief in kindness changed the whole classroom. | Today, her strong belief in kindness changed the whole classroom. | Freedom of religion protects personal belief without government interference. | My belief is that practice matters more than natural talent. |  |
| DAY12-10 | brief | 짧은<br>lasting only a short time | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>미디어와 음악 장면에서 '짧은'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A brief rain shower cooled the warm summer garden. | One brief rain shower cooled the warm summer garden. | The news anchor gave a brief summary of daily events. | A brief drum solo opened the school concert last night. |  |

## DAY 12 · set 2 · 문화&예술 / 미디어와 음악

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY12-11 | upset | 속상한<br>feeling sad or worried about something | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>미디어와 음악 장면에서 '속상한'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The little duck felt upset about the broken toy boat. | The small duck felt upset about the broken toy boat. | She felt upset when she lost her favorite library book. | The singer felt upset when the microphone stopped working. |  |
| DAY12-12 | respond | 응답하다<br>to answer or react to something | 국어 / 공감적 경청 (Active listening)<br>contextual<br>상대의 말을 정확히 들은 뒤 내용과 감정에 맞게 응답한다 | The plants respond to sunlight by turning their leaves. | Listeners restate the concern and respond with a helpful question. | Listeners must respond politely when someone asks a direct question. | Listeners respond to the caller's worry with a calm, matching answer. |  |
| DAY12-13 | steady | 꾸준한<br>moving or going at the same rate | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>미디어와 음악 장면에서 '꾸준한'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A steady rain fell all night on the tin roof. | One steady rain fell all night on the tin roof. | He maintained a steady study habit throughout the entire semester. | The drummer kept a steady beat through the whole song. |  |
| DAY12-14 | dislike | 싫어하다<br>to not like something at all | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>미디어와 음악 장면에서 '싫어하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Most cats dislike getting their soft paws very wet. | Today, most cats dislike getting their soft paws very wet. | Some young children strongly dislike bitter green vegetable dishes. | Some listeners dislike loud endings and prefer quiet final notes. |  |
| DAY12-15 | define | 정의하다<br>to explain what a word means | 국어 / 설명과 논증 (Explanation and argument)<br>direct<br>원인과 결과 또는 주장과 근거의 연결을 분명히 설명한다 | Can you define this new word for the class? | Students define evaporation, then explain its cause with evidence. | Dictionaries clearly define each vocabulary word with accurate explanations. | Let us define 'chorus' as the part that repeats after verses. |  |
| DAY12-16 | celebrity | 유명인<br>a person who is famous to many people | 국어 / 대중매체와 개인 방송 (Mass and creator media)<br>direct<br>방송과 개인 제작 매체가 정보를 고르고 전달하는 방식을 비교한다 | The singing frog became a celebrity in the whole pond. | A television celebrity reaches more viewers than a local creator. | The famous sports celebrity appeared in a public safety commercial. | One celebrity answered fan questions on a live video channel. |  |
| DAY12-17 | genius | 천재<br>a person who is very, very clever | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>미디어와 음악 장면에서 '천재'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The little inventor was a genius with springs and gears. | The small inventor was a genius with springs and gears. | The young mathematical genius solved the complex equation in seconds. | That young violin genius practiced eight hours every day. |  |
| DAY12-18 | visual | 시각적인<br>using pictures that you look at | 국어 / 복합양식 읽기 (Multimodal reading)<br>contextual<br>글과 사진·그림이 주는 정보를 함께 읽고 서로 보완하는지 확인한다 | The map used visual signs instead of long word lists. | A visual diagram explains details missing from the short paragraph. | Graphic charts provide helpful visual evidence to support written arguments. | The visual chart and the written text explain the same result. |  |
| DAY12-19 | compose | 작곡하다<br>to write music or a song | 국어 / 복합양식 매체 제작 (Multimodal media production)<br>direct<br>대본·영상·소리 자료를 역할에 맞게 묶어 하나의 매체 자료를 만든다 | The bear will compose a gentle song for the cubs. | Students compose music that matches the video's quiet ending. | Musicians compose beautiful background melodies for student short film projects. | Two students compose the background music for our class video. |  |
| DAY12-20 | deal | 거래<br>an agreement between two people | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>미디어와 음악 장면에서 '거래'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The two traders made a fair deal about the apples. | Today, the two traders made a fair deal about the apples. | The two trading companies signed an important business deal today. | Both sides signed a deal for two free school concerts. |  |

## DAY 12 · set 3 · 문화&예술 / 미디어와 음악

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY12-21 | poll | 투표 조사<br>a count of what people choose | 사회 / 정치과정과 여론 (Political Process and Public Opinion)<br>direct<br>설문과 토론으로 모인 여론이 정책 의제와 결정에 반영되는 과정을 추적한다 | The class poll showed that most children like winter. | A poll shows strong support, so the council discusses bike lanes. | The student council conducted an opinion poll before making decisions. | Our class poll showed most students want a longer lunch break. |  |
| DAY12-22 | pitch | 던지다<br>to throw a ball toward someone | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>미디어와 음악 장면에서 '던지다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The player will pitch the soft ball to a friend. | Today, the player will pitch the soft ball to a friend. | The baseball player learned how to pitch a fast strike. | The coach will pitch the ball slowly to young players. |  |
| DAY12-23 | eager | 열망하는<br>wanting to do something very much | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>미디어와 음악 장면에서 '열망하는'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The eager puppy waited by the door for a walk. | Today, the eager puppy waited by the door for a walk. | Curious students were eager to start the new chemistry experiment. | Young fans looked eager to hear the first drum beat. |  |
| DAY12-24 | silly | 우스꽝스러운<br>funny in a not serious way | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>미디어와 음악 장면에서 '우스꽝스러운'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The clown wore silly shoes that squeaked with each step. | Today, the clown wore silly shoes that squeaked with each step. | Wearing two different shoes to school looked rather silly today. | The clown's silly hat made the whole audience laugh together. |  |
| DAY12-25 | furious | 몹시 화난<br>feeling very, very angry | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>미디어와 음악 장면에서 '몹시 화난'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The cartoon dragon looked furious about the empty cookie jar. | Today, the cartoon dragon looked furious about the empty cookie jar. | The bus driver was furious about the reckless cyclist's behavior. | A cartoon dragon looked furious when someone hid its hat. |  |
| DAY12-26 | barely | 간신히<br>almost not at all | fallback<br>word_fallback<br>한 장면에서 '간신히'의 확정 뜻이 다른 뜻으로 오해되지 않게 직접 보여 준다 | The kitten could barely reach the toy under the sofa. | Today, the kitten could barely reach the toy under the sofa. | The tired hiker could barely reach the mountain summit shelter. | We barely finished the song before the bell rang. |  |
| DAY12-27 | thrill | 짜릿함<br>a sudden happy excited feeling | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>미디어와 음악 장면에서 '짜릿함'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The first snowfall gave the children a wonderful thrill. | The first snowfall offered the students a wonderful thrill. | Riding the giant roller coaster gave passengers an exciting thrill. | The first stage light gave the young singer a thrill. |  |
| DAY12-28 | particular | 특정한<br>one special one, not any other | fallback<br>word_fallback<br>한 장면에서 '특정한'의 확정 뜻이 다른 뜻으로 오해되지 않게 직접 보여 준다 | This particular seashell has a perfect pink spiral shape. | Today, this particular seashell has a perfect pink spiral shape. | She was looking for one particular book in the library. | This particular song needs two guitars and one soft drum. |  |
| DAY12-29 | constant | 끊임없는<br>happening all the time without stopping | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>미디어와 음악 장면에서 '끊임없는'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The constant hum of bees filled the flower garden. | Today, the constant hum of bees filled the flower garden. | Traffic noise creates constant background sound in large modern cities. | Constant street noise made the outdoor recording very difficult. |  |
| DAY12-30 | consistent | 일관된<br>always the same and not changing | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>미디어와 음악 장면에서 '일관된'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The baker keeps a consistent taste in every honey loaf. | Today, the baker keeps a consistent taste in every honey loaf. | Her consistent daily practice led to steady improvement on piano. | The choir's singing volume stayed consistent from start to end. |  |

## DAY 12 · set 4 · 문화&예술 / 미디어와 음악

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY12-31 | employ | 고용하다<br>to give someone a job and pay them | 사회 / 노동권 (Labor Rights)<br>direct<br>임금·안전·단결권 등 일터의 기본 권리를 사례에서 확인한다 | The farm will employ three helpers during apple picking season. | The farm will employ workers with fair pay and safe tools. | Local businesses employ skilled workers from the surrounding neighborhood area. | The theater will employ ten workers and provide safety training. |  |
| DAY12-32 | illusion | 착각<br>something that looks real but is not | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>미디어와 음악 장면에서 '착각'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The shimmering water was only an illusion in the desert. | Today, the shimmering water was only an illusion in the desert. | Mirages in hot deserts create an optical illusion of water. | Painted floor lines create an illusion of a deep hole. |  |
| DAY12-33 | funeral | 장례식<br>a time when people say goodbye to someone | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>미디어와 음악 장면에서 '장례식'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The village held a quiet funeral for the old oak. | Today, the village held a quiet funeral for the old oak. | Friends and family gathered solemnly for the memorial funeral service. | Quiet music played during the funeral for the village elder. |  |
| DAY12-34 | whisper | 속삭이다<br>to speak in a very soft voice | 국어 / 대화의 원리 (Dialogue principles)<br>direct<br>대화 차례와 상황에 맞게 인사·요청·응답을 주고받는다 | The friends whisper secrets under the big blanket fort. | Students whisper requests and wait for quiet replies. | Students must whisper quietly when speaking inside the library room. | Backstage helpers whisper short answers so the show continues quietly. |  |
| DAY12-35 | elaborate | 정교한<br>made with many careful details | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>미디어와 음악 장면에서 '정교한'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The snow palace had an elaborate roof of ice flowers. | Today, the snow palace had an elaborate roof of ice flowers. | The carpenter built an elaborate wooden cabinet with intricate carvings. | This elaborate paper mask took three weeks to finish. |  |
| DAY12-36 | bunch | 다발<br>a group of things held together | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>미디어와 음악 장면에서 '다발'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A bunch of yellow bananas hung from the tall tree. | One bunch of yellow bananas hung from the tall tree. | She bought a fresh bunch of red roses for grandmother. | Someone placed a bunch of yellow flowers beside the piano. |  |
| DAY12-37 | atmosphere | 분위기<br>the feeling a place gives you | 국어 / 배경과 사회문화 맥락 (Setting and social context)<br>contextual<br>작품의 시간·장소·사회 배경이 인물 행동에 주는 영향을 찾는다 | The library had a calm and cozy evening atmosphere. | The stormy setting creates an uneasy atmosphere for the hero. | Dim candlelight created a cozy atmosphere inside the small cafe. | Rainy village scenes give this film a lonely atmosphere. |  |
| DAY12-38 | escape | 탈출하다<br>to get away from a closed place | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>미디어와 음악 장면에서 '탈출하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The hamster tried to escape from its round plastic ball. | Today, the hamster tried to escape from its round plastic ball. | Small animals try to escape when they spot hungry predators. | In the story, the rabbit will escape from the wooden cage. |  |
| DAY12-39 | cultivate | 재배하다<br>to grow plants and care for them | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>미디어와 음악 장면에서 '재배하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Farmers cultivate rice in the wide flooded green fields. | Today, farmers cultivate rice in the wide flooded green fields. | Farmers cultivate organic tomatoes in sunny greenhouses during early spring. | Our music club members cultivate herbs beside the practice room. |  |
| DAY12-40 | cast | 배역진<br>all the actors in a show | 국어 / 복합양식 매체 제작 (Multimodal media production)<br>direct<br>대본·영상·소리 자료를 역할에 맞게 묶어 하나의 매체 자료를 만든다 | The whole cast bowed together after the funny play. | The cast combines acting, sound, and props for one show. | The director selected an enthusiastic cast of actors for play. | The whole cast recorded their lines before the filming day. |  |

## DAY 13 · set 1 · 문화&예술 / 문화와 종교

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY13-01 | represent | 대표하다<br>to speak or act for a group | 사회 / 대의 민주주의와 참여 민주주의 (Representative and Participatory Democracy)<br>direct<br>대표를 뽑는 방식과 시민이 직접 의견을 내는 방식을 비교한다 | Two students represent the class at the spring meeting. | Elected students represent classmates while others speak directly at meetings. | Elected representatives represent the interests of their local community members. | Two elected students represent our class at the school meeting. |  |
| DAY13-02 | exhibit | 전시하다<br>to show something for people to see | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>문화와 종교 장면에서 '전시하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The museum will exhibit old pots from a river village. | Today, the museum will exhibit old pots from a river village. | Museums exhibit traditional folk costumes from various global cultural regions. | The museum will exhibit old wedding clothes from three regions. |  |
| DAY13-03 | minister | 장관<br>an important leader in a government | 사회 / 사회적 지위와 사회적 역할 (Social Status and Social Role)<br>direct<br>한 사람이 가진 지위와 그에 기대되는 역할을 구별한다 | The minister opened the new bridge with a warm speech. | The minister leads the department and explains its new policy. | The education minister announced new policies to support public schools. | The culture minister opened the festival and explained new library plans. |  |
| DAY13-04 | vary | 다양하다<br>to be different from one another | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>문화와 종교 장면에서 '다양하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Butterfly wings vary in color across the wide meadow. | Today, butterfly wings vary in color across the wide meadow. | Traditional festival customs vary greatly across different regions and cultures. | Holiday foods vary from village to village across this country. |  |
| DAY13-05 | tribe | 부족<br>a group of families living together | 사회 / 문화경관과 문화 다양성 (Cultural Landscapes and Cultural Diversity)<br>direct<br>지역의 건축·의복·음식·종교가 만든 문화경관의 다양성을 비교한다 | The mountain tribe shared songs around a warm fire. | The tribe preserves its language, clothing, and harvest festival. | The isolated mountain tribe preserves its unique ancient dialect traditions. | This tribe builds round houses and wears woven grass hats. |  |
| DAY13-06 | community | 공동체<br>all the people living in one area | 사회 / 지방 자치와 시민 참여 (Local Self-Government and Civic Participation)<br>direct<br>주민이 지역 문제를 지방 의회나 담당 기관에 제안하고 결과를 확인한다 | The whole community planted trees along the sunny riverbank. | The community asks its council to improve the riverside path. | Residents in the community met to discuss local traffic safety. | People in our community joined the town meeting about the old market. |  |
| DAY13-07 | consider | 고려하다<br>to think carefully before deciding | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>문화와 종교 장면에서 '고려하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Please consider two colors before painting the wooden fence. | Kindly consider two colors before painting the wooden fence. | You should consider all possible options before making final choices. | We consider the weather before choosing the festival date. |  |
| DAY13-08 | concept | 개념<br>an idea about how something works | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>문화와 종교 장면에서 '개념'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Sharing is an easy concept for young children to learn. | Sharing is an easy concept for school children to learn. | The teacher explained the basic scientific concept of energy conservation. | The concept of sharing food appears in many harvest festivals. |  |
| DAY13-09 | pray | 기도하다<br>to speak quietly to ask for something | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>문화와 종교 장면에서 '기도하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The villagers pray for gentle rain during the dry season. | Local residents pray for gentle rain during the dry season. | Devout worshippers pray together peacefully inside the historic city cathedral. | Visitors pray quietly beside the candles in the stone hall. |  |
| DAY13-10 | prey | 먹이<br>an animal that another animal eats | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>문화와 종교 장면에서 '먹이'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Small fish are the prey of the fast diving bird. | Today, small fish are the prey of the fast diving bird. | Hawks spot small prey from high up in the sky. | A hawk searches for prey above the wide golden field. |  |

## DAY 13 · set 2 · 문화&예술 / 문화와 종교

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY13-11 | myth | 신화<br>a very old story about gods or heroes | 국어 / 해석과 상호텍스트성 (Interpretation and intertextuality)<br>direct<br>작품의 구절을 근거로 뜻을 해석하고 다른 이야기와 연결한다 | The myth told how the moon became silver white. | Readers compare one myth with another using matching story clues. | Ancient Greek myth explains natural phenomena through exciting heroic stories. | This myth about a sun god matches an older village story. |  |
| DAY13-12 | attitude | 태도<br>the way you think and act about things | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>문화와 종교 장면에서 '태도'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A cheerful attitude made the long hike feel shorter. | One cheerful attitude made the long hike feel shorter. | A respectful attitude helps people appreciate different cultural heritage customs. | A calm attitude helped the guide answer many visitor questions. |  |
| DAY13-13 | priest | 사제<br>a leader in a place of worship | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>문화와 종교 장면에서 '사제'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The kind priest greeted visitors at the old temple. | Today, the kind priest greeted visitors at the old temple. | The parish priest led the solemn Sunday morning worship service. | The village priest rang the old bell at sunrise. |  |
| DAY13-14 | approve | 승인하다<br>to say that something is good or allowed | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>문화와 종교 장면에서 '승인하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The teachers approve the new plan for the school garden. | Today, the teachers approve the new plan for the school garden. | The town council voted to approve the new park budget. | Elders approve the new festival plan after a long discussion. |  |
| DAY13-15 | carve | 조각하다<br>to cut shapes into wood or stone | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>문화와 종교 장면에서 '조각하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Artists carve gentle animal shapes into the soft wood. | Today, artists carve gentle animal shapes into the soft wood. | Artists carve beautiful decorative patterns into soft blocks of wood. | Artists carve small flower shapes into the temple's wooden door. |  |
| DAY13-16 | primitive | 원시의<br>very simple and from long ago | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>문화와 종교 장면에서 '원시의'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Primitive tools made of stone rested inside the display case. | Primitive tools made of stone rested within the display case. | Early humans made primitive tools using chipped stones and bones. | These primitive stone tools were found near the river cave. |  |
| DAY13-17 | invade | 침입하다<br>to come into a place without being asked | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>문화와 종교 장면에서 '침입하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Weeds may invade the garden if nobody pulls them. | Today, weeds may invade the garden if nobody pulls them. | Armies would invade neighboring lands to seize control of territory. | Wild goats sometimes invade the garden and eat young cabbage. |  |
| DAY13-18 | arise | 생기다<br>to begin or appear | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>문화와 종교 장면에서 '생기다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | New questions arise whenever the class studies old maps. | Today, new questions arise whenever the class studies old maps. | Unexpected difficulties may arise during large construction projects in winter. | New questions arise whenever we compare two different customs. |  |
| DAY13-19 | register | 등록하다<br>to put your name on a list | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>문화와 종교 장면에서 '등록하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | You must register early to join the swimming class. | Today, you must register early to join the swimming class. | Students must register online before attending the weekend coding workshop. | Please register your name before joining the pottery class. |  |
| DAY13-20 | custom | 관습<br>something people always do in their group | 사회 / 문화경관과 문화 다양성 (Cultural Landscapes and Cultural Diversity)<br>direct<br>지역의 건축·의복·음식·종교가 만든 문화경관의 다양성을 비교한다 | Bowing politely is a lovely custom in many countries. | Visitors compare one food custom across three regions. | Wearing hanbok on Lunar New Year is a treasured custom. | Bowing to elders is a common custom in our neighborhood. |  |

## DAY 13 · set 3 · 문화&예술 / 문화와 종교

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY13-21 | punish | 벌하다<br>to give someone trouble for breaking rules | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>문화와 종교 장면에서 '벌하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The game will punish a wrong move with lost points. | Today, the game will punish a wrong move with lost points. | Judges punish lawbreakers according to established legal rules and statutes. | Game rules punish players who move before the whistle blows. |  |
| DAY13-22 | tie | 묶다<br>to join things with string or rope | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>문화와 종교 장면에서 '묶다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Please tie the balloons to the back of the chair. | Kindly tie the balloons to the back of the chair. | Use strong hemp rope to tie the wooden raft securely. | Children tie colorful ribbons around the old village tree. |  |
| DAY13-23 | indicate | 나타내다<br>to show or point at something | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>문화와 종교 장면에서 '나타내다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Dark clouds indicate that rain is coming very soon. | Today, dark clouds indicate that rain is coming very soon. | Dark storm clouds usually indicate that heavy rain is coming. | Red flags indicate where the parade will turn left. |  |
| DAY13-24 | humor | 유머<br>the funny quality that makes people laugh | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>문화와 종교 장면에서 '유머'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The clown's gentle humor made the whole room giggle. | Today, the clown's gentle humor made the whole room giggle. | A gentle sense of humor helps defuse tense social situations. | The storyteller's gentle humor made every listener smile at once. |  |
| DAY13-25 | stack | 쌓다<br>to put things on top of each other | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>문화와 종교 장면에서 '쌓다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The baker will stack ten pancakes on one plate. | Today, the baker will stack ten pancakes on one plate. | Workers stack heavy cardboard boxes neatly in the storage warehouse. | Villagers stack round stones beside the mountain path for luck. |  |
| DAY13-26 | odd | 이상한<br>strange and not usual | fallback<br>word_fallback<br>한 장면에서 '이상한'의 확정 뜻이 다른 뜻으로 오해되지 않게 직접 보여 준다 | An odd blue mushroom grew beside the garden path. | An odd blue mushroom grew next to the garden path. | He noticed an odd humming sound coming from the engine. | That odd wooden mask has three eyes and no mouth. |  |
| DAY13-27 | persist | 계속하다<br>to keep trying without giving up | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>문화와 종교 장면에서 '계속하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The little ants persist until the crumb reaches home. | The small ants persist until the crumb reaches home. | If symptoms persist for several days, consult a medical doctor. | Young weavers persist until the difficult pattern finally looks right. |  |
| DAY13-28 | insight | 통찰<br>a clear understanding of something | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>문화와 종교 장면에서 '통찰'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | One sudden insight helped the inventor finish the machine. | Today, one sudden insight helped the inventor finish the machine. | Historical research provides valuable insight into ancient daily cultural practices. | The old diary gave us insight into daily village life. |  |
| DAY13-29 | novel | 소설<br>a long story book about made up people | 국어 / 해석과 상호텍스트성 (Interpretation and intertextuality)<br>direct<br>작품의 구절을 근거로 뜻을 해석하고 다른 이야기와 연결한다 | She read a thrilling fantasy novel about enchanted forest creatures. | Readers interpret the novel's ending using clues from earlier chapters. | She read a famous historical novel set in Joseon dynasty. | This novel repeats a scene we also read in the myth. |  |
| DAY13-30 | noble | 고귀한<br>kind and honest in a great way | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>문화와 종교 장면에서 '고귀한'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The noble knight helped villagers carry heavy water buckets. | Today, the noble knight helped villagers carry heavy water buckets. | The king was praised for his noble character and wisdom. | The story's noble farmer shared the last rice with strangers. |  |

## DAY 13 · set 4 · 문화&예술 / 문화와 종교

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY13-31 | outstanding | 뛰어난<br>much better than all the others | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>문화와 종교 장면에서 '뛰어난'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The outstanding young cellist won first prize this year. | Today, the outstanding young cellist won first prize this year. | Her outstanding academic achievement earned her a prestigious university scholarship. | One outstanding dancer led the whole line at the festival. |  |
| DAY13-32 | resist | 저항하다<br>to fight against or refuse something | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>문화와 종교 장면에서 '저항하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The heavy anchor helps the boat resist strong wind. | Today, the heavy anchor helps the boat resist strong wind. | Strong fortifications helped the castle resist enemy attacks for months. | Thick temple walls resist strong wind during the summer storms. |  |
| DAY13-33 | hesitate | 망설이다<br>to wait because you are unsure | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>문화와 종교 장면에서 '망설이다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The puppy will hesitate before jumping over the small stream. | The young dog will hesitate before jumping over the small stream. | Do not hesitate to ask questions when you need help. | New members hesitate before ringing the heavy bronze bell. |  |
| DAY13-34 | protest | 항의하다<br>to say strongly that something is wrong | 사회 / 지방 자치와 시민 참여 (Local Self-Government and Civic Participation)<br>contextual<br>주민이 지역 문제에 항의하고 담당 기관에 구체적인 개선을 요구한다 | The children protest when playtime ends too early. | Residents protest unsafe roads and request lights from the council. | Citizens gather peacefully to protest against unfair municipal tax increases. | Residents protest the noisy night work and ask for earlier stopping times. |  |
| DAY13-35 | restore | 복원하다<br>to make something old look new again | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>문화와 종교 장면에서 '복원하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Workers will restore the old fountain in the town square. | Today, workers will restore the old fountain in the town square. | Specialists work carefully to restore damaged ancient paintings to museum. | Workers restore the faded roof paint on the old shrine. |  |
| DAY13-36 | associate | 연관 짓다<br>to connect one thing with another in your mind | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>문화와 종교 장면에서 '연관 짓다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Many people associate warm cocoa with cold winter evenings. | Today, many people associate warm cocoa with cold winter evenings. | People often associate white doves with the idea of peace. | Many people associate this drum sound with the harvest festival. |  |
| DAY13-37 | heritage | 유산<br>traditions passed down from long ago | 사회 / 문화경관과 문화 다양성 (Cultural Landscapes and Cultural Diversity)<br>direct<br>지역의 건축·의복·음식·종교가 만든 문화경관의 다양성을 비교한다 | The dance is part of the island's rich heritage. | Island buildings, dances, and foods show its diverse heritage. | Historic palaces are an important part of our cultural heritage. | This wooden gate is village heritage from four hundred years ago. |  |
| DAY13-38 | restriction | 제한<br>a rule that limits what you can do | 사회 / 기본권 제한과 권리 구제 (Limitations and Remedies for Rights)<br>direct<br>권리 제한의 목적과 정도를 따지고 침해 시 도움받을 기관을 찾는다 | One restriction says no running beside the deep pool. | Students question whether the restriction protects swimmers without going too far. | The government placed a temporary restriction on water usage today. | One restriction protects the paintings, so visitors leave large bags outside. |  |
| DAY13-39 | perceive | 인식하다<br>to notice something with your senses | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>문화와 종교 장면에서 '인식하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Bats perceive tiny sounds that human ears cannot hear. | Today, bats perceive tiny sounds that human ears cannot hear. | Humans perceive colors through specialized photoreceptor cells in their eyes. | Inside the dark hall we perceive faint smells of incense. |  |
| DAY13-40 | scale | 규모, 저울, 비늘<br>the size or level of something, or a weighing device | 사회 / 지리적 스케일과 공간적 상호작용 (Geographic Scale and Spatial Interaction)<br>contextual<br>동네·도시·국가처럼 지리적 규모를 바꾸어 같은 현상의 범위를 비교한다 | The scientist measured the mineral weight on a precise digital scale. | The map changes scale to show neighborhoods, cities, and countries. | Geographers examine spatial patterns at both local and global scale. | We compared the festival at village scale and then city scale. |  |

## DAY 14 · set 1 · 문화&예술 / 역사와 전통

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY14-01 | ancient | 고대의<br>very old, from long, long ago | 국어 / 배경과 사회문화 맥락 (Setting and social context)<br>contextual<br>작품의 시간·장소·사회 배경이 인물 행동에 주는 영향을 찾는다 | An ancient stone road ran beside the quiet river. | The ancient setting explains why travelers use horses instead of cars. | Archaeologists uncovered ancient stone tools in the deep mountain cave. | This ancient setting explains why the hero travels by horse. |  |
| DAY14-02 | tradition | 전통<br>something a family does every year | 사회 / 문화경관과 문화 다양성 (Cultural Landscapes and Cultural Diversity)<br>direct<br>지역의 건축·의복·음식·종교가 만든 문화경관의 다양성을 비교한다 | Making rice cakes is a warm family tradition here. | Students compare a food tradition from two different regions. | Making kimchi together in autumn is a long-standing Korean tradition. | One family tradition is wearing hanbok on the New Year. |  |
| DAY14-03 | origin | 기원<br>the place where something first began | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>역사와 전통 장면에서 '기원'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The origin of the river is a mountain spring. | Today, the origin of the river is a mountain spring. | Scholars research the historical origin of the ancient silk trade. | The origin of this pottery style is a southern village. |  |
| DAY14-04 | folk | 민속의<br>about the ordinary people of a place | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>역사와 전통 장면에서 '민속의'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The band played cheerful folk songs at the market. | Today, the band played cheerful folk songs at the market. | The museum displayed colorful folk paintings from the rural countryside. | Grandmothers taught us a folk dance from their farming days. |  |
| DAY14-05 | device | 장치<br>a small machine made for one job | 과학 / 과학과 기술 (Science and Technology)<br>direct<br>문제를 해결할 장치를 설계하고 시제품의 조건과 한계를 시험한다 | A clever device measured rain inside the school garden. | Students test the device twice and record when it fails. | Inventors designed an ingenious mechanical device to measure solar time. | We built a simple device that lifts water from a bucket. |  |
| DAY14-06 | usual | 평소의<br>happening most of the time | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>역사와 전통 장면에서 '평소의'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The cat took its usual nap on the windowsill. | The house cat took its usual nap on the windowsill. | He woke up at his usual early hour before sunrise. | The market opens at its usual hour, even during festivals. |  |
| DAY14-07 | ritual | 의식<br>the same actions done in a special order | 사회 / 문화경관과 문화 다양성 (Cultural Landscapes and Cultural Diversity)<br>direct<br>지역의 건축·의복·음식·종교가 만든 문화경관의 다양성을 비교한다 | The morning ritual began with stretching and warm tea. | Visitors compare a greeting ritual across two cultures. | Villagers performed an ancient ritual to pray for abundant rain. | This ritual repeats three bows before the family shares rice. |  |
| DAY14-08 | entire | 전체의<br>whole, with nothing left out | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>역사와 전통 장면에서 '전체의'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The entire class helped paint the long garden wall. | Today, the entire class helped paint the long garden wall. | The wildfire destroyed the entire forest habitat within three days. | Our entire village joined the rope pulling game last autumn. |  |
| DAY14-09 | region | 지역<br>a large area of land | 사회 / 지리적 위치 (Geographic Location)<br>direct<br>위도와 경도 또는 주변 지형을 이용해 장소의 위치를 설명한다 | Apples grow well in this cool northern region. | The region lies north of the river near high mountains. | This arid region receives very little annual rainfall throughout seasons. | This region sits between two rivers and one low mountain. |  |
| DAY14-10 | religion | 종교<br>a set of beliefs people follow together | 사회 / 문화경관과 문화 다양성 (Cultural Landscapes and Cultural Diversity)<br>direct<br>지역의 건축·의복·음식·종교가 만든 문화경관의 다양성을 비교한다 | The museum showed art from every religion of the island. | Maps show how religion shapes buildings across several regions. | Buddhism is a prominent religion practiced across many Asian nations. | Each religion here built a different roof shape and gate. |  |

| DAY14-11 | record | 기록하다<br>to store information sounds or images | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>역사와 전통 장면에서 '기록하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The camera will record the bird building its nest. | Today, the camera will record the bird building its nest. | Historians record important national events in official chronological written archives. | Students record the old song so the melody stays. |  |

| DAY14-15 | tap | 가볍게 두드리다<br>to hit something lightly and quickly | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>역사와 전통 장면에서 '가볍게 두드리다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The woodpecker will tap gently on the old tree trunk. | Today, the woodpecker will tap gently on the old tree trunk. | Musicians tap the wooden drum surface lightly with their fingers. | Children tap the small drum while the parade passes by. |  |
| DAY14-16 | grasp | 붙잡다<br>to hold something firmly with your hand | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>역사와 전통 장면에서 '붙잡다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The baby monkey will grasp its mother's soft warm fur. | Today, the baby monkey will grasp its mother's soft warm fur. | Climbers grasp the rope firmly with both hands for safety. | The dancer must grasp the long ribbon pole with both hands. |  |
| DAY14-17 | fame | 명성<br>being known by very many people | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>역사와 전통 장면에서 '명성'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The dancing bear enjoyed fame across the whole forest. | Today, the dancing bear enjoyed fame across the whole forest. | The victorious general achieved widespread national fame for his bravery. | This paper craft brought fame to the whole mountain village. |  |
| DAY14-18 | comfort | 편안함<br>a feeling of being warm and relaxed | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>역사와 전통 장면에서 '편안함'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Thick wool blankets gave comfort during the cold snowy night. | Thick wool blankets offered comfort during the cold snowy night. | Warm blankets provided much-needed comfort during the freezing winter night. | Thick straw mats gave comfort during the cold winter nights. |  |
| DAY14-19 | hole | 구멍<br>an empty space or opening in something | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>역사와 전통 장면에서 '구멍'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A small hole in the fence let the rabbits through. | One small hole in the fence let the rabbits through. | The squirrel hid brown acorns inside a small tree hole. | A round hole in the old coin held a silk cord. |  |

| DAY14-25 | weapon | 무기<br>a tool once used for fighting long ago | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>역사와 전통 장면에서 '무기'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | An old wooden weapon rested safely inside the glass case. | An old wooden weapon rested safely within the glass case. | Ancient warriors carried a bronze weapon for self defense battles. | One old wooden weapon rests inside the museum's glass case. |  |
| DAY14-26 | celebrate | 축하하다<br>to do something fun for a special day | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>역사와 전통 장면에서 '축하하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The whole village will celebrate the harvest with music. | Today, the whole village will celebrate the harvest with music. | Townspeople celebrate the harvest festival with traditional folk music performances. | Neighbors celebrate the harvest with rice cakes and folk songs. |  |
| DAY14-27 | combat | 전투<br>a struggle between two sides long ago | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>역사와 전통 장면에서 '전투'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The old story described a combat between two chess kings. | Today, the old story described a combat between two chess kings. | Soldiers engaged in fierce hand-to-hand combat on the battlefield. | History books describe one famous combat between two old kingdoms. |  |
| DAY14-28 | historical | 역사적인<br>about things that happened long ago | 국어 / 배경과 사회문화 맥락 (Setting and social context)<br>contextual<br>작품의 시간·장소·사회 배경이 인물 행동에 주는 영향을 찾는다 | The historical map showed roads from three hundred years ago. | The historical setting explains why the character travels by horse. | The museum preserves historical artifacts from the Three Kingdoms period. | The historical setting shows why the family moved to the port. |  |

## DAY 14 · set 4 · 문화&예술 / 역사와 전통

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY14-31 | harvest | 수확<br>the crops gathered from the fields | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>역사와 전통 장면에서 '수확'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | This year's harvest filled every barn with golden corn. | This year's harvest filled every barn with yellow corn. | Farmers gathered a bountiful autumn harvest of rice and barley. | This year's harvest filled twelve straw baskets with golden rice. |  |
| DAY14-32 | illustrate | 삽화를 그리다<br>to draw pictures for a story | 국어 / 복합양식 매체 제작 (Multimodal media production)<br>contextual<br>이야기의 핵심 장면을 삽화로 그려 글과 그림의 의미가 맞는지 확인한다 | An artist will illustrate the new book about penguins. | Artists illustrate the story's main scene to match its words. | Artists illustrate children's picture books with bright colorful drawings throughout. | Students illustrate the story's main scene so pictures match the text. |  |
| DAY14-33 | dare | 감히 하다<br>to be brave enough to try | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>역사와 전통 장면에서 '감히 하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Few animals dare to cross the wide rushing river. | Today, few animals dare to cross the wide rushing river. | Few brave sailors dare to navigate through stormy arctic waters. | Few climbers dare to cross the narrow rope bridge alone. |  |
| DAY14-34 | voyage | 항해<br>a long trip by ship or space | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>역사와 전통 장면에서 '항해'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The wooden ship began a long voyage across the ocean. | Today, the wooden ship began a long voyage across the ocean. | The explorer embarked on a dangerous ocean voyage across Pacific. | The long voyage to the island took eleven windy days. |  |
| DAY14-35 | conventional | 전통적인<br>usual and done the normal way | 사회 / 문화와 문화 변동 (Culture and Cultural Change)<br>contextual<br>전통적인 방식과 현대의 변화를 비교하고 큰 변화가 사회에 미친 영향을 살핀다 | A conventional kite has a simple diamond shape. | The conventional hand loom works slower than the modern machine. | Conventional farming techniques were replaced by modern automated agricultural machinery. | Conventional wooden looms work slower than the new electric machines. |  |
| DAY14-36 | contemporary | 현대의<br>belonging to the present time | 사회 / 문화와 문화 변동 (Culture and Cultural Change)<br>contextual<br>전통적인 방식과 현대의 변화를 비교하고 큰 변화가 사회에 미친 영향을 살핀다 | Contemporary art filled the bright new gallery downtown. | Contemporary homes use solar panels beside traditional clay roofs. | The gallery exhibits contemporary digital art created by modern artists. | Contemporary dancers mix new steps with the old village rhythm. |  |
| DAY14-37 | revolution | 혁명<br>a very big change in how things work | 사회 / 문화와 문화 변동 (Culture and Cultural Change)<br>contextual<br>전통적인 방식과 현대의 변화를 비교하고 큰 변화가 사회에 미친 영향을 살핀다 | The printing press caused a revolution in sharing stories. | The printing revolution changed how quickly books reached readers. | The industrial revolution changed how goods were manufactured across cities. | The printing revolution let ordinary families own their first books. |  |
| DAY14-38 | cooperation | 협력<br>working together to finish something | 사회 / 지속 가능한 발전과 국제 협력 (Sustainable Development and International Cooperation)<br>contextual<br>공동 문제를 해결하려고 사람이나 집단이 자원과 역할을 나누어 협력한다 | Careful cooperation helped the ants move the heavy leaf. | Town cooperation brings workers and tools together to repair wells. | International cooperation is necessary to protect endangered global wildlife habitats. | Village cooperation rebuilt the shared well after the dry summer. |  |
| DAY14-39 | conquer | 정복하다<br>to win over something difficult | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>역사와 전통 장면에서 '정복하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The climber hopes to conquer her fear of heights. | Today, the climber hopes to conquer her fear of heights. | Ancient empires attempted to conquer distant territories across the sea. | Careful training helped the climbers conquer the steep mountain path. |  |
| DAY14-40 | stable | 안정된<br>steady and not likely to fall | 과학 / 힘의 평형 (Force Equilibrium)<br>contextual<br>서로 반대 방향의 힘이 균형을 이루어 물체가 안정된 상태를 유지한다 | A stable wooden ladder leaned against the apple tree. | Opposite ropes pull equally, keeping the platform stable. | Balanced opposing forces keep the suspension bridge in stable equilibrium. | The stone tower stays stable because opposite pushes balance each other. |  |

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY15-01 | university | 대학교<br>a school where people study after high school | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>교육과 학문 장면에서 '대학교'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The old university had ivy climbing across its walls. | Today, the old university had ivy climbing across its walls. | She plans to study biological sciences at a prestigious university. | My cousin studies plant science at a nearby university. |  |
| DAY15-02 | education | 교육<br>the learning you get at school | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>교육과 학문 장면에서 '교육'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A good education helps children understand the wide world. | One good education helps children understand the wide world. | Quality public education empowers young citizens to build better futures. | Free education helps every child in this town read well. |  |
| DAY15-03 | focus | 집중하다<br>to give all your attention to something | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>교육과 학문 장면에서 '집중하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The young chess player must focus on the next move. | Today, the young chess player must focus on the next move. | Students must focus on their homework assignments without phone distractions. | Please focus on the map while I read the directions. |  |
| DAY15-04 | entry | 입장<br>the way you go into a place | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>교육과 학문 장면에서 '입장'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The garden entry was covered with climbing pink roses. | Today, the garden entry was covered with climbing pink roses. | Visitors must present an official entry pass at the gate. | Visitors line up at the museum entry before nine o'clock. |  |
| DAY15-05 | medical | 의학의<br>about doctors and keeping people healthy | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>교육과 학문 장면에서 '의학의'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The medical team checked every puppy at the animal shelter. | Today, the medical team checked every puppy at the animal shelter. | Doctors provide essential medical care to patients in community clinics. | The medical students practiced washing hands for one full minute. |  |
| DAY15-06 | accept | 받아들이다<br>to take something that is offered | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>교육과 학문 장면에서 '받아들이다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Please accept this small gift from the whole class. | Kindly accept this small gift from the whole class. | Universities accept qualified student applicants based on academic merit standards. | Our teacher will accept the report until Friday afternoon. |  |
| DAY15-07 | pioneer | 개척자<br>someone who does something first | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>교육과 학문 장면에서 '개척자'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The pioneer built the first bridge across the wide canyon. | Today, the pioneer built the first bridge across the wide canyon. | Marie Curie was a courageous scientific pioneer in radioactivity research. | This scientist was a pioneer of Korean weather research. |  |
| DAY15-08 | mathematics | 수학<br>the study of numbers and shapes | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>교육과 학문 장면에서 '수학'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Mathematics helps builders make strong and beautiful stone arches. | Today, mathematics helps builders make strong and beautiful stone arches. | Geometry and algebra are fundamental branches of middle school mathematics. | In mathematics we study numbers, angles, and simple shapes. |  |
| DAY15-09 | professor | 교수<br>a teacher at a university | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>교육과 학문 장면에서 '교수'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The kind professor showed students a very old telescope. | The kind professor displayed students a very old telescope. | The physics professor explained quantum mechanics principles to the class. | A friendly professor showed us the university's old book room. |  |
| DAY15-10 | profession | 직업<br>a job that needs special training | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>교육과 학문 장면에서 '직업'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Nursing is a caring profession that helps many people. | Today, nursing is a caring profession that helps many people. | Nursing is a demanding profession that requires dedication and compassion. | Nursing is a profession that needs three years of training. |  |

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY15-11 | theory | 이론<br>an idea that explains why something happens | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>교육과 학문 장면에서 '이론'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | One theory says the moon was once part of earth. | Today, one theory says the moon was once part of earth. | The scientific theory of gravity explains how planetary orbits function. | This theory explains why warm air rises above cold air. |  |
| DAY15-12 | philosophy | 철학<br>deep thinking about life and what is true | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>교육과 학문 장면에서 '철학'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The old thinker taught philosophy under a shady olive tree. | Today, the old thinker taught philosophy under a shady olive tree. | Ancient Greek philosophy explored fundamental questions about ethics and truth. | In philosophy class we asked what a good life means. |  |
| DAY15-13 | method | 방법<br>a way of doing something | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>교육과 학문 장면에서 '방법'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | This simple method makes paper boats float much longer. | Today, this simple method makes paper boats float much longer. | The scientific method involves formulating testable hypotheses and gathering data. | Our science method starts with one question and one careful test. |  |
| DAY15-14 | praise | 칭찬하다<br>to say kind words about someone's work | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>교육과 학문 장면에서 '칭찬하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The teacher will praise every student who tried hard today. | An instructor will praise every student who tried hard today. | Teachers praise students who demonstrate honest effort and creative thinking. | Teachers praise students who explain their answers step by step. |  |
| DAY15-15 | standard | 기준<br>a level that things should reach | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>교육과 학문 장면에서 '기준'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The bakery keeps a high standard for every fruit tart. | Today, the bakery keeps a high standard for every fruit tart. | The laboratory maintains a strict standard for clean water testing. | Every poster must reach the same standard of neat handwriting. |  |
| DAY15-16 | character | 성격<br>the qualities that make a person behave a certain way | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>교육과 학문 장면에서 '성격'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Helping smaller animals shows the bear's kind character. | Today, helping smaller animals shows the bear's kind character. | Honesty and perseverance are admirable traits of good moral character. | A patient character helps this young tutor teach beginners. |  |
| DAY15-17 | throw | 던지다<br>to send something through the air | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>교육과 학문 장면에서 '던지다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Please throw the soft ball to the waiting puppy. | Kindly throw the soft ball to the waiting puppy. | Athletes throw the heavy javelin across the open grassy field. | Do not throw the chalk across the science classroom. |  |
| DAY15-18 | psychology | 심리학<br>the study of how minds work | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>교육과 학문 장면에서 '심리학'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Psychology helps us understand why people feel certain ways. | Today, psychology helps us understand why people feel certain ways. | Developmental psychology investigates how children acquire language and social skills. | In psychology we learn how memory holds new information. |  |
| DAY15-19 | intention | 의도<br>what you plan to do | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>교육과 학문 장면에서 '의도'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Her intention was to finish the painting before sunset. | Before sunset, her intention was to finish the painting. | He stated his sincere intention to study environmental engineering abroad. | My intention was helping, but I made the chart messier. |  |
| DAY15-20 | apply | 지원하다<br>to formally ask for a job or place | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>교육과 학문 장면에서 '지원하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The student will apply for a place in the art club. | One learner will apply for a place in the art club. | High school graduates apply for university admission before the deadline. | Older students apply for the summer science camp each May. |  |
