| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY15-21 | tune | 곡조<br>a short piece of music you hum | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>교육과 학문 장면에서 '곡조'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The bird sang a cheerful tune outside the open window. | Today, the bird sang a cheerful tune outside the open window. | The flutist played a cheerful folk tune on the stage. | Everyone hummed the same tune while walking to the bus. |  |
| DAY15-22 | submit | 제출하다<br>to hand in your work to someone | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>교육과 학문 장면에서 '제출하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Students submit their drawings before the class ends today. | Before the class ends today, students submit their drawings. | Students submit their completed science lab reports by Friday afternoon. | We submit our reading logs to the librarian every Monday. |  |
| DAY15-23 | logical | 논리적인<br>sensible and easy to follow | 국어 / 설명과 논증 (Explanation and argument)<br>direct<br>원인과 결과 또는 주장과 근거의 연결을 분명히 설명한다 | The robot gave a logical answer to the tricky riddle. | Her logical claim connects each reason to clear evidence. | She constructed a clear logical argument supported by verified facts. | A logical answer links the dry soil to the wilted leaves. |  |
| DAY15-24 | blame | 탓하다<br>to say someone caused a problem | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>교육과 학문 장면에서 '탓하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Do not blame the wind for the tangled kite string. | Today, do not blame the wind for the tangled kite string. | Do not blame others for your own avoidable homework mistakes. | Do not blame the weather when we forgot our umbrellas. |  |
| DAY15-25 | display | 전시하다<br>to put something where people can see it | 국어 / 복합양식 읽기 (Multimodal reading)<br>contextual<br>글과 사진·그림이 주는 정보를 함께 읽고 서로 보완하는지 확인한다 | The class will display their clay animals on the shelf. | The display combines captions and pictures to explain the animal. | Museums display informative historical maps alongside detailed explanatory wall texts. | We display the graph beside the text so both explain together. |  |
| DAY15-26 | likewise | 마찬가지로<br>in the same way | fallback<br>word_fallback<br>한 장면에서 '마찬가지로'의 확정 뜻이 다른 뜻으로 오해되지 않게 직접 보여 준다 | The tall bear bowed; likewise, the small fox bowed politely. | Today, the tall bear bowed; likewise, the small fox bowed politely. | Plants require sunlight to grow; likewise, animals need nutritious food. | Ants carry crumbs home; likewise, bees carry pollen to the hive. |  |
| DAY15-27 | discipline | 규율<br>the habit of following rules carefully | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>교육과 학문 장면에서 '규율'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Daily discipline helped the young swimmer improve every single week. | Today, daily discipline helped the young swimmer improve every single week. | Consistent daily discipline helps students achieve high academic learning goals. | Daily discipline keeps this violin group practicing at seven sharp. |  |
| DAY15-28 | boost | 높이다<br>to make something bigger or better | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>교육과 학문 장면에서 '높이다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Sunlight can boost the growth of these young tomato plants. | Today, sunlight can boost the growth of these young tomato plants. | Eating healthy meals will boost your immune system and stamina. | Extra reading time can boost our vocabulary before the contest. |  |
| DAY15-29 | shallow | 얕은<br>not deep at all | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>교육과 학문 장면에서 '얕은'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Ducklings paddled happily in the shallow end of the pond. | Ducklings paddled cheerfully in the shallow end of the pond. | Young children can wade safely in the shallow swimming pool. | The stream near school is shallow enough for rubber boots. |  |
| DAY15-30 | swallow | 삼키다<br>to make food go down your throat | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>교육과 학문 장면에서 '삼키다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Chew slowly and swallow before you take another bite. | Chew gently and swallow before you take another bite. | Be sure to chew your food well before you swallow. | Chew the rice well before you swallow each small bite. |  |

## DAY 15 · set 4 · 문화&예술 / 교육과 학문

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY15-31 | bias | 편향<br>liking one side more than the other | 국어 / 매체 문해력 (Media literacy)<br>direct<br>뉴스의 출처·표현·빠진 정보를 확인해 메시지를 비판적으로 읽는다 | A fair judge must leave every bias outside the room. | Readers detect bias by checking sources and missing information. | Critical news readers check multiple sources to identify media bias. | This news report shows bias because it quotes only one side. |  |
| DAY15-32 | string | 끈<br>a thin cord used to tie things | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>교육과 학문 장면에서 '끈'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The kite string stretched high above the grassy hill. | Today, the kite string stretched high above the grassy hill. | Tie a piece of sturdy string around the paper package. | We tied the plant to a stick with brown string. |  |
| DAY15-33 | examination | 시험<br>a test that shows what you learned | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>교육과 학문 장면에서 '시험'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The examination covered every plant in the school garden. | Today, the examination covered every plant in the school garden. | Students prepared diligently for their final statewide mathematics examination today. | The science examination asked us to name three simple machines. |  |
| DAY15-34 | rational | 이성적인<br>using clear thinking, not just feelings | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>교육과 학문 장면에서 '이성적인'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A rational plan helped the team finish the model bridge. | One rational plan helped the team finish the model bridge. | Judges make rational decisions based strictly on evidence and law. | A rational choice counts the actual costs before we buy anything. |  |
| DAY15-35 | laboratory | 실험실<br>a room where scientists do experiments | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>교육과 학문 장면에서 '실험실'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The laboratory had bright beakers along every clean white shelf. | Today, the laboratory had bright beakers along every clean white shelf. | Scientists conduct genetic experiments inside a sterile research laboratory environment. | Inside the laboratory we wear goggles and tie back long hair. |  |
| DAY15-36 | authority | 권한<br>the power to make rules or decisions | 사회 / 국민 주권과 입헌주의 (Popular Sovereignty and Constitutionalism)<br>contextual<br>국가 권력이 국민의 동의와 헌법의 제한 안에서 행사됨을 확인한다 | The park keeper has authority over the playground rules. | The constitution limits government authority granted by the people. | Elected officials exercise legal authority granted by the nation's constitution. | The mayor's authority comes from voters and stays inside the constitution. |  |
| DAY15-37 | misunderstanding | 오해<br>when people think something wrong about each other | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>교육과 학문 장면에서 '오해'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A small misunderstanding ended with laughter and warm handshakes. | One small misunderstanding ended with laughter and warm handshakes. | Clear honest communication clears up any lingering misunderstanding between friends. | One misunderstanding about the meeting time left two students waiting. |  |
| DAY15-38 | pursue | 추구하다<br>to keep trying to reach a goal | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>교육과 학문 장면에서 '추구하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The young violinist will pursue her dream of playing well. | Today, the young violinist will pursue her dream of playing well. | Graduates pursue rewarding careers in technology, medicine, and public education. | Young researchers pursue answers through many careful repeated tests. |  |
| DAY15-39 | deprived | 빼앗긴<br>not having something you really need | 사회 / 인권과 헌법상 기본권 (Human Rights and Constitutional Rights)<br>contextual<br>필요한 권리나 접근이 부족한 사람이 동등하게 교육과 서비스를 이용할 방법을 찾는다 | The deprived seedlings grew slowly without enough warm sunlight. | Students deprived of books receive equal library access. | Social welfare programs support children from economically deprived background environments. | Children deprived of school books received free copies from the city. |  |
| DAY15-40 | spell | 철자를 말하다<br>to say the letters of a word | 국어 / 음운과 문자 체계 (Phonology and writing systems)<br>direct<br>말소리의 크기와 발음을 비교하고 글자와 소리의 대응을 확인한다 | Can you spell the long name of that flower? | Students hear each sound before they spell the word. | Can you spell the difficult scientific vocabulary word correctly aloud? | We spell 'listen' aloud and notice the silent letter t. |  |

## DAY 16 · set 1 · 과학&기술 / 물리학과 화학

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY16-01 | nature | 자연<br>everything outdoors like plants and animals | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>물리학과 화학 장면에서 '자연'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The hikers enjoyed nature along the quiet forest trail. | Today, the hikers enjoyed nature along the quiet forest trail. | We observe the beauty of untouched nature in the national park. | Nature outside the classroom gives us leaves, stones, and insects to study. |  |
| DAY16-02 | experiment | 실험<br>a test to learn how something works | 과학 / 과학적 탐구 (Scientific Inquiry)<br>direct<br>관찰 질문을 세우고 한 조건만 바꾼 실험의 자료와 결론을 기록한다 | The simple experiment showed how plants drink colored water. | In the experiment, students change light and record plant growth. | Students conduct a plant growth experiment with controlled sunlight and water. | In our experiment we changed only the water amount each day. |  |
| DAY16-03 | condition | 상태<br>how good or bad something is | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>물리학과 화학 장면에서 '상태'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The old bicycle was in very good condition still. | Today, the old bicycle was in very good condition still. | The old microscope is still in excellent working condition for research. | The borrowed microscope stayed in good condition after ten uses. |  |
| DAY16-04 | distance | 거리<br>how far one place is from another | 사회 / 지리적 스케일과 공간적 상호작용 (Geographic Scale and Spatial Interaction)<br>direct<br>거리와 교통망에 따라 사람·물자의 이동 범위가 달라짐을 지도에서 본다 | The distance between the two islands looked quite short. | The map shows how distance changes the range of deliveries. | A detailed map shows the exact distance between two mountain cities. | A shorter distance to the highway brings more trucks to town. |  |
| DAY16-05 | evidence | 증거<br>facts that show something is true | 과학 / 과학적 탐구 (Scientific Inquiry)<br>direct<br>관찰 질문을 세우고 한 조건만 바꾼 실험의 자료와 결론을 기록한다 | Muddy paw prints were evidence that the fox visited. | Students change only sunlight and record plant growth as evidence. | The recorded experiment data gives strong evidence to support our conclusion. | Our notebook holds evidence that salt water froze more slowly. |  |
| DAY16-06 | chemical | 화학 물질<br>a substance used in science experiments | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>물리학과 화학 장면에서 '화학 물질'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | People loved the vivid green color before learning the chemical was arsenic. | The teacher stores each chemical safely after the classroom experiment. | The laboratory stores each hazardous chemical inside a sealed metal cabinet. | The teacher poured one safe chemical into the clear test tube. |  |
| DAY16-07 | fluid | 액체<br>something that flows, like water or juice | 과학 / 대류와 복사 (Convection and Radiation)<br>contextual<br>데워진 유체의 이동과 햇빛 복사로 열이 전달되는 모습을 비교한다 | The scientist poured a thick fluid into the glass jar. | Warm fluid rises while cooler fluid sinks in the tank. | Warm heated fluid rises to the top of the glass beaker. | Warm fluid rises in the pot while cooler fluid sinks down. |  |
| DAY16-08 | extensive | 광범위한<br>covering a very large area | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>물리학과 화학 장면에서 '광범위한'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | An extensive forest stretched beyond the tall blue mountains. | Today, an extensive forest stretched beyond the tall blue mountains. | Scientists conducted extensive laboratory research to analyze the new mineral compound. | An extensive layer of ice covered the whole school playground. |  |
| DAY16-09 | process | 과정<br>the steps that make something happen | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>물리학과 화학 장면에서 '과정'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The baking process takes about one warm quiet hour. | Today, the baking process takes about one warm quiet hour. | Photosynthesis is an essential biological process that produces sugar and oxygen. | Boiling water follows a clear process from bubbles to steam. |  |
| DAY16-10 | progress | 진전<br>movement forward toward a goal | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>물리학과 화학 장면에서 '진전'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The young reader made great progress during the summer. | Today, the young reader made great progress during the summer. | The research team made significant progress in developing clean solar cells. | Our plant chart shows steady progress toward twenty centimeters. |  |

## DAY 16 · set 2 · 과학&기술 / 물리학과 화학

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY16-11 | planet | 행성<br>a huge round body moving around a star | 과학 / 태양계 천체 (Solar System Objects)<br>direct<br>태양·행성·위성·소행성을 특징과 궤도로 분류한다 | The red planet looked bright through the small telescope. | Students classify each planet by size, surface, and orbit. | Mars is a rocky planet orbiting between Earth and giant Jupiter. | Mars is a rocky planet that circles the sun. |  |
| DAY16-12 | conduct | 수행하다<br>to carry out or do something | 과학 / 과학적 탐구 (Scientific Inquiry)<br>contextual<br>정해진 절차와 통제 조건에 따라 실험을 수행하고 결과를 기록한다 | Students conduct a simple test with magnets and pins. | Students conduct the magnet test and record results in order. | Students conduct a fair science test by changing only one variable. | We conduct the test three times and record every result. |  |
| DAY16-13 | solid | 고체의<br>hard and keeping its own shape | 과학 / 물질의 상태 (States of Matter)<br>direct<br>고체·액체·기체의 모양과 부피 및 입자 배열을 비교한다 | The solid ice cube slowly floated in warm juice. | A solid keeps its shape while a liquid fills containers. | Ice is the solid form of water at low cold temperatures. | Ice is solid, so it keeps its shape inside the cup. |  |
| DAY16-14 | eternal | 영원한<br>lasting forever without ending | fallback<br>word_fallback<br>한 장면에서 '영원한'의 확정 뜻이 다른 뜻으로 오해되지 않게 직접 보여 준다 | The story spoke of an eternal light inside the cave. | The story spoke of an eternal light within the cave. | The stone pyramid seemed to stand in eternal stillness under stars. | Nothing on this list is eternal; even mountains change slowly. |  |
| DAY16-15 | gravity | 중력<br>the pull that brings things down to earth | 과학 / 중력, 질량, 무게 (Gravity, Mass, and Weight)<br>direct<br>질량과 무게를 구별하고 중력이 물체를 아래로 당기는 힘을 잰다 | Gravity makes the apple fall from the tall branch. | Students measure how gravity changes an object's weight, not mass. | Earth's gravity pulls the dropped apple downward toward the grassy lawn. | The scale shows more weight because gravity pulls harder on heavier stones. |  |
| DAY16-16 | melt | 녹다<br>to change from solid into liquid | 과학 / 융해와 응고 (Melting and Freezing)<br>direct<br>고체가 녹아 액체가 되고 액체가 얼어 고체가 되는 온도를 잰다 | The snowman began to melt under the bright spring sun. | Students record the temperature when ice begins to melt. | Solid ice cubes melt quickly when placed in warm drinking water. | Ice cubes melt at zero degrees, turning into clear water. |  |
| DAY16-17 | astronaut | 우주 비행사<br>a person who travels into space | 과학 / 태양계 천체 (Solar System Objects)<br>direct<br>태양·행성·위성·소행성을 특징과 궤도로 분류한다 | An astronaut floated slowly inside the quiet space station. | An astronaut identifies a planet, moon, and asteroid by orbit. | The brave astronaut floated weightlessly outside the orbiting space station lab. | An astronaut floated near the moon and photographed its gray craters. |  |
| DAY16-18 | smooth | 매끄러운<br>flat and even, with no bumps | 과학 / 마찰력 (Friction)<br>contextual<br>표면의 거칠기와 마찰이 미끄러지는 움직임에 주는 영향을 비교한다 | The river stone felt smooth inside her small hand. | The block slides farther on smooth wood with less friction. | Wooden blocks slide easily across the smooth surface of the ramp. | A smooth glass surface lets the coin slide much farther. |  |
| DAY16-19 | oxygen | 산소<br>the gas in air that we breathe | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>물리학과 화학 장면에서 '산소'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Green plants give oxygen to everyone on the earth. | Today, green plants give oxygen to everyone on the earth. | Green plants release fresh oxygen into the air during sunny daylight. | Green plants release oxygen while sunlight reaches their leaves. |  |
| DAY16-20 | support | 받치다<br>to hold something up so it stays | 과학 / 힘의 평형 (Force Equilibrium)<br>direct<br>서로 반대 방향의 힘이 같을 때 물체가 안정된 상태를 유지함을 본다 | Strong poles support the roof of the garden shelter. | Two equal forces support the balanced hanging sign. | Four sturdy wooden legs support the heavy science laboratory work table. | Two wooden blocks support the shelf, so it does not tip. |  |

## DAY 16 · set 3 · 과학&기술 / 물리학과 화학

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY16-21 | acid | 산<br>a sour liquid used in science | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>물리학과 화학 장면에서 '산'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Lemon juice is a mild acid found in kitchens. | Today, lemon juice is a mild acid found in kitchens. | Lemon juice contains citric acid that tastes distinctly sharp and sour. | Lemon juice is a mild acid that tastes very sour. |  |
| DAY16-22 | orbit | 궤도<br>the path a planet takes around a star | 과학 / 태양계 천체 (Solar System Objects)<br>direct<br>태양·행성·위성·소행성을 특징과 궤도로 분류한다 | The tiny moon follows a wide orbit around the planet. | The small moon follows a wide orbit around the planet. | The Moon follows an elliptical orbit around Earth every single month. | The moon follows a curved orbit around our planet Earth. |  |
| DAY16-23 | toxic | 독성의<br>not safe to eat or drink | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>물리학과 화학 장면에서 '독성의'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Some bright berries are toxic, so nobody should taste them. | Today, some bright berries are toxic, so nobody should taste them. | Wear protective gloves when handling any toxic laboratory cleaning solvent liquids. | Some wild berries are toxic, so we never taste them. |  |
| DAY16-24 | atom | 원자<br>the tiniest piece of any material | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>물리학과 화학 장면에서 '원자'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Every single atom is far too small to see. | Today, every single atom is far too small to see. | Every single chemical element consists of a unique type of atom. | A single atom is far too small for any classroom microscope. |  |
| DAY16-25 | liquid | 액체<br>something wet that pours, like water | 과학 / 물질의 상태 (States of Matter)<br>direct<br>고체·액체·기체의 모양과 부피 및 입자 배열을 비교한다 | The clear liquid filled the tall measuring cup slowly. | A liquid keeps its volume but changes shape in each container. | Water is a clear liquid that takes the container's physical shape. | Poured liquid takes the shape of every container we use. |  |
| DAY16-26 | universe | 우주<br>everything in space, including all the stars | 과학 / 태양계 천체 (Solar System Objects)<br>direct<br>태양·행성·위성·소행성을 특징과 궤도로 분류한다 | The universe holds more stars than anyone can count. | Our universe contains stars, planets, moons, and asteroids. | Billions of shining galaxies exist throughout the vast observable physical universe. | Our solar system is one small part of the whole universe. |  |
| DAY16-27 | reverse | 뒤집다<br>to turn something the opposite way | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>물리학과 화학 장면에서 '뒤집다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | You can reverse the pattern to make a new design. | Today, you can reverse the pattern to make a new design. | Press the switch to reverse the direction of electric motor rotation. | Please reverse the magnet and watch the paper clips move. |  |
| DAY16-28 | extend | 늘리다<br>to make something longer or bigger | 과학 / 열팽창 (Thermal Expansion)<br>contextual<br>물체를 가열했을 때 길이나 부피가 늘어나는지 전후 값을 비교한다 | Workers will extend the wooden dock into the calm lake. | The metal rod will extend slightly when heated. | Heat causes the long copper pipe to extend in measurable length. | Heated metal bars extend slightly, so we measure them again. |  |
| DAY16-29 | extent | 정도<br>how big or how far something is | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>물리학과 화학 장면에서 '정도'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Nobody knew the full extent of the underground cave. | Today, nobody knew the full extent of the underground cave. | Scientists measured the vast extent of forest damage caused by wildfire. | We measured the extent of the wet spot with a ruler. |  |
| DAY16-30 | expand | 확장하다<br>to grow bigger in size | 과학 / 열팽창 (Thermal Expansion)<br>contextual<br>물체를 가열했을 때 길이나 부피가 늘어나는지 전후 값을 비교한다 | Warm air makes the balloon expand into a round shape. | Today, warm air makes the balloon expand into a round shape. | Gases expand when heated inside a sealed glass laboratory flask container. | Warm air makes the balloon expand until it looks round. |  |

## DAY 16 · set 4 · 과학&기술 / 물리학과 화학

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY16-31 | preserve | 보존하다<br>to keep something safe from changing | 과학 / 지속 가능한 발전 (Sustainable Development)<br>contextual<br>자원을 보존하고 불필요한 낭비를 줄이는 방법의 환경 영향을 비교한다 | Cold air helps preserve the fresh berries much longer. | Families preserve water by fixing leaks and reducing waste. | We must preserve clean freshwater resources for future human generation needs. | Careful farmers preserve the forest soil by planting cover grass. |  |
| DAY16-32 | vapor | 증기<br>tiny drops of water floating in air | 과학 / 기화와 액화 (Vaporization and Condensation)<br>direct<br>액체가 증발하고 차가운 표면에서 다시 물방울로 맺히는 것을 관찰한다 | Warm vapor rose slowly from the steaming teapot spout. | Water becomes vapor, then forms drops on a cold lid. | Boiling water turns into invisible water vapor in hot kitchen air. | Water vapor from the kettle turns to drops on cold glass. |  |
| DAY16-33 | invisible | 보이지 않는<br>not able to be seen | 과학 / 기체 입자 모형 (Particle Model of Gases)<br>contextual<br>보이지 않는 기체를 입자 모형으로 나타내 움직임과 충돌을 설명한다 | The wind is invisible, but leaves show where it goes. | An invisible gas still has particles that strike container walls. | Air consists of invisible gas particles moving rapidly in all directions. | Air is invisible, so we draw its moving particles as dots. |  |
| DAY16-34 | substance | 물질<br>any material that things are made of | 과학 / 비열 (Specific Heat)<br>contextual<br>같은 질량의 서로 다른 물질에 같은 열을 주고 온도 변화를 비교한다 | A sticky substance covered the inside of the honey jar. | Each substance warms differently after receiving the same heat. | Water has higher specific heat than any other common liquid substance. | Each substance warmed differently, though we added the same heat. |  |
| DAY16-35 | particle | 입자<br>a very tiny piece of something | 과학 / 온도와 입자 운동 (Temperature and Particle Motion)<br>direct<br>온도가 높아질수록 입자 운동이 빨라지는 모형을 관찰한다 | Each dust particle danced inside the bright sunbeam. | Each particle moves faster when the model's temperature rises. | Each gas particle moves faster as temperature increases inside the container. | Every particle moves faster when the water temperature rises. |  |
| DAY16-36 | moisture | 습기<br>small amounts of water in air or soil | 과학 / 기화와 액화 (Vaporization and Condensation)<br>direct<br>액체가 증발하고 차가운 표면에서 다시 물방울로 맺히는 것을 관찰한다 | Morning moisture made the green grass sparkle everywhere. | Air moisture forms drops on the cold glass. | Morning moisture condenses into tiny water droplets on cold grass leaves. | Morning moisture collected on the cold window and formed small drops. |  |
| DAY16-37 | abstract | 추상적인<br>about ideas, not things you can touch | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>물리학과 화학 장면에서 '추상적인'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The abstract painting used only shapes and bright colors. | Today, the abstract painting used only shapes and bright colors. | Mathematics often deals with abstract concepts like infinity and imaginary numbers. | Fairness is an abstract idea; this metal ball is not. |  |
| DAY16-38 | valid | 유효한<br>correct and able to be accepted | 국어 / 자료 신뢰성 (Source credibility)<br>contextual<br>자료의 작성자·날짜·근거를 확인하고 다른 출처와 대조한다 | That was a valid answer to the tricky question. | Students call the claim valid after checking two reliable sources. | The experiment conclusion is valid only when supported by verified data. | This measurement stays valid because two students checked the same scale. |  |
| DAY16-39 | invention | 발명품<br>a new thing that someone made first | 과학 / 과학과 기술 (Science and Technology)<br>direct<br>문제를 해결할 장치를 설계하고 시제품의 조건과 한계를 시험한다 | The clever invention watered every plant while people slept. | Students test the invention and record where it fails. | The microscope was a revolutionary scientific invention for observing tiny cells. | Our invention lifts books with two pulleys and one string. |  |
| DAY16-40 | stick | 붙다<br>to join to something and stay there | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>물리학과 화학 장면에서 '붙다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Wet leaves stick to the window after a heavy rain. | After a heavy rain, wet leaves stick to the window. | Electrostatic charges cause small pieces of dry paper to stick together. | Rubbed balloons stick to the wall for a short time. |  |

## DAY 17 · set 1 · 과학&기술 / 생물학과 유전학

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY17-01 | biology | 생물학<br>the study of living plants and animals | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>생물학과 유전학 장면에서 '생물학'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Biology class explored the pond life this sunny morning. | Today, biology class explored the pond life this sunny morning. | Middle school biology introduces students to cell structure and plant classification. | In biology we compare the leaves of two different trees. |  |
| DAY17-02 | gene | 유전자<br>a tiny part that decides how living things grow | 과학 / 변이 (Variation)<br>direct<br>같은 종 개체의 서로 다른 형질이 유전과 환경에서 생김을 비교한다 | One gene decides whether the flower petals turn purple. | A gene can influence one trait, alongside the environment. | A specific gene determines whether pea plant flowers are purple white. | One gene decides whether this pea plant grows tall or short. |  |
| DAY17-03 | factor | 요인<br>one thing that helps cause a result | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>생물학과 유전학 장면에서 '요인'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Sunlight is an important factor in how plants grow. | Today, sunlight is an important factor in how plants grow. | Sunlight is an important environmental factor in healthy plant growth. | Sunlight is one factor that changes how fast seedlings grow. |  |
| DAY17-04 | trace | 추적하다<br>to follow marks and discover where something went | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>생물학과 유전학 장면에서 '추적하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The ranger will trace animal footprints through the soft mud. | Today, the ranger will trace animal footprints through the soft mud. | Scientists trace the evolutionary lineage of modern birds from ancient dinosaurs. | Scientists trace bird paths by following small marks in mud. |  |
| DAY17-05 | require | 필요로 하다<br>to need something very much | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>생물학과 유전학 장면에서 '필요로 하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Baby birds require warm food many times each day. | Today, baby birds require warm food many times each day. | Green plants require sunlight, water, and minerals to sustain healthy growth. | Frog eggs require clean water and warm spring weather. |  |
| DAY17-06 | structure | 구조<br>the way parts are put together | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>생물학과 유전학 장면에서 '구조'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The honeycomb structure has many neat six sided rooms. | Today, the honeycomb structure has many neat six sided rooms. | The cellular structure of plant tissue is visible under optical microscope. | The honeycomb structure holds many six-sided rooms side by side. |  |
| DAY17-07 | research | 연구<br>careful study to find new facts | 과학 / 과학적 탐구 (Scientific Inquiry)<br>direct<br>관찰 질문을 세우고 한 조건만 바꾼 실험의 자료와 결론을 기록한다 | Their research showed how sea turtles find warm beaches. | Their research changes one condition and records turtle behavior. | Her botanical research revealed how roots absorb water from sandy soil. | This research recorded leaf color every week for two months. |  |
| DAY17-08 | intelligent | 똑똑한<br>able to learn and understand quickly | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>생물학과 유전학 장면에서 '똑똑한'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Crows are intelligent birds that solve puzzles for food. | Today, crows are intelligent birds that solve puzzles for food. | Dolphins are intelligent marine mammals that communicate with complex sound clicks. | Crows are intelligent birds that use sticks to reach food. |  |
| DAY17-09 | sign | 표지판<br>a board that shows people something | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>생물학과 유전학 장면에서 '표지판'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A wooden sign pointed toward the quiet forest pond. | One wooden sign pointed toward the quiet forest pond. | A warning sign near the wetland protects the fragile bird habitat. | A wooden sign points toward the frog pond behind the school. |  |
| DAY17-10 | sigh | 한숨 쉬다<br>to breathe out slowly showing a feeling | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>생물학과 유전학 장면에서 '한숨 쉬다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The tired dog will sigh before falling fast asleep. | The weary dog will sigh before falling fast asleep. | The tired botanist let out a quiet sigh after fieldwork. | Tired hikers sigh and sit on the flat gray rock. |  |

## DAY 17 · set 2 · 과학&기술 / 생물학과 유전학

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY17-11 | insect | 곤충<br>a small animal with six legs | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>생물학과 유전학 장면에서 '곤충'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A shiny green insect rested on the tall grass blade. | One shiny green insect rested on the tall grass blade. | A honeybee is a helpful insect that pollinates bright orchard flowers. | This insect has six legs and two thin clear wings. |  |
| DAY17-12 | circumstance | 상황<br>a fact or event around a situation | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>생물학과 유전학 장면에서 '상황'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | One lucky circumstance let the picnic happen after all. | After all, one lucky circumstance let the picnic happen. | Desert animals adapt to the harsh circumstance of scarce freshwater supplies. | Heavy rain was the one circumstance that delayed our field study. |  |
| DAY17-13 | normal | 정상적인<br>usual and what you expect | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>생물학과 유전학 장면에서 '정상적인'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | It was a normal quiet evening in the small village. | Today, it was a normal quiet evening in the small village. | A healthy human body maintains a normal temperature around thirty-seven degrees. | Green leaves and firm stems show normal growth in these plants. |  |
| DAY17-14 | threat | 위협<br>something that could cause a problem | 과학 / 생물다양성 보전 (Biodiversity Conservation)<br>contextual<br>서식지 파괴가 특정 생물종에 주는 위협과 보호 방법을 연결한다 | Strong wind was a threat to the young apple trees. | Habitat loss is a threat to frogs living near wetlands. | Habitat loss poses a serious environmental threat to endangered forest wildlife. | Losing wetlands is the biggest threat to these migrating birds. |  |
| DAY17-15 | improve | 개선하다<br>to make something better than before | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>생물학과 유전학 장면에서 '개선하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Daily practice will improve the young pianist's gentle touch. | Today, daily practice will improve the young pianist's gentle touch. | Selective breeding can improve crop yield and resistance to common pests. | Adding compost will improve this thin soil before spring planting. |  |
| DAY17-16 | repeat | 반복하다<br>to do or say something again | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>생물학과 유전학 장면에서 '반복하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The parrot loves to repeat the same cheerful whistle. | Today, the parrot loves to repeat the same cheerful whistle. | Researchers repeat the genetic experiment three times to ensure consistent data. | We repeat the same measurement to check our first result. |  |
| DAY17-17 | resemble | 닮다<br>to look like someone or something else | 과학 / 변이 (Variation)<br>contextual<br>같은 종의 개체가 닮은 형질과 서로 다른 변이를 함께 비교한다 | The puppies resemble their mother with matching brown spots. | The young dogs resemble their mother with matching brown spots. | Young offspring closely resemble their biological parents in physical facial features. | Kittens resemble their mother but each one has different fur marks. |  |
| DAY17-18 | protein | 단백질<br>food that helps your body grow strong | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>생물학과 유전학 장면에서 '단백질'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Beans and eggs give protein to growing young bodies. | Today, beans and eggs give protein to growing young bodies. | Soybeans provide a rich source of plant protein for healthy diets. | Beans and eggs give protein that helps young bodies grow. |  |
| DAY17-19 | hatch | 부화하다<br>to break out of an egg | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>생물학과 유전학 장면에서 '부화하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The duck eggs will hatch beside the quiet pond. | The duck eggs will hatch next to the quiet pond. | Baby sea turtles hatch from eggs buried in warm beach sand. | Duck eggs hatch after about four weeks of steady warmth. |  |
| DAY17-20 | direct | 직접적인<br>going straight without turning aside | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>생물학과 유전학 장면에서 '직접적인'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The direct path leads right to the old stone well. | Today, the direct path leads right to the old stone well. | Sunlight provides a direct source of energy for green plant leaves. | Sunflowers need direct sunlight for most of the day. |  |

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY17-21 | individual | 개인의<br>for one person only | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>생물학과 유전학 장면에서 '개인의'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Each camper received an individual bowl of warm soup. | Today, each camper received an individual bowl of warm soup. | Each individual plant in the garden exhibits unique stem height variations. | Each student used an individual tray for the soil samples. |  |
| DAY17-22 | silent | 조용한<br>making no sound at all | 국어 / 음운과 문자 체계 (Phonology and writing systems)<br>contextual<br>말소리의 크기와 들림 정도를 비교해 소리의 특징을 설명한다 | The snowy forest stayed completely silent at early dawn. | Students compare a silent room with recorded speech. | A silent whisper carries less vocal sound energy than a shout. | The classroom went silent, so we heard every soft breath sound. |  |
| DAY17-23 | fiber | 섬유<br>a thin thread inside plants or cloth | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>생물학과 유전학 장면에서 '섬유'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Strong fiber from the plant became a woven basket. | Today, strong fiber from the plant became a woven basket. | Cotton cloth is woven from natural plant fiber harvested from bolls. | One cotton fiber looks thin and white under the microscope. |  |
| DAY17-24 | ancestor | 조상<br>a family member from long, long ago | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>생물학과 유전학 장면에서 '조상'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | An ancestor of this tree grew here two centuries ago. | Today, an ancestor of this tree grew here two centuries ago. | The ancient wolf is the biological ancestor of modern domestic dogs. | My great-grandmother is the oldest ancestor in our family photo. |  |
| DAY17-25 | yawn | 하품하다<br>to open your mouth when you are sleepy | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>생물학과 유전학 장면에서 '하품하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The lion cubs yawn widely before their afternoon nap. | Before their afternoon nap, the lion cubs yawn widely. | Sleepy animals often yawn when their bodies need more fresh oxygen. | Sleepy puppies yawn together before curling into the warm basket. |  |
| DAY17-26 | pregnant | 임신한<br>having a baby growing inside | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>생물학과 유전학 장면에서 '임신한'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The pregnant cat rested in a soft warm basket. | Today, the pregnant cat rested in a soft warm basket. | The veterinarian examined the pregnant sheep to ensure a safe delivery. | The pregnant cat rested quietly inside the soft straw box. |  |
| DAY17-27 | stimulate | 자극하다<br>to make something more active | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>생물학과 유전학 장면에서 '자극하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Bright colors stimulate curiosity in very young children. | Bright colors stimulate curiosity in very school children. | Warm spring temperatures stimulate plant seeds to germinate in garden soil. | Warm water can stimulate these seeds to sprout more quickly. |  |
| DAY17-28 | slightly | 약간<br>a very small amount | fallback<br>word_fallback<br>한 장면에서 '약간'의 확정 뜻이 다른 뜻으로 오해되지 않게 직접 보여 준다 | The tower leaned slightly toward the sunny garden side. | Today, the tower leaned slightly toward the sunny garden side. | The second seedling grew slightly taller than the first test plant. | The left plant grew slightly taller than the right one. |  |
| DAY17-29 | bleed | 피가 나다<br>to lose blood from the body | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>생물학과 유전학 장면에서 '피가 나다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The dog began to bleed, so we wrapped his injured paw. | The dog started to bleed, so we wrapped his injured paw. | Minor paper cuts can bleed slightly before forming a protective scab. | If your nose begins to bleed, sit down and stay calm. |  |
| DAY17-30 | breed | 기르다<br>to raise animals so they have babies | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>생물학과 유전학 장면에서 '기르다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Farmers breed gentle rabbits on the small green hill. | Today, farmers breed gentle rabbits on the small green hill. | Farmers breed hardy sheep to produce warm wool for winter clothing. | Our neighbors breed rabbits and keep careful notes about each litter. |  |

| DAY17-35 | interfere | 방해하다<br>to get in the way of something | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>생물학과 유전학 장면에서 '방해하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Tall weeds interfere with the growth of small carrots. | Today, tall weeds interfere with the growth of small carrots. | Excessive noise can interfere with birds communicating their mating territory calls. | Loud noises interfere with the birds feeding near our window. |  |
| DAY17-36 | identical | 동일한<br>exactly the same in every way | 수학 / 합동인 도형 (Congruent Figures)<br>contextual<br>두 도형을 포개어 대응하는 변과 각이 동일한지 확인한다 | The twin kittens looked identical except for one whisker. | Two identical triangles match when placed directly together. | The two congruent triangles have identical side lengths and angle measurements. | These two triangles are identical when we place one over another. |  |
| DAY17-37 | empathy | 공감<br>understanding how another person feels | 국어 / 공감적 경청 (Active listening)<br>direct<br>상대의 말을 듣고 고개를 끄덕이거나 뜻을 확인해 공감을 드러낸다 | Her empathy helped the shy new student feel welcome. | She shows empathy by restating her friend's worry before answering. | She showed active empathy by listening attentively to her friend's problem. | Nodding while listening showed empathy for my friend's difficult week. |  |
| DAY17-38 | dramatic | 극적인<br>very big and easy to notice | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>생물학과 유전학 장면에서 '극적인'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A dramatic sunset painted the whole sky bright orange. | One dramatic sunset painted the whole sky bright orange. | Tadpoles undergo a dramatic physical transformation into adult jumping green frogs. | The bean plant showed dramatic growth after three sunny days. |  |
| DAY17-39 | explicit | 명확한<br>very clear and easy to understand | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>생물학과 유전학 장면에서 '명확한'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The explicit map showed every turn along the trail. | The explicit map displayed every turn along the trail. | The biology teacher gave explicit instructions for the microscope slide preparation. | Our lab rules are explicit about washing hands before leaving. |  |
| DAY17-40 | tissue | 화장지<br>soft thin paper used for wiping | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>생물학과 유전학 장면에서 '화장지'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A soft tissue wiped the spilled juice off quickly. | The child uses a tissue to wipe spilled juice. | Use a soft paper tissue to wipe the microscope glass lens. | Wipe the wet table with one soft paper tissue. |  |

| DAY18-09 | grab | 붙잡다<br>to take hold of something quickly | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>의학과 질병 장면에서 '붙잡다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The monkey will grab a banana from the low branch. | Today, the monkey will grab a banana from the low branch. | He had to grab the railing to keep his balance firmly. | I grab the handrail quickly when the bus starts moving. |  |

## DAY 18 · set 2 · 과학&기술 / 의학과 질병

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY18-11 | prevent | 막다<br>to stop something from happening | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>의학과 질병 장면에서 '막다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A warm hat can prevent cold ears in winter. | One warm hat can prevent cold ears in winter. | Washing hands regularly helps prevent the spread of common viral infections. | Washing hands often can prevent colds during the winter. |  |
| DAY18-12 | suffer | 고통받다<br>to feel something unpleasant for a while | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>의학과 질병 장면에서 '고통받다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Young plants suffer when the soil becomes too dry. | When the soil becomes too dry, young plants suffer. | Patients suffer from throat discomfort during severe seasonal winter cold infections. | Many people suffer from itchy eyes during the spring pollen season. |  |
| DAY18-13 | ruin | 망치다<br>to spoil something completely | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>의학과 질병 장면에서 '망치다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Heavy rain can ruin a paper kite very quickly. | Heavy rain can ruin a paper kite very rapidly. | Leaving food unrefrigerated in summer will ruin its nutritional quality quickly. | Too much water can ruin these paper drawings completely. |  |
| DAY18-14 | organ | 장기<br>a body part that does one job | 과학 / 생물의 구성 단계 (Levels of Biological Organization)<br>direct<br>세포가 조직·기관·기관계·개체를 이루는 순서를 배열한다 | The heart is an organ that pumps blood everywhere. | Cells form tissues, and tissues form each organ. | The human heart is an essential organ in the circulatory system. | The heart is one organ built from many muscle tissues. |  |
| DAY18-15 | instead | 대신에<br>in place of something else | fallback<br>word_fallback<br>한 장면에서 '대신에'의 확정 뜻이 다른 뜻으로 오해되지 않게 직접 보여 준다 | The cat chose the box instead of the soft bed. | The house cat chose the box instead of the soft bed. | Drink clean fresh water instead of sugary soda drinks with meals. | We drank warm water instead of cold juice today. |  |
| DAY18-16 | ankle | 발목<br>the joint between your foot and leg | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>의학과 질병 장면에서 '발목'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The runner wrapped a warm band around her ankle. | Today, the runner wrapped a warm band around her ankle. | The runner wrapped an elastic bandage around his sprained right ankle. | The runner wrapped a soft band around one sore ankle. |  |
| DAY18-17 | nutrition | 영양<br>the good things in food for your body | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>의학과 질병 장면에서 '영양'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Fresh vegetables give excellent nutrition to growing young children. | Fresh vegetables give excellent nutrition to growing school children. | Balanced dietary nutrition provides essential vitamins for growing children and teenagers. | Beans, rice, and vegetables give balanced nutrition at lunch. |  |
| DAY18-18 | decrease | 감소하다<br>to become smaller in number or size | 수학 / 그래프 해석 (Interpreting Graphs)<br>contextual<br>그래프의 축과 눈금을 읽고 값이 증가·감소하는 구간과 변화 속도를 설명한다 | The snow will decrease as warm spring days arrive. | Students find where graph values decrease along the time axis. | The line graph shows temperatures decrease steadily during late autumn months. | The line graph shows cold cases decrease sharply after March. |  |
| DAY18-19 | trouble | 문제<br>a problem that makes things difficult | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>의학과 질병 장면에서 '문제'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The tangled string caused trouble for the young kite flyer. | Today, the tangled string caused trouble for the young kite flyer. | Faulty wiring caused electrical trouble in the hospital emergency room clinic. | Breathing trouble sent the swimmer to the school nurse. |  |
| DAY18-20 | seal | 밀봉하다<br>to close something tightly | 과학 / 기체 압력 (Gas Pressure)<br>contextual<br>밀봉한 용기 안 기체 입자의 충돌이 압력을 만드는 모습을 모형으로 본다 | The cook will seal the container with a firm lid. | Students seal a bottle before testing how trapped air pushes. | Scientists seal the glass container to measure rising internal gas pressure. | We seal the bottle, so trapped air pushes on its walls. |  |

## DAY 18 · set 3 · 과학&기술 / 의학과 질병

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY18-21 | track | 추적하다<br>to follow the movement or progress of something | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>의학과 질병 장면에서 '추적하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The ranger will track the deer using prints in snow. | Nurses track the patient's temperature on a daily chart. | Doctors track the patient's heart rate using digital electrocardiogram monitor screens. | Nurses track each patient's temperature on a simple daily chart. |  |
| DAY18-22 | perhaps | 아마도<br>maybe, but not for sure | fallback<br>word_fallback<br>한 장면에서 '아마도'의 확정 뜻이 다른 뜻으로 오해되지 않게 직접 보여 준다 | Perhaps the rain will stop before the picnic begins. | Before the picnic begins, perhaps the rain will stop. | Getting more rest will perhaps relieve your mild afternoon headache symptoms. | The clinic opens at nine, or perhaps a little later. |  |
| DAY18-23 | bump | 부딪히다, 찧다<br>to hit something accidentally | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>의학과 질병 장면에서 '부딪히다, 찧다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Walk carefully in the dark so you do not bump into furniture. | The student may bump the chair while walking backward. | Be careful not to bump your head against the low ceiling. | Be careful not to bump your head on the low shelf. |  |
| DAY18-24 | alert | 경계하는<br>watching carefully and ready to act | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>의학과 질병 장면에서 '경계하는'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The alert meerkat stood tall and watched the horizon. | Today, the alert meerkat stood tall and watched the horizon. | The emergency nurse stayed alert during the busy hospital night shift. | The lifeguard stayed alert and watched every swimmer closely. |  |
| DAY18-25 | slip | 미끄러지다<br>to slide by accident on something | 과학 / 마찰력 (Friction)<br>contextual<br>표면의 거칠기와 마찰이 미끄러지는 움직임에 주는 영향을 비교한다 | Do not slip on the shiny wet kitchen floor. | Shoes slip farther on smooth tiles with less friction. | Shoes with rubber soles will not slip on wet laboratory floors. | Shoes slip on wet tiles but hold on rough mats. |  |
| DAY18-26 | modest | 겸손한<br>not boastful about what you did | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>의학과 질병 장면에서 '겸손한'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The modest winner smiled quietly and thanked her coach. | Today, the modest winner smiled quietly and thanked her coach. | The celebrated surgeon gave a modest response to the prestigious award. | Our young doctor stayed modest about the difficult rescue. |  |
| DAY18-27 | disabled | 장애가 있는<br>having a body part that works differently | 사회 / 인권과 헌법상 기본권 (Human Rights and Constitutional Rights)<br>contextual<br>필요한 권리나 접근이 부족한 사람이 동등하게 교육과 서비스를 이용할 방법을 찾는다 | The disabled bird used a tiny wheeled cart happily. | A ramp gives disabled students equal access to the classroom. | Public buildings must provide wheelchair ramps for disabled citizens and visitors. | A new ramp lets disabled visitors enter the library easily. |  |
| DAY18-28 | rapid | 빠른<br>moving or happening very fast | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>의학과 질병 장면에서 '빠른'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A rapid stream rushed between the smooth gray rocks. | One rapid stream rushed between the smooth gray rocks. | Antibiotics caused a rapid improvement in the patient's bacterial infection condition. | Rapid breathing slowed down after the swimmer rested five minutes. |  |
| DAY18-29 | spin | 돌다<br>to turn around and around quickly | 수학 / 회전체 (Solids of Revolution)<br>contextual<br>평면도형을 한 축 둘레로 돌려 생기는 입체 모양을 관찰한다 | The colorful top will spin across the wooden table. | Students spin a paper triangle around one fixed axis. | Spin a rectangle around its axis to form a solid cylinder. | Spin this flat rectangle around one edge to make a cylinder. |  |
| DAY18-30 | spine | 척추<br>the line of bones along your back | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>의학과 질병 장면에서 '척추'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A cat stretches its spine after a long nap. | After a long nap, a cat stretches its spine. | The human spine supports body weight and protects the spinal cord. | Sitting straight keeps the spine in a healthy line. |  |

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |

| DAY18-31 | rare | 드문<br>not seen or found very often | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>의학과 질병 장면에서 '드문'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A rare blue butterfly landed on the garden gate. | One rare blue butterfly landed on the garden gate. | Medical researchers studied a rare genetic condition affecting muscle development cells. | This rare mountain flower blooms only one week each year. |  |
| DAY18-32 | beat | 이기다, 치다, 두드리다<br>to defeat an opponent in a game or hit rhythmically | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>의학과 질병 장면에서 '이기다, 치다, 두드리다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Our soccer team managed to beat the champions in final minutes. | Doctors hear the patient's heart beat steadily through a stethoscope. | Regular exercise makes the human heart beat with strong steady rhythm. | Doctors count how many times a heart can beat each minute. |  |

| DAY18-35 | antibiotic | 항생제<br>a medicine that fights tiny germs | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>의학과 질병 장면에서 '항생제'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The vet used an antibiotic to help the kitten heal. | Today, the vet used an antibiotic to help the kitten heal. | The doctor prescribed an effective antibiotic to cure the bacterial infection. | The doctor explained that an antibiotic works only against bacteria. |  |
| DAY18-36 | chronic | 만성의<br>lasting a very long time | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>의학과 질병 장면에서 '만성의'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The old tree had a chronic lean toward the pond. | Today, the old tree had a chronic lean toward the pond. | Regular physical therapy helps patients manage chronic back pain and stiffness. | A chronic cough lasted many months before the treatment helped. |  |
| DAY18-37 | sight | 시력<br>the power to see with your eyes | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>의학과 질병 장면에서 '시력'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | An eagle has amazing sight from very high above. | Today, an eagle has amazing sight from very high above. | Wearing corrective glasses helped restore her clear distant vision and sight. | Regular eye checkups protect the sight of young students. |  |
| DAY18-38 | fatal | 치명적인<br>very serious | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>의학과 질병 장면에서 '치명적인'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The snake's fatal venom can quickly kill a small animal. | A plant disease became fatal after its leaves turned brown. | Early diagnosis helps prevent serious diseases from causing fatal medical complications. | Skipping the safety check was a fatal error for the model rocket. |  |

| DAY18-40 | patient | 환자<br>a person a doctor is helping | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>의학과 질병 장면에서 '환자'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The patient rested quietly in a bright sunny room. | A nurse brings warm soup to the recovering patient. | The caring nurse checked on the recovering patient every two hours. | One patient waited calmly while the nurse checked the chart. |  |

## DAY 19 · set 1 · 과학&기술 / 정보와 기술

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY19-01 | modern | 현대의<br>new and from the present time | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>정보와 기술 장면에서 '현대의'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The modern kitchen had shiny buttons on every drawer. | Today, the modern kitchen had shiny buttons on every drawer. | Modern computer networks allow instant communication across distant global research laboratories. | This modern printer works faster than the old office one. |  |
| DAY19-02 | mobile | 이동하는<br>able to be moved from place to place | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>정보와 기술 장면에서 '이동하는'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A mobile library visits the village every second week. | One mobile library visits the village every second week. | The mobile hospital clinic travels to rural villages to deliver vaccines. | The library keeps a mobile book cart with small wheels. |  |
| DAY19-03 | function | 기능<br>the job that a thing does | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>정보와 기술 장면에서 '기능'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The main function of an umbrella is blocking rain. | Today, the main function of an umbrella is blocking rain. | An essential function of the computer processor is executing mathematical calculations. | The main function of this small fan is cooling air. |  |
| DAY19-04 | machine | 기계<br>a tool with parts that does work | 과학 / 과학과 기술 (Science and Technology)<br>direct<br>문제를 해결할 장치를 설계하고 시제품의 조건과 한계를 시험한다 | A friendly machine washed every dish after the dinner. | Students test the machine under heavy loads and record limits. | The young engineer tests a small machine prototype to sort recyclables. | Our sorting machine drops light balls into the left box. |  |
| DAY19-05 | repair | 수리하다<br>to fix something that is broken | 과학 / 적정 기술 (Appropriate Technology)<br>contextual<br>지역의 비용과 재료·수리 조건에 맞는 간단한 장치를 시험한다 | The mechanic will repair the squeaky old garden gate. | Villagers repair the simple pump using cheap local parts. | Technicians use simple affordable tools to repair solar cookers in villages. | Villagers repair the water pump with cheap local parts. |  |
| DAY19-06 | efficient | 효율적인<br>doing a job well without wasting anything | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>정보와 기술 장면에서 '효율적인'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The efficient robot cleaned the whole hallway in minutes. | Today, the efficient robot cleaned the whole hallway in minutes. | Electric motors provide an efficient method for converting electrical energy cleanly. | This lamp is efficient because it uses very little electricity. |  |
| DAY19-07 | install | 설치하다<br>to put something in place so it works | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>정보와 기술 장면에서 '설치하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Workers will install new lamps along the quiet park path. | Today, workers will install new lamps along the quiet park path. | Technicians install solar energy panels on the school library roof today. | Workers install a new fan above the computer room door. |  |
| DAY19-08 | innovation | 혁신<br>a clever new idea or invention | 과학 / 과학과 기술 (Science and Technology)<br>direct<br>문제를 해결할 장치를 설계하고 시제품의 조건과 한계를 시험한다 | This little innovation saves water in every village home. | Students test the water-saving innovation and record its limits. | Technological innovation produces new devices that solve local clean water shortages. | This innovation lets the model cart carry twice its old load. |  |
| DAY19-09 | trust | 신뢰하다<br>to believe someone will do the right thing | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>정보와 기술 장면에서 '신뢰하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The ducklings trust the old goose to lead them. | Today, the ducklings trust the old goose to lead them. | Computer users trust verified software programs to protect their personal data. | We trust this weather site because it lists its sources. |  |
| DAY19-10 | truth | 진실<br>what is really true | 국어 / 자료 신뢰성 (Source credibility)<br>contextual<br>자료의 작성자·날짜·근거를 확인하고 다른 출처와 대조한다 | The truth about the missing pie made everyone laugh. | Students seek the truth by checking dates and two sources. | Researchers verify data from multiple sources to determine the factual truth. | To find the truth we checked the author and the date. |  |

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY19-11 | imagine | 상상하다<br>to make a picture in your mind | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>정보와 기술 장면에서 '상상하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Children imagine castles when they look at fluffy clouds. | When they look at fluffy clouds, children imagine castles. | Creative engineers imagine new robotic tools to assist firefighters during emergencies. | Close your eyes and imagine a robot folding paper cranes. |  |
| DAY19-12 | artificial | 인공의<br>made by people, not by nature | 과학 / 인공지능과 과학 (Artificial Intelligence and Science)<br>contextual<br>사람이 만든 인공지능이 자료에서 규칙을 찾아 예측하는 과정을 확인한다 | The artificial pond in the park has plastic lilies. | An artificial system studies weather data and predicts tomorrow's rain. | Computer scientists train artificial intelligence models using large datasets of images. | This artificial helper learned rules from thousands of weather records. |  |
| DAY19-13 | adjust | 조정하다<br>to change something a little to fit better | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>정보와 기술 장면에서 '조정하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Please adjust the seat before you ride the bicycle. | Kindly adjust the seat before you ride the bicycle. | Engineers adjust the optical telescope focus to capture clearer space images. | Please adjust the chair height before you start typing. |  |
| DAY19-14 | ideal | 이상적인<br>the best it could possibly be | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>정보와 기술 장면에서 '이상적인'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A calm sunny day is ideal for flying kites. | One calm sunny day is ideal for flying kites. | A sunny rooftop is an ideal location for solar panel installation. | A cool dry room is ideal for storing these batteries. |  |
| DAY19-15 | gather | 모으다<br>to bring things or people together | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>정보와 기술 장면에서 '모으다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The villagers gather firewood before the cold season starts. | Local residents gather firewood before the cold season starts. | Autonomous weather drones gather atmospheric temperature data across the storm area. | Students gather rainfall numbers from three different school sensors. |  |
| DAY19-16 | mechanic | 정비사<br>a person who fixes machines | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>정보와 기술 장면에서 '정비사'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A cheerful mechanic checked the wheels of the old bus. | One cheerful mechanic checked the wheels of the old bus. | The skilled auto mechanic repaired the hybrid car's electric braking system. | The mechanic opened the engine cover and checked each belt. |  |
| DAY19-17 | portable | 휴대용의<br>easy to carry from place to place | 과학 / 적정 기술 (Appropriate Technology)<br>contextual<br>지역의 비용과 재료·수리 조건에 맞는 간단한 장치를 시험한다 | A portable lamp lit the small camping tent softly. | The portable filter uses local sand and is easy to repair. | Volunteers carry a portable water filter to purify muddy river water. | This portable water filter fits inside one small backpack. |  |
| DAY19-18 | remove | 제거하다<br>to take something away from a place | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>정보와 기술 장면에서 '제거하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Please remove your muddy boots before entering the hall. | Kindly remove your muddy boots before entering the hall. | Specialized filter membranes remove microplastic particles from municipal drinking water supplies. | Please remove the dust from the keyboard with a brush. |  |
| DAY19-19 | master | 숙달하다<br>to learn to do something very well | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>정보와 기술 장면에서 '숙달하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The young baker will master this bread recipe soon. | Today, the young baker will master this bread recipe soon. | Dedicated students master computer programming languages through daily problem solving practice. | Young coders master simple loops after many practice sessions. |  |
| DAY19-20 | screen | 화면<br>a flat surface that shows pictures | 국어 / 상호 작용적 매체 (Interactive media)<br>direct<br>화면의 선택에 따라 정보가 바뀌고 사용자가 피드백을 보내는 과정을 살핀다 | A big screen showed the funny film outdoors tonight. | The screen changes information after the user selects a button. | Users tap the digital screen to select options and receive feedback. | Touching the screen changes the map, and the program answers back. |  |

## DAY 19 · set 3 · 과학&기술 / 정보와 기술

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY19-21 | widespread | 널리 퍼진<br>found in many places | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>정보와 기술 장면에서 '널리 퍼진'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Wildflowers became widespread across the whole green hillside. | Today, wildflowers became widespread across the whole green hillside. | High-speed internet access has achieved widespread adoption across urban school districts. | Solar lamps became widespread across these small mountain villages. |  |
| DAY19-22 | edge | 가장자리<br>the line where something ends | 수학 / 점, 선, 면, 각 (Basic Geometric Objects)<br>direct<br>점·선·면·각을 그리고 서로 평행하거나 수직인지 확인한다 | A cup sat near the edge of the wooden table. | Students count each edge where two flat faces meet. | Draw a sharp straight line along the edge of the ruler. | We drew one straight edge parallel to the paper's bottom line. |  |
| DAY19-23 | remind | 상기시키다<br>to help someone remember something | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>정보와 기술 장면에서 '상기시키다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Please remind me to water the little herb pots. | Kindly remind me to water the small herb pots. | Automated calendar notifications remind students about upcoming homework submission deadlines daily. | This alarm will remind us to water the classroom plants. |  |
| DAY19-24 | acquire | 얻다<br>to get or learn something over time | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>정보와 기술 장면에서 '얻다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Young otters acquire swimming skills from their patient parents. | Today, young otters acquire swimming skills from their patient parents. | Modern sensors acquire real-time environmental data to monitor air quality levels. | Beginners acquire coding skills slowly through weekly club meetings. |  |
| DAY19-25 | interact | 상호 작용하다<br>to talk or work together with others | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>정보와 기술 장면에서 '상호 작용하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Visitors interact with friendly goats at the small farm. | Today, visitors interact with friendly goats at the small farm. | Students interact with educational simulation software during interactive science classroom lessons. | Two robots interact by passing the ball back and forth. |  |
| DAY19-26 | scatter | 흩뿌리다<br>to throw things in many directions | 과학 / 확산과 증발 (Diffusion and Evaporation)<br>contextual<br>입자가 여러 방향으로 흩어지며 농도 차이가 줄어드는 모습을 관찰한다 | The farmer will scatter seeds across the wide field. | Perfume particles scatter until the smell spreads across the room. | Gas molecules scatter randomly throughout the room until scent becomes uniform. | Perfume particles scatter through the room until the smell spreads evenly. |  |
| DAY19-27 | virtual | 가상의<br>made by a computer, not really there | 국어 / 상호 작용적 매체 (Interactive media)<br>contextual<br>가상 화면에서 사용자의 선택에 따라 정보와 반응이 달라지는 과정을 살핀다 | A virtual fish swam across the glowing museum wall. | A virtual map changes when users choose another route. | Students explore ancient historical monuments inside an interactive virtual reality simulation. | In this virtual museum, clicking a painting opens more information. |  |
| DAY19-28 | convert | 바꾸다<br>to change something into another form | 과학 / 상태 변화 (Changes of State)<br>contextual<br>가열하거나 식혀 물질을 한 상태에서 다른 상태로 바꾸는 과정을 관찰한다 | Panels convert bright sunlight into useful electric power. | Heating can convert solid ice into liquid water. | Heating will convert solid ice blocks directly into liquid drinking water. | Heating will convert this solid butter into smooth yellow liquid. |  |
| DAY19-29 | ease | 쉬움, 편안함<br>the state of experiencing no difficulty, effort, or pain | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>정보와 기술 장면에서 '쉬움, 편안함'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The experienced gymnast completed the difficult balance routine with ease. | Today, the experienced gymnast completed the difficult balance routine with ease. | Modern automated software tools perform complex calculations with remarkable computational ease. | The new handle lets us lift the box with ease. |  |
| DAY19-30 | erase | 지우다<br>to rub something out completely | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>정보와 기술 장면에서 '지우다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Waves erase every footprint along the smooth wet sand. | Today, waves erase every footprint along the smooth wet sand. | Users can erase temporary cache files to free up disk storage. | Please erase the old numbers before writing today's results. |  |

## DAY 19 · set 4 · 과학&기술 / 정보와 기술

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY19-31 | navigate | 항해하다<br>to find your way to a place | 사회 / 지리 정보와 지도 (Geographic Information and Maps)<br>contextual<br>지도와 위치 정보를 이용해 목적지까지 가는 길을 찾는다 | Sailors navigate using bright stars on clear dark nights. | Sailors navigate toward the harbor using a map and compass. | Digital map applications help drivers navigate through unfamiliar city streets safely. | We navigate to the museum using a paper map and compass. |  |
| DAY19-32 | reproduce | 복제하다<br>to make a copy of something | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>정보와 기술 장면에서 '복제하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The printer can reproduce the drawing in perfect color. | Today, the printer can reproduce the drawing in perfect color. | Digital printers reproduce high-resolution color photographs with sharp visual detail accuracy. | This printer can reproduce the old photograph without losing detail. |  |
| DAY19-33 | differ | 다르다<br>to be not the same as another | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>정보와 기술 장면에서 '다르다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | These two leaves differ in shape and green color. | Today, these two leaves differ in shape and green color. | The two software versions differ in their user interface design layouts. | The two battery models differ in weight and charging time. |  |
| DAY19-34 | instruction | 지시<br>words that tell you how to do something | 국어 / 정보 전달 글쓰기 (Informative writing)<br>direct<br>독자가 절차나 사실을 이해하도록 정확한 정보를 순서 있게 쓴다 | One simple instruction helped everyone fold the paper crane. | Each instruction gives one clear step for folding the crane. | The manual provides clear instruction for assembling the new drone kit. | Each instruction lists one action, so beginners follow the order easily. |  |
| DAY19-35 | significant | 중요한<br>important enough to notice | fallback<br>word_fallback<br>한 장면에서 '중요한'의 확정 뜻이 다른 뜻으로 오해되지 않게 직접 보여 준다 | A significant change happened after the garden got sunlight. | After the garden got sunlight, a significant change happened. | The invention of microchips was a significant milestone in computer technology. | The battery lasted three hours longer, a significant improvement. |  |
| DAY19-36 | satellite | 인공위성<br>a machine that circles high above earth | 과학 / 태양계 천체 (Solar System Objects)<br>direct<br>태양·행성·위성·소행성을 특징과 궤도로 분류한다 | The satellite sent weather pictures back to the scientists. | A satellite orbits a planet, unlike an asteroid around the Sun. | The communications satellite orbits Earth to transmit international television broadcast signals. | This weather satellite circles Earth and sends cloud pictures daily. |  |
| DAY19-37 | demonstrate | 시연하다<br>to show clearly how something works | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>정보와 기술 장면에서 '시연하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The teacher will demonstrate how magnets pull metal pins. | An instructor will demonstrate how magnets pull metal pins. | Engineers demonstrate how the new robotic arm sorts recyclable metal cans. | Our teacher will demonstrate how the small motor spins the wheel. |  |
| DAY19-38 | emerge | 나타나다<br>to come out into view | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>정보와 기술 장면에서 '나타나다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Green shoots emerge from the soil after warm rain. | After warm rain, green shoots emerge from the soil. | New technological solutions emerge when creative inventors collaborate on difficult challenges. | Green shoots emerge from the soil after four warm days. |  |
| DAY19-39 | trigger | 유발하다<br>to make something start happening | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>정보와 기술 장면에서 '유발하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Bright light can trigger the flowers to open wide. | Today, bright light can trigger the flowers to open wide. | Sensors trigger an automatic emergency alarm when detecting elevated smoke levels. | Bright sunlight can trigger this sensor to close the blinds. |  |
| DAY19-40 | current | 현재의<br>happening right now | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>정보와 기술 장면에서 '현재의'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The current weather is perfect for a long walk. | Today, the current weather is perfect for a long walk. | The website displays the current local temperature and hourly weather forecast. | The screen shows the current temperature inside the greenhouse. |  |

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |

| DAY20-02 | major | 주요한<br>very big and important | fallback<br>word_fallback<br>한 장면에서 '주요한'의 확정 뜻이 다른 뜻으로 오해되지 않게 직접 보여 준다 | The major road leads straight to the busy harbor. | Today, the major road leads straight to the busy harbor. | Subway expansion is a major public transportation infrastructure project for city. | The major road through our town has four wide lanes. |  |
| DAY20-03 | route | 경로<br>the way you take to a place | 사회 / 지리적 스케일과 공간적 상호작용 (Geographic Scale and Spatial Interaction)<br>direct<br>거리와 교통망에 따라 사람·물자의 이동 범위가 달라짐을 지도에서 본다 | The bus route passes three parks and one library. | The route shortens travel distance between farms and markets. | The delivery truck follows a designated route to deliver packages promptly. | This bus route reaches three villages beyond the river. |  |
| DAY20-04 | rush | 서두르다<br>to move or do something very quickly | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>교통과 통신 장면에서 '서두르다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Do not rush while carrying the tall stack of plates. | While carrying the tall stack of plates, do not rush. | Passengers rush toward the train platform when boarding call is announced. | Commuters rush toward the platform when the bell rings. |  |
| DAY20-05 | vehicle | 차량<br>a machine that carries people or things | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>교통과 통신 장면에서 '차량'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A small yellow vehicle delivered bread to the village. | One small yellow vehicle delivered bread to the village. | An electric vehicle produces zero tailpipe emissions while driving on streets. | One small electric vehicle carries mail around the campus. |  |
| DAY20-06 | transport | 운송하다<br>to carry things from one place to another | 사회 / 지리적 스케일과 공간적 상호작용 (Geographic Scale and Spatial Interaction)<br>direct<br>거리와 교통망에 따라 사람·물자의 이동 범위가 달라짐을 지도에서 본다 | Boats transport fresh fruit across the calm blue bay. | Boats transport fruit farther where rivers connect distant towns. | Cargo freight trains transport heavy raw materials across long continental distances. | Trains transport apples from mountain farms to the coastal city. |  |
| DAY20-07 | utilize | 활용하다<br>to use something in a helpful way | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>교통과 통신 장면에서 '활용하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Farmers utilize rainwater to keep the vegetables green. | Today, farmers utilize rainwater to keep the vegetables green. | Commuters utilize mobile navigation apps to find faster bus transit routes. | Drivers utilize the new tunnel to save fifteen minutes. |  |
| DAY20-08 | remote | 외딴<br>far away from towns and people | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>교통과 통신 장면에서 '외딴'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A remote cabin stood alone among the snowy pines. | One remote cabin stood alone among the snowy pines. | Small propeller aircraft provide essential supply transport to remote island communities. | One remote island receives mail by boat twice each month. |  |
| DAY20-09 | leap | 뛰어오르다<br>to jump high or far | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>교통과 통신 장면에서 '뛰어오르다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Frogs leap easily from one lily pad to another. | Today, frogs leap easily from one lily pad to another. | Athletes leap over high hurdles during track and field competitions. | Deer sometimes leap over the low fence beside the highway. |  |
| DAY20-10 | leak | 새다<br>to let liquid escape through a small hole | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>교통과 통신 장면에서 '새다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The old bucket started to leak water onto the ground. | Today, the old bucket started to leak water onto the ground. | A rusty pipe began to leak clean water onto floor. | Old pipes leak water onto the sidewalk every rainy week. |  |

| DAY20-16 | accurate | 정확한<br>exactly right with no mistakes | 국어 / 자료 신뢰성 (Source credibility)<br>contextual<br>자료의 작성자·날짜·근거를 확인하고 다른 출처와 대조한다 | The accurate map showed every turn along the river. | Students verify the accurate map by checking two current sources. | GPS receivers provide accurate geographic positioning data for commercial aviation navigation. | This timetable is accurate because the station updated it today. |  |
