| DAY20-19 | fuel | 연료<br>something burned to make a machine run | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>교통과 통신 장면에서 '연료'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The little boat needed fuel before the long trip. | The small boat needed fuel before the long trip. | Aviation fuel powers jet turbine engines during long transcontinental passenger flights. | This bus uses less fuel than the older diesel one. |  |
| DAY20-20 | miss | 놓치다<br>to be too late for something | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>교통과 통신 장면에서 '놓치다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Hurry, or we will miss the last evening ferry. | Today, hurry, or we will miss the last evening ferry. | Hurry so you do not miss the last evening express bus. | We might miss the ferry if the taxi arrives late. |  |

## DAY 20 · set 3 · 과학&기술 / 교통과 통신

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY20-21 | license | 면허증<br>a card that lets you do something | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>교통과 통신 장면에서 '면허증'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A boat license hung proudly inside the small cabin. | A boat license hung proudly within the small cabin. | Drivers must carry a valid driver's license while operating any vehicle. | The driver showed a license before renting the small van. |  |
| DAY20-22 | construction | 건설<br>the work of building something new | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>교통과 통신 장면에서 '건설'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The bridge construction continued all through the warm summer. | Today, the bridge construction continued all through the warm summer. | Highway construction workers repair asphalt pavement to ensure safe driving conditions. | Bridge construction closed one lane for the whole summer. |  |
| DAY20-23 | wireless | 무선의<br>working without any connecting wires | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>교통과 통신 장면에서 '무선의'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A wireless speaker played music beside the picnic blanket. | A wireless speaker played music next to the picnic blanket. | Smartphones connect to wireless local area networks for high-speed internet access. | This wireless speaker plays music without any cable attached. |  |
| DAY20-24 | nevertheless | 그럼에도 불구하고<br>even though that is true | fallback<br>word_fallback<br>한 장면에서 '그럼에도 불구하고'의 확정 뜻이 다른 뜻으로 오해되지 않게 직접 보여 준다 | It rained; nevertheless, the parade continued down the street. | Today, it rained; nevertheless, the parade continued down the street. | The rain poured heavily; nevertheless, the train arrived exactly on time. | Rain fell hard; nevertheless, the ferry left exactly on time. |  |
| DAY20-25 | barrier | 장벽<br>something that blocks the way | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>교통과 통신 장면에서 '장벽'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A wooden barrier kept sheep off the busy road. | One wooden barrier kept sheep off the busy road. | A concrete sound barrier reduces highway noise for nearby residential neighborhoods. | A yellow barrier blocks the road beside the wet cement. |  |
| DAY20-26 | lift | 들어 올리다<br>to raise something up higher | 과학 / 힘과 알짜힘 (Force and Net Force)<br>direct<br>물체에 작용하는 힘의 크기와 방향을 화살표로 합성한다 | Two friends will lift the heavy crate together. | Two upward forces lift the box against downward gravity. | Cranes lift heavy steel construction girders to the upper building floors. | The crane must lift the beam with force greater than its weight. |  |
| DAY20-27 | curved | 굽은<br>bent in a smooth round line | 수학 / 점, 선, 면, 각 (Basic Geometric Objects)<br>contextual<br>곧은 선분과 부드럽게 굽은 선을 나란히 그리고 차이를 찾는다 | A curved bridge arched gently over the green stream. | One curved bridge arched gently over the green stream. | The highway follows a gently curved path along the scenic coastline. | We drew one curved line beside a perfectly straight one. |  |
| DAY20-28 | eliminate | 없애다<br>to remove something completely | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>교통과 통신 장면에서 '없애다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Careful sweeping can eliminate dust from every corner. | Today, careful sweeping can eliminate dust from every corner. | Modern transit authorities work to eliminate unnecessary delays on commuter routes. | New sound walls eliminate most highway noise from our classroom. |  |
| DAY20-29 | commit | 약속하다<br>to promise to do something fully | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>교통과 통신 장면에서 '약속하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The team will commit to practicing every single morning. | The club will commit to practicing every single morning. | Airlines commit to strict maintenance inspection schedules to ensure flight safety. | Drivers commit to stopping fully at every school crossing. |  |
| DAY20-30 | commute | 통근하다<br>to travel to work or school daily | 사회 / 지리적 스케일과 공간적 상호작용 (Geographic Scale and Spatial Interaction)<br>direct<br>거리와 교통망에 따라 사람·물자의 이동 범위가 달라짐을 지도에서 본다 | Many people commute by bicycle along the river path. | Workers commute farther when trains connect distant towns. | Thousands of suburban workers commute to downtown offices by subway daily. | Many workers commute forty minutes by subway from nearby cities. |  |

## DAY 20 · set 4 · 과학&기술 / 교통과 통신

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY20-31 | drag | 끌다<br>to pull something heavy along the ground | 과학 / 힘과 알짜힘 (Force and Net Force)<br>direct<br>물체에 작용하는 힘의 크기와 방향을 화살표로 합성한다 | The strong ox will drag the log across the field. | Students drag the box right while friction pulls left. | Workhorses drag heavy wooden timber sleds across snowy winter forest logging. | Two students drag the heavy sled while friction pulls backward. |  |
| DAY20-32 | fasten | 매다<br>to close or tie something firmly | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>교통과 통신 장면에서 '매다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Please fasten your helmet before riding the fast bicycle. | Kindly fasten your helmet before riding the fast bicycle. | Please fasten your airplane seat belt securely before the plane takes. | Passengers fasten their seat belts before the bus leaves. |  |
| DAY20-33 | precise | 정확한<br>exact and very carefully measured | 수학 / 작도 (Geometric Constructions)<br>contextual<br>눈금 없는 자와 컴퍼스로 같은 길이와 각을 정확히 작도한다 | The baker made precise cuts across the smooth dough. | Students draw a precise angle using a ruler and compass. | Use a compass and ruler to construct precise geometric angle bisectors. | With a compass we made a precise copy of that angle. |  |
| DAY20-34 | vital | 필수적인<br>needed very much for something to work | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>교통과 통신 장면에서 '필수적인'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Clean water is vital for every living green plant. | Today, clean water is vital for every living green plant. | Clear communication protocols are vital for safe air traffic control operations. | Working brake lights are vital for safe night driving. |  |
| DAY20-35 | component | 부품<br>one part of a bigger machine | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>교통과 통신 장면에서 '부품'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | One tiny component made the whole clock tick again. | Today, one tiny component made the whole clock tick again. | A faulty brake component was replaced by the certified auto mechanic. | One small component inside the radio stopped working yesterday. |  |
| DAY20-36 | seldom | 좀처럼 않다<br>almost never | fallback<br>word_fallback<br>한 장면에서 '좀처럼 않다'의 확정 뜻이 다른 뜻으로 오해되지 않게 직접 보여 준다 | Snow seldom falls in this warm southern valley. | Today, snow seldom falls in this warm southern valley. | High-speed bullet trains seldom experience mechanical delays during scheduled passenger service. | This quiet village road seldom carries more than five cars. |  |
| DAY20-37 | enhance | 향상시키다<br>to make something even better | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>교통과 통신 장면에서 '향상시키다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Fresh herbs enhance the flavor of the warm soup. | Today, fresh herbs enhance the flavor of the warm soup. | Upgrading signal systems will enhance railway safety and increase train frequency. | New street lamps enhance how well drivers see the crossing. |  |
| DAY20-38 | sink | 가라앉다<br>to go down under the water | 과학 / 부력 (Buoyancy)<br>contextual<br>물체의 밀도와 밀어낸 물의 양에 따라 뜨거나 가라앉는 결과를 비교한다 | Heavy stones sink quickly to the bottom of ponds. | Dense stones sink while a same-sized wooden block floats. | Heavy metal anchor chains sink rapidly to the muddy ocean floor. | A heavy stone will sink, but the light cork floats. |  |
| DAY20-39 | destination | 목적지<br>the place you are traveling to | 사회 / 지리적 위치 (Geographic Location)<br>direct<br>위도와 경도 또는 주변 지형을 이용해 장소의 위치를 설명한다 | The island was the happy destination of their trip. | The map marks our destination north of the river. | The high-speed ferry reached its coastal destination ahead of schedule today. | Our destination sits north of the river and east of the hill. |  |
| DAY20-40 | station | 역<br>a place where trains or buses stop | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>교통과 통신 장면에서 '역'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The busy station filled with travelers every early morning. | Today, the busy station filled with travelers every early morning. | Commuters wait on the platform at the central subway station terminal. | Three buses wait outside the small country train station. |  |

## DAY 21 · set 1 · 자연&환경 / 동물과 식물

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY21-01 | wild | 야생의<br>living freely in nature, not with people | 과학 / 생물다양성 보전 (Biodiversity Conservation)<br>contextual<br>야생 생물이 자연 서식지에서 살아가도록 보호하는 방법을 살핀다 | Wild horses ran across the open grassy plain. | Rangers protect wild horses by preserving their grassland habitat. | Rangers establish protected nature reserves for wild animals to roam freely. | We leave wild deer alone so they stay in the forest. |  |
| DAY21-02 | species | 종<br>one kind of animal or plant | 과학 / 생물 분류 (Biological Classification)<br>direct<br>생물의 공통 특징을 기준으로 단계적으로 분류한다 | This forest holds over twenty species of colorful birds. | Scientists group each species by shared feathers, fur, or scales. | Biologists classify each bird species by examining shared physical wing traits. | Scientists place this species in the same group as other frogs. |  |
| DAY21-03 | bark | 짖다<br>to make the loud sound a dog makes | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>동물과 식물 장면에서 '짖다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The puppies bark happily whenever the mail arrives. | The young dogs bark cheerfully whenever the mail arrives. | Watchdogs bark loudly when unfamiliar visitors approach the front gate. | Our dog will bark whenever the delivery gate opens. |  |
| DAY21-04 | nest | 둥지<br>a home birds build for their eggs | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>동물과 식물 장면에서 '둥지'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A robin built a small nest inside the hedge. | A robin built a small nest within the hedge. | The sparrow built a cozy twig nest high inside tree. | Two robins built a small nest inside the apple tree. |  |
| DAY21-05 | certain | 확실한<br>completely sure about something | fallback<br>word_fallback<br>한 장면에서 '확실한'의 확정 뜻이 다른 뜻으로 오해되지 않게 직접 보여 준다 | The guide felt certain about the path through the woods. | Today, the guide felt certain about the path through the woods. | The botanist was certain about the rare plant species classification. | I am certain these round seeds came from the sunflower. |  |
| DAY21-06 | greet | 인사하다<br>to say hello in a friendly way | 국어 / 대화의 원리 (Dialogue principles)<br>direct<br>대화 차례와 상황에 맞게 인사·요청·응답을 주고받는다 | The neighbors greet each other every bright spring morning. | Students greet visitors, then wait for their spoken response. | Polite students greet their morning school bus driver with warm smiles. | We greet the farm guide first, then ask our questions. |  |
| DAY21-07 | seed | 씨앗<br>the small part a new plant grows from | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>동물과 식물 장면에서 '씨앗'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | One sunflower seed grew into a very tall plant. | Today, one sunflower seed grew into a very tall plant. | Water and warm sunlight stimulate the sunflower seed to sprout. | One apple seed sprouted after two weeks in wet cotton. |  |
| DAY21-08 | predator | 포식자<br>an animal that hunts other animals for food | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>동물과 식물 장면에서 '포식자'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | An owl is a quiet predator of the night forest. | Today, an owl is a quiet predator of the night forest. | The owl is a nocturnal predator that hunts small field mice. | The owl is a night predator that eats small mice. |  |
| DAY21-09 | scene | 장면<br>a view or a part of a story | 국어 / 배경과 사회문화 맥락 (Setting and social context)<br>contextual<br>작품 속 한 장면의 시간과 장소가 인물 행동에 주는 영향을 찾는다 | The mountain scene looked beautiful at soft golden sunrise. | The winter scene explains why the character stays indoors. | The morning sun creates a picturesque scene across the alpine meadow. | This rainy scene explains why the children stay inside the barn. |  |
| DAY21-10 | scent | 향기<br>a smell, often a pleasant one | 과학 / 확산과 증발 (Diffusion and Evaporation)<br>contextual<br>한곳의 향기 입자가 공기 중으로 퍼져 멀리서도 느껴지는 과정을 관찰한다 | The scent of fresh bread filled the whole kitchen. | The scent spreads as particles move across the kitchen. | A sweet floral scent diffuses through the warm greenhouse room air. | The flower's scent spreads across the room within a few minutes. |  |

| DAY21-14 | lay | 알을 낳다, 놓다<br>to produce an egg from the body, or place down | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>동물과 식물 장면에서 '알을 낳다, 놓다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The mother hen will lay brown eggs in her straw nest. | Today, the mother hen will lay brown eggs in her straw nest. | Female ducks lay their smooth white eggs inside hidden reed nests. | Hens lay one egg almost every morning in warm seasons. |  |
| DAY21-15 | male | 수컷의<br>of the boy kind in animals | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>동물과 식물 장면에서 '수컷의'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The male peacock spread its bright tail feathers proudly. | Today, the male peacock spread its bright tail feathers proudly. | The colorful male peacock displays its bright tail feathers to attract. | The male peacock opens bright tail feathers during spring. |  |
| DAY21-16 | foster | 기르다<br>to care for a young animal or child | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>동물과 식물 장면에서 '기르다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The gentle hen will foster three lost little ducklings. | Today, the gentle hen will foster three lost little ducklings. | Zookeepers foster the orphaned tiger cub until it gains sufficient strength. | Volunteers foster kittens until the animal shelter finds homes. |  |
| DAY21-17 | surface | 표면<br>the top or outside part of something | 수학 / 점, 선, 면, 각 (Basic Geometric Objects)<br>contextual<br>입체 물체의 바깥 표면을 여러 평면으로 구분해 표시한다 | The pond surface stayed perfectly still at quiet dawn. | Students mark each flat surface on a cardboard cube. | Water droplets rest on the waxy surface of lotus leaves. | We marked each flat surface of the wooden block with chalk. |  |
| DAY21-18 | engage | 참여시키다<br>to get someone interested and involved | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>동물과 식물 장면에서 '참여시키다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The story will engage every child in the room. | Today, the story will engage every child in the room. | Interactive museum exhibits engage young visitors in learning about wildlife conservation. | Our guide will engage every visitor by asking simple questions. |  |
| DAY21-19 | location | 위치<br>the place where something is | 사회 / 지리적 위치 (Geographic Location)<br>direct<br>위도와 경도 또는 주변 지형을 이용해 장소의 위치를 설명한다 | A hidden location behind the rocks held bird nests. | Students describe the location north of the river and hills. | Latitude coordinates identify the precise geographic location of the wildlife sanctuary. | This tree's location is south of the pond and near the fence. |  |
| DAY21-20 | store | 저장하다<br>to keep something for later use | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>동물과 식물 장면에서 '저장하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Squirrels store nuts inside a hollow tree each autumn. | Today, squirrels store nuts inside a hollow tree each autumn. | Cactus plants store precious water inside their thick green fleshy stems. | Squirrels store acorns inside tree holes before winter arrives. |  |

## DAY 21 · set 3 · 자연&환경 / 동물과 식물

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY21-21 | attract | 끌어당기다<br>to make something come closer to you | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>동물과 식물 장면에서 '끌어당기다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Bright flowers attract many busy bees each warm morning. | Today, bright flowers attract many busy bees each warm morning. | Bright colorful flower petals attract honeybees for essential spring pollen dispersal. | Bright petals attract bees toward the center of the flower. |  |
| DAY21-22 | poison | 독<br>something that is not safe to eat | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>동물과 식물 장면에서 '독'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The bright frog's skin holds a natural poison inside. | A locked cabinet keeps each poison safely away from children. | Certain venomous tree snakes use lethal poison for hunting small prey. | Some mushrooms carry poison, so we never pick them. |  |
| DAY21-23 | weigh | 무게가 나가다<br>to show how heavy something is | 과학 / 중력, 질량, 무게 (Gravity, Mass, and Weight)<br>direct<br>질량과 무게를 구별하고 중력이 물체를 아래로 당기는 힘을 잰다 | These melons weigh more than the small green apples. | Students weigh two objects and compare mass with measured weight. | Students weigh the dry soil sample using a digital laboratory balance. | These two apples weigh the same on the kitchen scale. |  |
| DAY21-24 | automatic | 자동의<br>working by itself without a person | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>동물과 식물 장면에서 '자동의'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | An automatic gate opened when the truck came close. | When the truck came close, an automatic gate opened. | The greenhouse features an automatic irrigation system to water potted plants. | This automatic feeder drops seeds for the birds each morning. |  |
| DAY21-25 | role | 역할<br>the part someone plays in a group | 사회 / 사회적 지위와 사회적 역할 (Social Status and Social Role)<br>direct<br>한 사람이 가진 지위와 그에 기대되는 역할을 구별한다 | Each ant has a special role inside the busy nest. | Her role as captain includes leading practice and hearing teammates. | Earthworms play an important ecological role in enriching nutrient farm soil. | The club leader's role includes watering plants every Friday. |  |
| DAY21-26 | exchange | 교환하다<br>to give one thing and get another | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>동물과 식물 장면에서 '교환하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The children exchange colorful stickers during the lunch break. | Several children exchange colorful stickers during the lunch break. | Plants and animals exchange oxygen and carbon dioxide through natural respiration. | Two gardeners exchange tomato seeds for pumpkin seeds today. |  |
| DAY21-27 | contain | 담고 있다<br>to hold something inside | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>동물과 식물 장면에서 '담고 있다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | This basket can contain twelve round brown eggs. | Today, this basket can contain twelve round brown eggs. | Ripe oranges contain natural citric acid and essential dietary vitamin nutrients. | These small pods contain four dark beans inside them. |  |
| DAY21-28 | trail | 오솔길<br>a rough path through woods or hills | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>동물과 식물 장면에서 '오솔길'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A narrow trail wound upward through the misty forest. | A narrow trail curves upward through the quiet forest. | Hikers walk along the forest trail to view mountain wildflowers. | A narrow trail leads through the pine woods to the pond. |  |
| DAY21-29 | trait | 특성<br>a quality that makes someone special | 과학 / 변이 (Variation)<br>direct<br>같은 종 개체의 서로 다른 형질이 유전과 환경에서 생김을 비교한다 | Kindness is the best trait of the village baker. | Students compare one inherited trait among puppies from the same litter. | Stem height is a measurable physical trait in garden pea plants. | Curly fur is one trait some rabbits inherit from parents. |  |
| DAY21-30 | trial | 시도<br>a test to see if something works | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>동물과 식물 장면에서 '시도'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The first flight trial of the paper glider succeeded. | Today, the first flight trial of the paper glider succeeded. | The scientific trial tested plant growth under three different light conditions. | Our first trial with cold water failed, so we tried warm. |  |

## DAY 21 · set 4 · 자연&환경 / 동물과 식물

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |

| DAY21-34 | bury | 묻다<br>to put something under the ground | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>동물과 식물 장면에서 '묻다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Dogs often bury bones beneath the soft garden soil. | Today, dogs often bury bones beneath the soft garden soil. | Squirrels bury brown oak acorns in soil for winter food storage. | Gardeners bury the flower bulbs under ten centimeters of soil. |  |
| DAY21-35 | stem | 줄기<br>the long part that holds a flower | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>동물과 식물 장면에서 '줄기'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The tulip stem bent gently in the warm breeze. | Today, the tulip stem bent gently in the warm breeze. | Nutrients travel upward through the plant stem to nourish upper leaves. | A thick green stem holds this heavy sunflower head upright. |  |
| DAY21-36 | diameter | 지름<br>the distance straight across a circle | 수학 / 부채꼴과 호 (Sectors and Arcs)<br>direct<br>원의 중심각·반지름·호를 표시하고 부채꼴의 크기를 비교한다 | The pizza had a diameter wider than the plate. | Students draw a diameter, then mark two equal semicircle arcs. | Measure the circle diameter to calculate total circular area accurately today. | We measured the diameter of this circle through its center point. |  |
| DAY21-37 | fascinate | 매혹하다<br>to interest someone very much | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>동물과 식물 장면에서 '매혹하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Sea creatures fascinate the curious young museum visitors. | Today, sea creatures fascinate the curious young museum visitors. | Colorful exotic birds fascinate tourists visiting the tropical island rainforest sanctuary. | Moving leaf insects fascinate the children at the nature center. |  |
| DAY21-38 | furthermore | 게다가<br>also, and in addition | fallback<br>word_fallback<br>한 장면에서 '게다가'의 확정 뜻이 다른 뜻으로 오해되지 않게 직접 보여 준다 | The soup was warm; furthermore, it tasted quite delicious. | Today, the soup was warm; furthermore, it tasted quite delicious. | Exercise builds strength; furthermore, it enhances overall cardiovascular stamina and endurance. | This plant grows quickly; furthermore, it needs very little water. |  |
| DAY21-39 | concentrate | 집중하다<br>to think hard about only one thing | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>동물과 식물 장면에서 '집중하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | You must concentrate while balancing on the narrow beam. | While balancing on the narrow beam, you must concentrate. | Students concentrate on observing live microscopic organisms during biology laboratory class. | Birdwatchers concentrate on one branch and wait without talking. |  |
| DAY21-40 | general | 일반적인<br>true for most people or things | fallback<br>word_fallback<br>한 장면에서 '일반적인'의 확정 뜻이 다른 뜻으로 오해되지 않게 직접 보여 준다 | The general rule is to water plants each morning. | Today, the general rule is to water plants each morning. | The textbook outlines the general characteristics of temperate forest biome regions. | The general rule is that green plants need light. |  |

## DAY 22 · set 1 · 자연&환경 / 자연과 생태계

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY22-01 | ecosystem | 생태계<br>all living things sharing one place together | 과학 / 생물다양성 (Biodiversity)<br>direct<br>한 서식지에 사는 여러 종과 생태계의 다양성을 조사한다 | The pond ecosystem includes frogs, fish, and green plants. | Today, the pond ecosystem includes frogs, fish, and green plants. | Forest biodiversity supports a healthy balanced ecosystem for diverse living organisms. | This pond ecosystem holds frogs, water plants, and many insects. |  |
| DAY22-02 | desert | 사막<br>a very dry land with lots of sand | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>자연과 생태계 장면에서 '사막'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The desert glowed orange under the late evening sun. | Today, the desert glowed orange under the late evening sun. | Succulent plants survive in the dry desert with minimal annual rainfall. | Few plants grow in this desert because rain falls rarely. |  |
| DAY22-03 | creature | 생물<br>any living animal, big or small | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>자연과 생태계 장면에서 '생물'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A strange sea creature glided past the glass window. | One strange sea creature glided past the glass window. | The deep ocean trench is home to an unusual marine creature. | One tiny creature moved slowly under the wet leaf. |  |
| DAY22-04 | mature | 성숙한<br>fully grown and developed | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>자연과 생태계 장면에서 '성숙한'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The mature oak gave shade to the whole yard. | The mature oak offered shade to the whole yard. | The mature oak tree provides shelter for many nesting woodland birds. | A mature oak tree produces many acorns every autumn season. |  |
| DAY22-05 | habitat | 서식지<br>the natural home of a plant or animal | 과학 / 생물다양성 보전 (Biodiversity Conservation)<br>direct<br>서식지 손실과 멸종 위험을 줄이는 보호 방법을 비교한다 | The panda habitat is a cool bamboo mountain forest. | Fences protect the wetland habitat from nearby construction. | Pollution threatens the natural wetland habitat of migratory water birds worldwide. | Protecting this wetland habitat keeps rare frogs from disappearing. |  |
| DAY22-06 | maintain | 유지하다<br>to keep something in good condition | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>자연과 생태계 장면에서 '유지하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Gardeners maintain the park paths through every busy season. | Today, gardeners maintain the park paths through every busy season. | Predators help maintain ecological equilibrium among grazing herbivore populations in savannah. | Rangers maintain the forest paths so plants stay undamaged. |  |
| DAY22-07 | tide | 조수<br>the daily rise and fall of the sea | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>자연과 생태계 장면에서 '조수'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The tide left tiny shells along the wet shore. | Today, the tide left tiny shells along the wet shore. | The high tide brought ocean waves crashing against coastal rocky cliffs. | The tide rises twice each day along this rocky shore. |  |
| DAY22-08 | balance | 균형<br>a steady state where nothing tips over | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>자연과 생태계 장면에서 '균형'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The heron kept perfect balance on one thin leg. | Today, the heron kept perfect balance on one thin leg. | Nature maintains a delicate balance between predator and prey species populations. | The seesaw found its balance when both children sat still. |  |
| DAY22-09 | grand | 웅장한<br>very large and impressive | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>자연과 생태계 장면에서 '웅장한'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A grand waterfall thundered into the deep green pool. | One grand waterfall thundered into the deep green pool. | Visitors admired the grand mountain vista from the scenic cliff overlook. | A grand waterfall drops down the cliff into the river. |  |
| DAY22-10 | grant | 주다<br>to give something to someone officially | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>자연과 생태계 장면에서 '주다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The town will grant land for a new garden. | Today, the town will grant land for a new garden. | The park authorities will grant permission for our field biology survey. | The city will grant our club money for tree planting. |  |

|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY22-11 | huge | 거대한<br>very, very big | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>자연과 생태계 장면에서 '거대한'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A huge pumpkin sat proudly in the autumn field. | One huge pumpkin sat proudly in the autumn field. | A huge blue whale surfaced to breathe in chilly ocean waters. | One huge whale swam beside our small watching boat. |  |
| DAY22-12 | pile | 더미<br>many things stacked on top of each other | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>자연과 생태계 장면에서 '더미'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A pile of golden leaves grew beside the path. | A pile of yellow leaves grew next to the path. | Workers stacked a neat pile of dry firewood near the cabin. | A pile of dry leaves grew beside the garden wall. |  |
| DAY22-13 | react | 반응하다<br>to act because of something that happened | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>자연과 생태계 장면에서 '반응하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Sunflowers react to sunlight by turning their bright heads. | Today, sunflowers react to sunlight by turning their bright heads. | Plant leaves react to sunlight by turning slowly toward the window. | Sunflowers react to sunlight by turning their heads slowly. |  |
| DAY22-14 | exist | 존재하다<br>to be real and alive somewhere | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>자연과 생태계 장면에서 '존재하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Deep sea creatures exist far below the ocean waves. | Today, deep sea creatures exist far below the ocean waves. | Countless microscopic bacterial organisms exist within rich fertile forest soil layers. | Some deep sea fish exist far below the sunlight zone. |  |
| DAY22-15 | shade | 그늘<br>a cool dark place out of sunlight | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>자연과 생태계 장면에서 '그늘'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The cows rested in the shade of the tree. | Today, the cows rested in the shade of the tree. | Hikers rested under the cooling shade of a tall pine tree. | Cows rested in the shade under the wide chestnut tree. |  |
| DAY22-16 | element | 요소<br>one basic part of something bigger | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>자연과 생태계 장면에서 '요소'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Water is an important element of every healthy garden. | Today, water is an important element of every healthy garden. | Carbon is a fundamental chemical element in all organic living molecules. | Clean water is one important element of every healthy wetland. |  |
| DAY22-17 | layer | 층<br>one flat level lying over another | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>자연과 생태계 장면에서 '층'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A thin layer of snow covered the sleeping garden. | One thin layer of snow covered the sleeping garden. | Fallen leaves form a thick protective layer over garden soil beds. | A thin layer of moss covers the north side rocks. |  |
| DAY22-18 | horizon | 수평선<br>the line where sky meets land or sea | 과학 / 천체의 겉보기 운동 (Apparent Motion of Celestial Objects)<br>contextual<br>천체가 지평선에서 떠서 이동해 보이는 방향을 시간별로 기록한다 | The sun sank slowly below the far blue horizon. | Students record the Sun rising above the eastern horizon. | The sun dipped below the distant ocean horizon at twilight hour. | The sun rose above the eastern horizon at six o'clock. |  |
| DAY22-19 | observe | 관찰하다<br>to watch something closely and carefully | 과학 / 과학적 탐구 (Scientific Inquiry)<br>direct<br>관찰 질문을 세우고 한 조건만 바꾼 실험의 자료와 결론을 기록한다 | Students observe tiny insects using a strong magnifying glass. | Students observe plant growth after changing only the light. | Scientists observe animal behavior in natural habitats to record daily habits. | We observe the pond each morning and record the water level. |  |
| DAY22-20 | block | 막다<br>to stop something from getting through | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>자연과 생태계 장면에서 '막다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Tall trees block the strong wind near the house. | Tall trees block the strong wind close to the house. | Tall mountain ridges block cold polar winds from entering the valley. | Tall reeds block the wind along the edge of the marsh. |  |

| DAY22-22 | herd | 무리<br>a large group of big animals | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>자연과 생태계 장면에서 '무리'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A herd of deer crossed the wide quiet meadow. | One herd of deer crossed the wide quiet meadow. | A large herd of wild zebras grazed across grassy plains. | A herd of wild horses crossed the open grassland. |  |
| DAY22-23 | filter | 거르다<br>to remove unwanted material from something | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>자연과 생태계 장면에서 '거르다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The cloth will filter sand from the cloudy water. | Today, the cloth will filter sand from the cloudy water. | Mangrove roots filter sediment from coastal water to protect coral reefs. | Wetland plants filter mud from the water before it flows on. |  |
| DAY22-24 | eco-friendly | 친환경적인<br>kind to the earth and nature | 과학 / 지속 가능한 발전 (Sustainable Development)<br>direct<br>자원 사용의 환경·사회 영향을 함께 비교해 오래 지속할 방법을 고른다 | The eco-friendly bag was made from old cotton cloth. | The eco-friendly plan saves water and reduces plastic waste. | Solar power is an eco-friendly energy choice that reduces carbon emissions. | Cloth bags are eco-friendly because we reuse them many times. |  |
| DAY22-25 | portion | 부분<br>one part of a whole thing | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>자연과 생태계 장면에서 '부분'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A small portion of the field held young carrots. | One small portion of the field held young carrots. | A large portion of the ancient rainforest was declared a sanctuary. | Only a small portion of this field stays wet all year. |  |
| DAY22-26 | deadly | 치명적인<br>able to cause very serious harm | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>자연과 생태계 장면에서 '치명적인'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | In the board game, that square is deadly for pieces. | Today, in the board game, that square is deadly for pieces. | Certain mushroom species contain deadly toxins that cause severe organ failure. | Oil spills are deadly for seabirds, so cleanup starts quickly. |  |
| DAY22-27 | tension | 긴장<br>a tight feeling in something pulled | 과학 / 힘의 평형 (Force Equilibrium)<br>contextual<br>팽팽한 줄이 양쪽 물체를 당기는 힘의 방향과 크기를 표시한다 | The rope tension held the tent firmly against wind. | Equal tension pulls both ends of the balanced sign. | Tension in the rope increases when two teams pull in opposite. | The rope tension pulls both climbers toward the middle equally. |  |
| DAY22-28 | migrate | 이동하다<br>to travel to another place each year | 사회 / 이주와 문화 혼종성 (Migration and Cultural Hybridity)<br>direct<br>사람이 다른 지역으로 이주하며 문화와 정체성이 섞이는 사례를 살핀다 | Geese migrate south before the first cold snow falls. | Families migrate to cities and blend old foods with new ones. | Geese migrate south across continents to find warmer winter feeding grounds. | Families who migrate bring recipes that mix with local food. |  |
| DAY22-29 | access | 접근<br>a way to get into a place | 사회 / 지리적 스케일과 공간적 상호작용 (Geographic Scale and Spatial Interaction)<br>contextual<br>거리와 교통망에 따라 학교·병원 같은 장소에 접근하기 쉬운 정도를 비교한다 | The garden access is through the small blue gate. | New bus routes improve hospital access for distant villages. | Urban public transportation provides convenient access to city parks and hospitals. | Villages near the new road gained faster access to the hospital. |  |
| DAY22-30 | excess | 초과<br>more than what is needed | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>자연과 생태계 장면에서 '초과'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The excess water drained out of the flower pot. | Today, the excess water drained out of the flower pot. | Drain the excess rainwater from plant pots to prevent root rot. | We poured the excess water back into the garden barrel. |  |

| DAY22-35 | consist | 이루어져 있다<br>to be made of certain parts | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>자연과 생태계 장면에서 '이루어져 있다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Healthy meals consist of fruits, grains, and fresh vegetables. | Today, healthy meals consist of fruits, grains, and fresh vegetables. | Sand dunes consist of billions of tiny quartz mineral rock grains. | These sand samples consist of tiny shells and broken rock. |  |

|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY23-01 | climate | 기후<br>the usual weather in one place | 사회 / 자연환경과 인문환경 (Physical and Human Environments)<br>direct<br>한 지역의 장기간 기후가 생활과 토지 이용에 미치는 영향을 비교한다 | The island climate stays warm through the whole year. | A dry climate leads farmers to grow crops needing little water. | The Mediterranean climate features mild wet winters and dry sunny summers. | This warm climate lets farmers grow two rice crops yearly. |  |
| DAY23-02 | landscape | 풍경<br>the wide view of land around you | 사회 / 자연환경과 인문환경 (Physical and Human Environments)<br>direct<br>자연환경과 사람이 만든 토지 이용·정착 모습을 한 지역에서 비교한다 | The green landscape stretched far beyond the low hills. | The landscape combines natural hills with farms and roads. | Rolling green hills create a breathtaking natural landscape in the countryside. | The landscape here mixes rice fields with low green hills. |  |
| DAY23-03 | storm | 폭풍<br>weather with strong wind and heavy rain | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>기후와 지리 장면에서 '폭풍'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A summer storm rolled across the wide open sea. | One summer storm rolled across the wide open sea. | The violent coastal storm produced massive ocean waves and gale winds. | The evening storm bent thin branches and soaked the road. |  |
| DAY23-04 | average | 평균의<br>in the middle, not high or low | 수학 / 통계적 탐구 (Statistical Investigation)<br>contextual<br>모은 자료에서 평균을 구하고 전체 값을 근거 있게 추정한다 | The average of four and eight is exactly six. | Today, the average of four and eight is exactly six. | The average annual rainfall in the desert region is extremely low. | We added seven days and found the average rainfall was ninety millimeters. |  |
| DAY23-05 | drown | 물에 빠지다<br>to sink completely under water | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>기후와 지리 장면에서 '물에 빠지다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A lifeguard prevents the swimmer from beginning to drown. | One lifeguard prevents the swimmer from beginning to drown. | Life guards rescue swimmers who might drown in rough ocean surf. | Wear a life jacket so nobody can drown in deep water. |  |
| DAY23-06 | happen | 일어나다<br>to take place or occur | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>기후와 지리 장면에서 '일어나다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Wonderful things happen when everyone helps in the garden. | When everyone helps in the garden, wonderful things happen. | Unpredictable volcanic eruptions can happen without long advance warning in islands. | Strong winds often happen here during the late autumn months. |  |
| DAY23-07 | rough | 거친<br>not smooth, with a bumpy surface | 과학 / 마찰력 (Friction)<br>contextual<br>표면의 거칠기와 마찰이 미끄러지는 움직임에 주는 영향을 비교한다 | The rough tree bark felt scratchy under her fingers. | The block moves slowly on rough wood because friction is stronger. | The rough sandpaper surface creates high friction against moving wooden blocks. | A rough stone path slows the cart more than smooth tiles. |  |
| DAY23-08 | geography | 지리<br>the study of lands, seas, and places | 사회 / 지리 정보와 지도 (Geographic Information and Maps)<br>direct<br>지도 축척·방향·기호와 위치 자료를 이용해 장소를 찾는다 | Geography class explored rivers and mountains on a globe. | Students use scale, direction, and symbols to study geography. | Physical geography examines mountain ranges, river basins, and global climatic zones. | In geography we used the map scale to measure real distance. |  |

| DAY23-10 | affect | 영향을 주다<br>to make a change to something | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>기후와 지리 장면에서 '영향을 주다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Cold nights affect how quickly the flowers open up. | Cold nights affect how rapidly the flowers open up. | Rising ocean temperatures affect migratory patterns of marine fish species significantly. | Mountains affect how much rain reaches the eastern villages. |  |

## DAY 24 · set 1 · 자연&환경 / 자원과 에너지

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY24-01 | resource | 자원<br>something useful that people can use | 과학 / 지속 가능한 발전 (Sustainable Development)<br>direct<br>자원 사용의 환경·사회 영향을 함께 비교해 오래 지속할 방법을 고른다 | Clean water is the most important resource for villages. | Villages compare each resource by use, waste, and renewal. | Solar radiation is an abundant clean energy resource for our planet. | Rain water is one resource we save for the school garden. |  |
| DAY24-02 | fossil | 화석<br>the shape of an old animal in stone | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>자원과 에너지 장면에서 '화석'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A shell fossil sat inside the smooth gray rock. | A shell fossil sat within the smooth gray rock. | Paleontologists discovered a fossil ammonite shell embedded inside sedimentary limestone rock. | This flat stone shows the fossil of an ancient fish. |  |
| DAY24-03 | supply | 공급하다<br>to give people what they need | 사회 / 산업 구조와 글로벌 가치 사슬 (Industrial Structure and Global Value Chains)<br>direct<br>원료 생산부터 제조·운송·판매까지 산업 단계와 부가가치를 잇는다 | The giant wind turbine can supply steady power on windy autumn days. | Farms supply cotton that factories turn into shirts for stores. | Pipelines supply clean fresh water from mountain reservoirs to city homes. | Local farms supply beans to the factory that makes tofu. |  |

| DAY24-07 | material | 재료<br>the stuff something is made from | 과학 / 비열 (Specific Heat)<br>contextual<br>같은 질량의 서로 다른 물질에 같은 열을 주고 온도 변화를 비교한다 | This soft material came from a plant called cotton. | Each material warms differently under the same lamp. | Copper is an excellent conductive material for making electrical power cables. | Each material warmed at a different speed under the same lamp. |  |

| DAY24-09 | metal | 금속<br>a hard shiny material like iron | 과학 / 열전도 (Conduction)<br>direct<br>금속 막대의 한쪽을 데우고 열이 고체를 따라 이동하는 순서를 관찰한다 | The metal spoon felt cold inside the warm soup. | Heat travels along the metal spoon toward its handle. | Heat conducts rapidly through the solid metal rod when heated flame. | The far end of this metal bar warmed after two minutes. |  |

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY25-01 | environment | 환경<br>the natural world around living things | 사회 / 자연환경과 인문환경 (Physical and Human Environments)<br>direct<br>자연환경과 사람이 만든 토지 이용·정착 모습을 한 지역에서 비교한다 | Clean rivers keep the forest environment healthy and green. | The environment includes natural rivers and human roads across the valley. | Human industrial activities transform the natural environment across entire regional landscapes. | Around this bay the environment mixes wide mudflats with new houses. |  |
| DAY25-02 | release | 놓아주다<br>to let something go free | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>환경 문제 장면에서 '놓아주다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The children release the butterfly into the sunny garden. | Several children release the butterfly into the sunny garden. | Wildlife veterinarians release the rehabilitated eagle back into the mountain forest. | Volunteers release the healed turtle back into the warm sea. |  |
| DAY25-03 | disaster | 재난<br>a very bad event that causes problems | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>환경 문제 장면에서 '재난'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The spilled paint was a small kitchen disaster. | Today, the spilled paint was a small kitchen disaster. | The devastating flood disaster damaged hundreds of homes along the river. | The flood was a disaster that closed roads for weeks. |  |
| DAY25-04 | destroy | 파괴하다<br>to break something completely | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>환경 문제 장면에서 '파괴하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Strong waves can destroy a sandcastle in one minute. | Today, strong waves can destroy a sandcastle in one minute. | Wildfires can destroy thousands of hectares of pine forest within days. | Careless fires destroy the grass that holds this soil together. |  |
| DAY25-05 | waste | 낭비하다<br>to use something without any good reason | 과학 / 지속 가능한 발전 (Sustainable Development)<br>contextual<br>자원을 보존하고 불필요한 낭비를 줄이는 방법의 환경 영향을 비교한다 | Please turn off the lights so we do not waste electricity. | Kindly turn off the lights so we do not waste electricity. | Turn off running water taps so you do not waste resources. | Do not waste paper; print on both sides each time. |  |
| DAY25-06 | period | 기간<br>a length of time | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>환경 문제 장면에서 '기간'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The rainy period lasted for three whole cool weeks. | Today, the rainy period lasted for three whole cool weeks. | The region experienced a prolonged dry period with zero rainfall months. | During this dry period the well gave very little water. |  |
| DAY25-07 | announce | 발표하다<br>to tell news to many people | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>환경 문제 장면에서 '발표하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The teacher will announce the winner after the contest. | An instructor will announce the winner after the contest. | Government officials will announce new environmental protection policies on national television. | The city will announce new recycling rules next Monday. |  |
| DAY25-08 | occur | 발생하다<br>to happen, often without warning | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>환경 문제 장면에서 '발생하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Rainbows occur when sunlight shines through falling rain drops. | When sunlight shines through falling rain drops, rainbows occur. | Severe oceanic storm surges occur during high autumn king tide cycles. | Small landslides occur here after several days of heavy rain. |  |
| DAY25-09 | quality | 품질<br>how good or bad something is | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>환경 문제 장면에서 '품질'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The quality of this wool blanket is truly excellent. | Today, the quality of this wool blanket is truly excellent. | Inspectors test municipal tap water quality to ensure safe drinking standards. | The air quality improved after the factory changed its filters. |  |
| DAY25-10 | quantity | 양<br>how much or how many there are | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>환경 문제 장면에서 '양'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A large quantity of apples filled the wooden crates. | One large quantity of apples filled the wooden crates. | Factories monitor the quantity of industrial wastewater discharged into local rivers. | A large quantity of plastic bottles filled the collection bin. |  |

## DAY 25 · set 2 · 자연&환경 / 환경 문제

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY25-11 | protect | 보호하다<br>to keep something safe from trouble | 과학 / 생물다양성 보전 (Biodiversity Conservation)<br>direct<br>서식지 손실과 멸종 위험을 줄이는 보호 방법을 비교한다 | Tall fences protect the young trees from strong wind. | Park fences protect rare birds by keeping their habitat quiet. | Strict national laws protect endangered wildlife species from illegal poaching activities. | Fences protect the nesting beach so fewer turtle eggs disappear. |  |
| DAY25-12 | loss | 손실<br>when something you had is gone | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>환경 문제 장면에서 '손실'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The loss of one glove made the walk chilly. | Today, the loss of one glove made the walk chilly. | Deforestation causes the loss of critical habitat for rare rainforest animals. | The loss of old trees left this hillside bare. |  |
| DAY25-13 | generate | 발생시키다<br>to make power or something new | 과학 / 재생 에너지 (Renewable Energy)<br>contextual<br>태양빛을 이용해 전기를 발생시키는 장치의 에너지 변환을 확인한다 | Windmills generate clean power for the small seaside town. | Solar panels generate electricity by changing energy from sunlight. | Solar photovoltaic panels generate clean electrical power directly from bright sunlight. | These panels generate electricity whenever bright sunlight hits them. |  |
| DAY25-14 | amazed | 놀란<br>very surprised in a happy way | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>환경 문제 장면에서 '놀란'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The visitors looked amazed at the glowing blue cave. | Today, the visitors looked amazed at the glowing blue cave. | Visitors were amazed by the breathtaking beauty of the coral reef. | Students looked amazed when the cleaned stream showed small fish. |  |
| DAY25-15 | harm | 해를 끼치다<br>to hurt or damage something | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>환경 문제 장면에서 '해를 끼치다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Litter can harm the small fish in ponds. | Today, litter can harm the small fish in ponds. | Chemical pollutants cause serious harm to aquatic life in freshwater lakes. | Plastic rings can harm birds, so we cut them first. |  |
| DAY25-16 | intense | 강렬한<br>very strong or very great | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>환경 문제 장면에서 '강렬한'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Intense sunlight made the beach sand feel hot. | Today, intense sunlight made the beach sand feel hot. | Summer afternoon brings intense heat to the asphalt streets of city. | Intense afternoon sunlight dried the young rice plants quickly. |  |
| DAY25-17 | amount | 양<br>how much of something there is | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>환경 문제 장면에서 '양'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A tiny amount of salt improved the whole soup. | A small amount of salt improved the whole soup. | Recycling reduces the massive amount of garbage sent to local landfills. | We measured the amount of paper our class recycled weekly. |  |
| DAY25-18 | marine | 해양의<br>about the sea and its animals | 사회 / 태평양 지역의 환경 문제 (Pacific Environmental Challenges)<br>contextual<br>해수면 상승과 해양 오염이 태평양 섬과 생태계에 주는 영향을 살핀다 | Marine turtles swim slowly through the warm blue water. | Plastic waste harms marine animals around small Pacific islands. | Plastic debris poses a severe threat to delicate marine ecosystem species. | Rising seas and plastic threaten marine life around small Pacific islands. |  |
| DAY25-19 | permanent | 영구적인<br>lasting forever without changing | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>환경 문제 장면에서 '영구적인'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The stone bridge became a permanent part of town. | Today, the stone bridge became a permanent part of town. | Habitat destruction causes permanent loss of biological diversity in fragile wetlands. | The new stone wall is permanent, unlike the summer sandbags. |  |
| DAY25-20 | fine | 벌금<br>money paid as punishment for breaking a rule | 사회 / 법, 도덕과 법치주의 (Law, Morality, and the Rule of Law)<br>direct<br>법적 의무와 도덕적 의무를 구별하고 모든 사람에게 같은 절차를 적용한다 | The driver receives a fine for entering the protected path. | The same legal rule gives each driver an equal fine. | Littering in public parks results in a steep monetary fine penalty. | Anyone who dumps trash here pays the same fine. |  |

| DAY25-25 | refreshing | 상쾌한<br>making you feel cool and new again | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>환경 문제 장면에서 '상쾌한'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A refreshing breeze cooled the hikers on the ridge. | One refreshing breeze cooled the hikers on the ridge. | A cool breeze felt very refreshing after a hot afternoon walk. | The cool morning air felt refreshing after the long climb. |  |
| DAY25-26 | responsible | 책임감 있는<br>careful about doing what you should do | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>환경 문제 장면에서 '책임감 있는'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A responsible camper puts out the campfire completely. | A responsible camper placeds out the campfire completely. | Every responsible citizen sorts recyclable paper, glass, and plastic into bins. | Responsible campers carry every wrapper home in their backpacks. |  |
| DAY25-27 | confuse | 혼동하다<br>to mix up two things by mistake | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>환경 문제 장면에서 '혼동하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | People often confuse these two very similar leaves. | Today, people often confuse these two very similar leaves. | Do not confuse biodegradable organic waste with non-recyclable synthetic plastic packaging. | People often confuse paper cups with plastic ones while sorting. |  |
| DAY25-28 | separate | 분리하다<br>to put things into different groups | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>환경 문제 장면에서 '분리하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Please separate the paper from the glass before recycling. | Kindly separate the paper from the glass before recycling. | Residents separate household trash into designated recycling bins outside their homes. | We separate glass, cans, and paper into three labeled boxes. |  |
| DAY25-29 | delicate | 섬세한<br>thin and easy to break | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>환경 문제 장면에서 '섬세한'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The delicate spider web sparkled with morning dew drops. | Today, the delicate spider web sparkled with morning dew drops. | Coral reefs are delicate marine structures vulnerable to rising ocean temperatures. | These delicate moss plants tear easily under heavy boots. |  |
| DAY25-30 | dedicate | 바치다<br>to give your time to something important | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>환경 문제 장면에서 '바치다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Volunteers dedicate every weekend to cleaning the river bank. | Today, volunteers dedicate every weekend to cleaning the river bank. | Environmental activists dedicate their lives to protecting ancient old-growth temperate forests. | Our club members dedicate every Saturday morning to river cleaning. |  |

## DAY 26 · set 1 · 사회 / 법과 사회

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY26-01 | legal | 법적인<br>allowed by the rules of a country | 사회 / 법, 도덕과 법치주의 (Law, Morality, and the Rule of Law)<br>direct<br>법적 의무와 도덕적 의무를 구별하고 모든 사람에게 같은 절차를 적용한다 | Fishing here is legal only during the warm months. | Both sides follow the same legal steps during the fair trial. | Every citizen has a legal duty to follow established traffic safety rules. | The judge applies the same legal steps to both sides fairly. |  |
| DAY26-02 | obey | 따르다<br>to do what a rule tells you | 사회 / 법, 도덕과 법치주의 (Law, Morality, and the Rule of Law)<br>direct<br>법적 의무와 도덕적 의무를 구별하고 모든 사람에게 같은 절차를 적용한다 | Good drivers obey every sign along the mountain road. | Drivers obey the law, while kindness remains a moral duty. | Responsible drivers obey traffic signals to protect pedestrians crossing streets safely. | Every driver must obey the same traffic light rules. |  |
| DAY26-03 | arrest | 체포하다<br>to take someone to answer for a rule | 사회 / 민사재판과 형사재판 (Civil and Criminal Trials)<br>direct<br>민사와 형사 사건에서 당사자·목적·결과가 어떻게 다른지 비교한다 | In the board game, guards arrest the sneaky fox piece. | In the courtroom story, police arrest a suspect after finding evidence. | Police officers arrest the criminal suspect based on solid forensic evidence. | In this story, officers arrest the fox who took the pies. |  |
| DAY26-04 | report | 보고하다<br>to tell people about something that happened | 국어 / 정보 전달 글쓰기 (Informative writing)<br>direct<br>독자가 절차나 사실을 이해하도록 정확한 정보를 순서 있게 쓴다 | Please report the lost umbrella at the front desk. | Students report each experiment step and result in clear order. | Journalists report factual news events accurately in the evening newspaper article. | We report each step of the class vote in order. |  |
| DAY26-05 | innocent | 무죄의<br>not having done anything wrong | 사회 / 민사재판과 형사재판 (Civil and Criminal Trials)<br>direct<br>민사와 형사 사건에서 당사자·목적·결과가 어떻게 다른지 비교한다 | The innocent puppy was not the cookie thief here. | Evidence shows the suspect is innocent during the criminal trial. | The jury found the innocent defendant not guilty of the crime. | The court found the shopkeeper innocent after hearing every witness. |  |
| DAY26-06 | caution | 주의<br>great care to stay safe | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>법과 사회 장면에서 '주의'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Walk with caution near the slippery mossy stone steps. | Walk with caution close to the slippery mossy stone steps. | Pedestrians should exercise caution when crossing icy roads in winter mornings. | Workers use caution when carrying glass across the wet floor. |  |
| DAY26-07 | charge | 요금을 청구하다, 충전하다<br>to ask for money as a price, or supply electric power | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>법과 사회 장면에서 '요금을 청구하다, 충전하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Remember to charge your tablet battery before the science class starts. | The campsite will charge each visitor five dollars per night. | Electric vehicle owners charge their car battery at local charging stations. | Bus companies charge less money for students on weekends. |  |
| DAY26-08 | violate | 위반하다<br>to break a rule or promise | 사회 / 법, 도덕과 법치주의 (Law, Morality, and the Rule of Law)<br>direct<br>법적 의무와 도덕적 의무를 구별하고 모든 사람에게 같은 절차를 적용한다 | Players who violate the game rules lose one turn. | Players violate a written rule and receive the same penalty. | Drivers who violate speed limits receive a monetary fine from police. | Riders who violate the helmet rule receive the same warning. |  |
| DAY26-09 | law | 법<br>a rule everyone in a country follows | 사회 / 법, 도덕과 법치주의 (Law, Morality, and the Rule of Law)<br>direct<br>법적 의무와 도덕적 의무를 구별하고 모든 사람에게 같은 절차를 적용한다 | A new law protects the old trees in parks. | A law requires drivers to stop, while kindness is moral. | Democratic society relies on the fair rule of constitutional public law. | This law protects every child's right to attend school. |  |
| DAY26-10 | raw | 날것의<br>not cooked at all | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>법과 사회 장면에서 '날것의'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Raw carrots taste crunchy and sweet in a salad. | Today, raw carrots taste crunchy and sweet in a salad. | Eating raw meat can cause severe food poisoning from harmful bacteria. | Wash the raw carrots before you put them in salad. |  |

## DAY 26 · set 2 · 사회 / 법과 사회

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY26-11 | search | 찾다<br>to look carefully for something | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>법과 사회 장면에서 '찾다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The children search the garden for the hidden key. | Several children search the garden for the hidden key. | Detectives search the room carefully to find important missing evidence documents. | Librarians search the shelves for the missing history book. |  |
| DAY26-12 | raise | 올리다<br>to lift something to a higher place | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>법과 사회 장면에서 '올리다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Sailors raise the bright flag every sunny morning. | Sailors raise the colorful flag every sunny morning. | Students raise their hands politely before asking questions during class discussions. | Please raise your hand before answering the guest speaker. |  |
| DAY26-13 | aim | 겨누다<br>to point something toward a target | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>법과 사회 장면에서 '겨누다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The archer will aim the arrow at the target. | Today, the archer will aim the arrow at the target. | Archers aim at the center target circle before releasing their arrows. | Players aim the beanbag toward the center of the target. |  |
| DAY26-14 | criminal | 범죄자<br>someone in stories who breaks the law | 사회 / 민사재판과 형사재판 (Civil and Criminal Trials)<br>direct<br>민사와 형사 사건에서 당사자·목적·결과가 어떻게 다른지 비교한다 | The storybook criminal was a very clumsy cartoon raccoon. | A criminal trial examines whether the suspect broke a law. | The convicted criminal received a fair sentence from the presiding judge. | In the detective story, the criminal returned the stolen painting. |  |
| DAY26-15 | suspect | 용의자<br>a person who might have done something | 사회 / 민사재판과 형사재판 (Civil and Criminal Trials)<br>direct<br>민사와 형사 사건에서 당사자·목적·결과가 어떻게 다른지 비교한다 | The main suspect in the mystery was a sleepy cat. | The suspect hears the evidence during a criminal trial. | The police questioned the main suspect about his whereabouts that evening. | The suspect explained where the missing bicycle had been parked. |  |
| DAY26-16 | neutral | 중립의<br>not choosing either of two sides | 사회 / 사법부 독립과 상소 (Judicial Independence and Appeals)<br>contextual<br>재판관이 외부 압력 없이 법과 증거로 판단하고 상소로 오류를 검토한다 | The referee stayed neutral during the friendly ball game. | The neutral judge follows evidence despite outside pressure. | Judges maintain a neutral attitude while evaluating evidence presented in court. | Court judges stay neutral, so outside pressure cannot change decisions. |  |
| DAY26-17 | accuse | 비난하다<br>to say someone did something wrong | 사회 / 민사재판과 형사재판 (Civil and Criminal Trials)<br>direct<br>민사와 형사 사건에서 당사자·목적·결과가 어떻게 다른지 비교한다 | Do not accuse anyone before checking the whole story. | Lawyers accuse the suspect only after presenting evidence in court. | Prosecutors accuse the company of violating environmental clean water regulations openly. | Do not accuse anyone before hearing what really happened. |  |
| DAY26-18 | firm | 단호한<br>strong and not easily moved | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>법과 사회 장면에서 '단호한'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The coach gave firm but kind advice to everyone. | A sports teacher offered firm but kind advice to everyone. | The school principal took a firm stand against any bullying behavior. | The teacher was firm about returning library books on time. |  |
| DAY26-19 | aid | 도움<br>help given to someone who needs it | 사회 / 지속 가능한 발전과 국제 협력 (Sustainable Development and International Cooperation)<br>contextual<br>재난이나 빈곤 문제에 필요한 도움을 여러 기관이 역할을 나누어 제공한다 | Warm blankets arrived as aid for the snowy village. | Today, warm blankets arrived as aid for the snowy village. | International organizations provide emergency disaster relief aid to earthquake affected regions. | Two groups sent aid, so families received blankets and rice. |  |
| DAY26-20 | interest | 관심<br>a wish to know more about something | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>법과 사회 장면에서 '관심'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Her interest in stars grew after the museum visit. | After the museum visit, her interest in stars grew. | The fascinating science exhibit sparked great student interest in astronomy careers. | My interest in old coins started at the school museum. |  |

## DAY 26 · set 3 · 사회 / 법과 사회

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY26-21 | complex | 복잡한<br>having many parts and not simple | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>법과 사회 장면에서 '복잡한'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The complex puzzle took the whole afternoon to finish. | Today, the complex puzzle took the whole afternoon to finish. | Solving the complex math puzzle required logical thinking and persistent effort. | This rule book is complex, so we made a simple summary. |  |
| DAY26-22 | organize | 정리하다<br>to put things in a good order | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>법과 사회 장면에서 '정리하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Let us organize these books by color and size. | Today, let us organize these books by color and size. | Librarians organize returned books neatly on wooden shelves by subject category. | We organize the club papers by date inside three folders. |  |
| DAY26-23 | plenty | 많음<br>more than enough of something | fallback<br>word_fallback<br>한 장면에서 '많음'의 확정 뜻이 다른 뜻으로 오해되지 않게 직접 보여 준다 | There was plenty of soup for every hungry guest. | Today, there was plenty of soup for every hungry guest. | The orchard produced plenty of sweet juicy apples during autumn harvest. | There is plenty of paper left for tomorrow's posters. |  |
| DAY26-24 | phenomenon | 현상<br>something amazing that happens in nature | 과학 / 과학적 탐구 (Scientific Inquiry)<br>contextual<br>자연 현상을 관찰해 질문을 만들고 자료로 가능한 설명을 검토한다 | The northern lights are a beautiful natural phenomenon. | Students observe the phenomenon, ask why, and record possible explanations. | A solar eclipse is an amazing astronomical phenomenon observed from Earth. | We watched this phenomenon and asked why fog forms at dawn. |  |
| DAY26-25 | mount | 오르다<br>to climb up onto something | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>법과 사회 장면에서 '오르다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The rider will mount the gentle brown horse slowly. | The rider will mount the gentle brown horse gently. | Hikers mount the steep rocky trail to reach the scenic summit. | Riders mount the horse from the left side carefully. |  |
| DAY26-26 | found | 설립하다<br>to start a new club or town | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>법과 사회 장면에서 '설립하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | They will found a reading club at the library. | The group will found a reading club at the library. | Civic leaders met to found a new public community health center. | Three students will found a reading club this September. |  |
| DAY26-27 | prove | 증명하다<br>to show that something is really true | 국어 / 설명과 논증 (Explanation and argument)<br>direct<br>원인과 결과 또는 주장과 근거의 연결을 분명히 설명한다 | These footprints prove a deer walked past last night. | These footprints prove a deer moved past last night. | Scientists prove their hypothesis using verified data gathered from controlled experiments. | These photographs prove the wall cracked before the storm arrived. |  |
| DAY26-28 | forbid | 금지하다<br>to say that something must not happen | 사회 / 법, 도덕과 법치주의 (Law, Morality, and the Rule of Law)<br>direct<br>법적 의무와 도덕적 의무를 구별하고 모든 사람에게 같은 절차를 적용한다 | Park rules forbid picking flowers from the garden beds. | Today, park rules forbid picking flowers from the garden beds. | School safety rules forbid running along crowded indoor hallway corridors strictly. | School rules forbid running, and the same rule covers everyone. |  |
| DAY26-29 | contrast | 대조<br>a big difference between two things | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>법과 사회 장면에서 '대조'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The contrast between night and morning colors was striking. | Today, the contrast between night and morning colors was striking. | Bright white snow formed a sharp visual contrast against dark rocks. | The contrast between the two drawings is the sky color. |  |
| DAY26-30 | contract | 계약<br>a written promise between two people | 사회 / 공법, 사법과 사회법 (Public, Private, and Social Law)<br>direct<br>국가와 개인 또는 개인 사이 관계를 보고 적용할 법 영역을 분류한다 | The two friends signed a playful club contract today. | Two neighbors sign a contract for repairing their shared fence. | The two business partners signed a binding written contract for trade. | This contract between two neighbors explains who repairs the fence. |  |
