| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY26-31 | stamp | 우표<br>a small paper you stick on mail | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>법과 사회 장면에서 '우표'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A colorful stamp decorated the corner of the envelope. | One colorful stamp decorated the corner of the envelope. | She attached a colorful postage stamp to the handwritten postal envelope. | One blue stamp sits in the corner of this envelope. |  |
| DAY26-32 | secure | 안전한, 튼튼한<br>safe from danger, firmly fastened, and not likely to fail | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>법과 사회 장면에서 '안전한, 튼튼한'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Make sure the bicycle lock is secure before leaving the rack. | Before leaving the rack, make sure the bicycle lock is secure. | Store your bicycle in a secure lockable garage during stormy nights. | The bicycle stayed secure because we locked it to the rack. |  |
| DAY26-33 | conference | 회의<br>a meeting where people share ideas | 국어 / 토의 (Discussion)<br>direct<br>여러 사람이 문제의 대안을 말하고 공통 결론이나 타협점을 찾는다 | The garden conference brought growers from many small towns. | At the conference, groups compare plans and choose one solution. | Environmental delegates attended an international climate conference to discuss carbon emissions. | At the conference each group suggested one plan, then we agreed. |  |
| DAY26-34 | urge | 촉구하다<br>to strongly ask someone to do something | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>법과 사회 장면에서 '촉구하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Coaches urge the runners to drink water after practice. | After practice, coaches urge the runners to drink water. | Doctors urge parents to ensure their children get adequate daily sleep. | Parents urge the council to add lights near the crossing. |  |
| DAY26-35 | confront | 맞서다<br>to face a problem bravely | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>법과 사회 장면에서 '맞서다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The young climber will confront the hardest wall today. | Now, the young climber will confront the hardest wall today. | Brave leaders confront difficult social problems with honesty and decisive action. | The young hero must confront the difficult puzzle alone. |  |
| DAY26-36 | persuade | 설득하다<br>to make someone agree with your idea | 국어 / 토론과 반박 (Debate and rebuttal)<br>direct<br>주장과 근거를 제시하고 상대 주장에 근거로 반박한다 | The children persuade their friends to join the cleanup. | Students use evidence to persuade classmates to reduce plastic. | Debaters use compelling factual evidence to persuade listeners of their position. | Students persuade the principal by showing two years of safety data. |  |
| DAY26-37 | scan | 훑어보다<br>to look over something quickly | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>법과 사회 장면에서 '훑어보다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Sailors scan the horizon for the first island shape. | Today, sailors scan the horizon for the first island shape. | Students scan the index to locate specific historical topics in textbooks. | Readers scan the notice quickly to find the meeting time. |  |
| DAY26-38 | prohibit | 금지하다<br>to not allow something at all | 사회 / 법, 도덕과 법치주의 (Law, Morality, and the Rule of Law)<br>direct<br>법적 의무와 도덕적 의무를 구별하고 모든 사람에게 같은 절차를 적용한다 | Library rules prohibit loud talking inside the reading room. | Library rules prohibit loud talking within the reading room. | City bylaws prohibit loud construction work during late evening quiet hours. | Park signs prohibit fishing here, and rangers check every visitor. |  |
| DAY26-39 | moreover | 게다가<br>also, and in addition | fallback<br>word_fallback<br>한 장면에서 '게다가'의 확정 뜻이 다른 뜻으로 오해되지 않게 직접 보여 준다 | The soup was tasty; moreover, it warmed everyone quickly. | The soup was tasty; moreover, it warmed everyone rapidly. | Reading expands vocabulary; moreover, it strengthens critical thinking and analytical reasoning. | The new rule is short; moreover, everyone can understand it. |  |
| DAY26-40 | determine | 결정하다<br>to decide something after looking carefully | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>법과 사회 장면에서 '결정하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Judges determine which pumpkin is the roundest and biggest. | Today, judges determine which pumpkin is the roundest and biggest. | Test results determine which students qualify for advanced mathematics study programs. | Judges determine the winner after measuring each paper airplane flight. |  |

## DAY 27 · set 1 · 사회 / 정치와 행정

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY27-01 | politics | 정치<br>the work of leading a town or country | 사회 / 민주주의와 시민성 (Democracy and Citizenship)<br>direct<br>시민이 자유와 평등을 지키며 공공 문제 결정에 참여한다 | Class politics decided who would plan the spring festival. | Citizens discuss politics and vote on a public park plan. | Citizens learn about democratic politics by voting in local community elections. | In politics, ordinary citizens help decide how public money is used. |  |
| DAY27-02 | facility | 시설<br>a building made for one special use | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>정치와 행정 장면에서 '시설'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The new sports facility opened beside the town park. | The new sports facility opened next to the town park. | Our town opened a modern recreational facility for youth sports activities. | Our town built a sports facility with two indoor courts. |  |
| DAY27-03 | vote | 투표하다<br>to choose by raising a hand or paper | 사회 / 자유롭고 공정한 선거 (Free and Fair Elections)<br>direct<br>비밀 투표와 정확한 개표로 유권자의 선택을 공정하게 반영한다 | Students vote for the best name for the garden. | Students vote secretly, and teachers count every ballot accurately. | Voters cast their ballot to vote for city council representatives fairly. | Students vote in secret, then two helpers count every paper. |  |
| DAY27-04 | candidate | 후보자<br>a person hoping to be chosen | 사회 / 자유롭고 공정한 선거 (Free and Fair Elections)<br>direct<br>비밀 투표와 정확한 개표로 유권자의 선택을 공정하게 반영한다 | Each candidate shared one idea for a kinder classroom. | Each candidate presents one idea before the secret vote. | Each election candidate presented clear plans for improving local public schools. | Each candidate spoke for two minutes before the class voted. |  |
| DAY27-05 | civil | 시민의<br>about the people of a town | 사회 / 민주주의와 시민성 (Democracy and Citizenship)<br>direct<br>시민이 자유와 평등을 지키며 공공 문제 결정에 참여한다 | Civil groups planted trees along the wide main street. | Civil groups help citizens join decisions about public problems. | Volunteering for community cleanups is an important civil duty for citizens. | Voting is one civil right that adults use every election. |  |
| DAY27-06 | limit | 제한<br>the largest amount that is allowed | 사회 / 기본권 제한과 권리 구제 (Limitations and Remedies for Rights)<br>contextual<br>공익을 위한 권리 제한의 목적·범위·대안을 비교해 비례성을 따진다 | The boat has a limit of six small passengers. | Officials limit loud events only enough to protect nearby homes. | The speed limit on residential streets is thirty kilometers per hour. | This noise limit protects sleep but still allows daytime concerts. |  |
| DAY27-07 | conservative | 보수적인<br>liking old ways and careful change | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>정치와 행정 장면에서 '보수적인'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The conservative baker keeps the same recipe every year. | Today, the conservative baker keeps the same recipe every year. | The conservative party emphasizes fiscal restraint and preserving traditional cultural values. | Our club stayed conservative and kept the old meeting schedule. |  |
| DAY27-08 | belong | 속하다<br>to be part of a group | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>정치와 행정 장면에서 '속하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | These red boots belong to the smallest team member. | Today, these red boots belong to the smallest team member. | These historical artifacts belong to the national cultural history museum collection. | These two folders belong to the school gardening committee. |  |
| DAY27-09 | official | 공식적인, 공인의<br>agreed to or confirmed by the government or an authority | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>정치와 행정 장면에서 '공식적인, 공인의'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The school principal signed the official certificate with golden seal. | The school principal signed the official certificate with yellow seal. | The mayor issued an official statement regarding the city park renovation. | The official result was posted after both helpers checked it. |  |
| DAY27-10 | officer | 경찰관, 장교, 임원<br>a person who holds a position of authority in organization/police | 사회 / 사회적 지위와 사회적 역할 (Social Status and Social Role)<br>contextual<br>조직에서 직책을 가진 사람이 맡는 권한과 책임을 구별한다 | The friendly police officer helped children cross the busy avenue safely. | The helpful police officer helped children cross the busy avenue safely. | The police officer directed afternoon traffic safely around the road construction. | The club officer keeps the records but cannot change rules alone. |  |

## DAY 27 · set 2 · 사회 / 정치와 행정

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY27-11 | result | 결과<br>what happens because of something else | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>정치와 행정 장면에서 '결과'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The result of the sunny week was ripe berries. | Today, the result of the sunny week was ripe berries. | The final test result reflected the student's diligent daily study efforts. | The vote result showed thirty students preferred the park trip. |  |
| DAY27-12 | survey | 설문 조사<br>questions asked to learn what people think | 사회 / 정치과정과 여론 (Political Process and Public Opinion)<br>direct<br>설문과 토론으로 모인 여론이 정책 의제와 결정에 반영되는 과정을 추적한다 | The class survey showed everyone loves outdoor art days. | A survey shows public opinion about the new bus route. | The city conducted a public survey to gather opinions on parks. | The town survey asked residents which bus stop needs shelter. |  |
| DAY27-13 | notice | 안내문, 공지, 통지<br>information or a warning | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>정치와 행정 장면에서 '안내문, 공지, 통지'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The teacher pinned an important field trip notice on board. | An instructor pinned an important field trip notice on board. | A warning notice was posted near the dangerous steep cliff edge. | A paper notice on the door explains the new opening hours. |  |
| DAY27-14 | guard | 지키다<br>to watch over and keep something safe | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>정치와 행정 장면에서 '지키다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Two friendly dogs guard the sheep in the meadow. | Today, two friendly dogs guard the sheep in the meadow. | Security guards patrol the building perimeter to guard against unauthorized entry. | Two volunteers guard the crosswalk every school morning until eight. |  |
| DAY27-15 | profit | 이익<br>money left after paying all the costs | 사회 / 산업 구조와 글로벌 가치 사슬 (Industrial Structure and Global Value Chains)<br>contextual<br>생산 단계별 비용과 부가가치가 기업의 이익과 경제 활동으로 이어짐을 본다 | The bake sale profit bought new books for everyone. | The factory's profit equals sales money after production costs. | The company donated a portion of its annual profit to charities. | After paying for beans and boxes, the shop kept a small profit. |  |
| DAY27-16 | deny | 부인하다<br>to say something is not true | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>정치와 행정 장면에서 '부인하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The kitten cannot deny the crumbs on its whiskers. | Today, the kitten cannot deny the crumbs on its whiskers. | Honest debaters do not deny facts supported by verified statistical evidence. | The driver did not deny parking beside the fire exit. |  |
| DAY27-17 | opinion | 의견<br>what a person thinks about something | 사회 / 정치과정과 여론 (Political Process and Public Opinion)<br>direct<br>설문과 토론으로 모인 여론이 정책 의제와 결정에 반영되는 과정을 추적한다 | Every opinion was heard during the friendly class meeting. | The council uses each opinion from surveys to shape policy. | Citizens express their personal opinion during open town hall public hearings. | Every written opinion from residents went into the council meeting. |  |
| DAY27-18 | rural | 시골의<br>in the countryside, away from cities | 사회 / 자연환경과 인문환경 (Physical and Human Environments)<br>direct<br>자연환경과 사람이 만든 토지 이용·정착 모습을 한 지역에서 비교한다 | The rural road passed quiet farms and green fields. | A rural landscape has fields, villages, and fewer tall buildings. | Farming is the main economic activity in this peaceful rural village. | This rural area has wide rice fields and few tall buildings. |  |
| DAY27-19 | urban | 도시의<br>in or about a big city | 사회 / 자연환경과 인문환경 (Physical and Human Environments)<br>direct<br>자연환경과 사람이 만든 토지 이용·정착 모습을 한 지역에서 비교한다 | The urban park was busy with children after school. | An urban landscape has dense roads, homes, and offices. | High-density subway systems provide efficient transportation in modern urban centers worldwide. | In urban areas, apartments and shops replace most open farmland. |  |
| DAY27-20 | issue | 문제<br>a problem that people talk about | 사회 / 정치과정과 여론 (Political Process and Public Opinion)<br>contextual<br>사람들이 논의하는 공공 문제가 여론과 정책 의제로 이어지는 과정을 추적한다 | The main issue was where to place the bench. | Citizens discuss the issue before the council sets policy. | Affordable housing is an urgent social issue for growing city populations. | Bus safety became the main issue at last night's town meeting. |  |

## DAY 27 · set 3 · 사회 / 정치와 행정

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY27-21 | simplify | 단순화하다<br>to make something easier to understand | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>정치와 행정 장면에서 '단순화하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The teacher will simplify the long rules for everyone. | An instructor will simplify the long rules for everyone. | Infographics simplify complex economic data into easy visual charts for readers. | We simplify the long form so parents finish it quickly. |  |
| DAY27-22 | committee | 위원회<br>a small group chosen to do work | 사회 / 대의 민주주의와 참여 민주주의 (Representative and Participatory Democracy)<br>contextual<br>선출된 대표와 시민 위원회가 의견을 모으고 결정을 내리는 방식을 비교한다 | The garden committee met beside the old apple tree. | Voters choose a committee, while citizens speak directly at meetings. | The student committee organized a successful charity book donation drive today. | The elected committee listens to residents before choosing the park design. |  |
| DAY27-23 | accompany | 동행하다<br>to go somewhere together with someone | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>정치와 행정 장면에서 '동행하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A guide will accompany the class into the cave. | One guide will accompany the class into the cave. | Parents accompany young children during their first visit to the zoo. | One teacher will accompany the students to the city hall. |  |
| DAY27-24 | proportion | 비율<br>how big one part is compared to another | 수학 / 정비례와 반비례 (Direct and Inverse Proportion)<br>direct<br>한 양이 변할 때 다른 양이 일정한 비율로 변하는지 표와 그래프로 확인한다 | A large proportion of the field grew golden wheat. | The table shows water increasing in direct proportion to cups. | Speed and travel time maintain an inverse proportion relationship on graphs. | As tickets double, the total price rises in the same proportion. |  |
| DAY27-25 | household | 가정<br>all the people living in one home | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>정치와 행정 장면에서 '가정'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Every household on the street shared garden tools happily. | Every household on the street shared garden tools cheerfully. | Every single household in the neighborhood participates in weekly recycling collections. | Each household received one recycling bin from the city office. |  |
| DAY27-26 | absence | 부재<br>when someone or something is not there | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>정치와 행정 장면에서 '부재'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The absence of wind made the lake perfectly still. | Today, the absence of wind made the lake perfectly still. | His unexpected absence from the meeting was due to sudden illness. | In the mayor's absence, the deputy answered the residents' questions. |  |
| DAY27-27 | primary | 주요한<br>first and most important | fallback<br>word_fallback<br>한 장면에서 '주요한'의 확정 뜻이 다른 뜻으로 오해되지 않게 직접 보여 준다 | The primary goal was finishing the treehouse before winter. | Before winter, the primary goal was finishing the treehouse. | Education is the primary factor in reducing youth unemployment across regions. | Our primary goal this month is finishing the playground repair. |  |
| DAY27-28 | spot | 발견하다<br>to see something small or hidden | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>정치와 행정 장면에서 '발견하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Can you spot the poison dart frog among the leaves? | Today, can you spot the poison dart frog among the leaves? | Bird watchers can spot colorful migratory ducks in the wetland marsh. | Sharp eyes spot the small crack near the stair rail. |  |
| DAY27-29 | bother | 귀찮게 하다<br>to trouble someone who is busy | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>정치와 행정 장면에서 '귀찮게 하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Please do not bother the bees near the flowers. | Kindly do not bother the bees close to the flowers. | Loud lawn mowers can bother residents sleeping on quiet Sunday mornings. | Please do not bother the clerk while the forms are counted. |  |
| DAY27-30 | border | 국경<br>the line where two lands meet | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>정치와 행정 장면에서 '국경'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A river forms the border between two green countries. | One river forms the border between two green countries. | Passports are checked when crossing the international border between two nations. | Travelers show passports at the border before entering the country. |  |

## DAY 27 · set 4 · 사회 / 정치와 행정

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY27-31 | available | 이용 가능한<br>ready and able to be used | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>정치와 행정 장면에서 '이용 가능한'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Free bicycles are available beside the small train station. | Free bicycles are available next to the small train station. | Fresh drinking water is readily available from public water fountains downtown. | Free bicycles are available at the station until six o'clock. |  |
| DAY27-32 | harbor | 항구<br>a safe place where boats stay | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>정치와 행정 장면에서 '항구'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Fishing boats rested inside the calm harbor at dawn. | Fishing boats rested within the calm harbor at dawn. | Fishing boats returned to the calm harbor before the evening storm. | Fishing boats rest inside the small harbor during strong wind. |  |
| DAY27-33 | garage | 차고<br>a place where cars are kept | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>정치와 행정 장면에서 '차고'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The old garage held bicycles, boxes, and paint cans. | Today, the old garage held bicycles, boxes, and paint cans. | He parked his electric bicycle inside the clean residential garage building. | Our neighbor parks the delivery van inside a narrow garage. |  |
| DAY27-34 | notion | 개념<br>an idea in someone's mind | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>정치와 행정 장면에서 '개념'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | She had a fun notion about painting the mailbox. | A student had a fun notion about painting the mailbox. | Ancient philosophers explored the philosophical notion of justice and moral virtue. | The notion that recycling costs nothing is simply not correct. |  |
| DAY27-35 | suburb | 교외<br>a quiet area outside a big city | 사회 / 지속 가능한 도시 (Sustainable Cities)<br>contextual<br>대중교통·녹지·에너지 절약으로 도시의 환경 부담을 줄이는 방안을 비교한다 | The quiet suburb had wide streets and green yards. | A suburb reduces car use with trains and safe bike lanes. | Many families prefer living in a quiet green residential suburb neighborhood. | This suburb added bus lines so fewer families drive downtown. |  |
| DAY27-36 | resident | 주민<br>a person who lives in a place | 사회 / 지방 자치와 시민 참여 (Local Self-Government and Civic Participation)<br>direct<br>주민이 지역 문제를 지방 의회나 담당 기관에 제안하고 결과를 확인한다 | Every resident helped decorate the street for the festival. | Each resident asks the local council to repair the road. | Every local resident has the right to attend town council meetings. | Each resident can check the council's answer on the notice board. |  |
| DAY27-37 | investigate | 조사하다<br>to look carefully to find the truth | 국어 / 자료 신뢰성 (Source credibility)<br>contextual<br>자료의 작성자·날짜·근거를 확인하고 다른 출처와 대조한다 | The young detectives investigate the missing garden gnome mystery. | Students investigate the claim by checking authors, dates, and evidence. | Journalists investigate the origin of the false viral rumor on social. | We investigate who wrote this report before trusting its numbers. |  |
| DAY27-38 | reinforce | 강화하다<br>to make something stronger than before | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>정치와 행정 장면에서 '강화하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Thick ropes reinforce the swinging bridge over the stream. | Today, thick ropes reinforce the swinging bridge over the stream. | Engineers reinforce the concrete bridge pillars to withstand strong earthquake tremors. | Workers reinforce the old bridge with thick steel bars. |  |
| DAY27-39 | govern | 통치하다<br>to lead and make rules for people | 사회 / 국민 주권과 입헌주의 (Popular Sovereignty and Constitutionalism)<br>contextual<br>국가 권력이 국민의 동의와 헌법의 제한 안에서 행사됨을 확인한다 | Kind leaders govern the small island town very fairly. | Elected leaders govern under limits written in the constitution. | Elected democratic representatives govern the country according to constitutional law principles. | Leaders govern only within limits the constitution clearly sets. |  |
| DAY27-40 | rate | 속도<br>how fast something happens | 수학 / 그래프 해석 (Interpreting Graphs)<br>contextual<br>그래프의 축과 눈금을 읽고 값이 증가·감소하는 구간과 변화 속도를 설명한다 | The plant grew at an amazing rate this spring. | Students read the graph's rate from its rising line. | The speedometer indicates the rate of vehicle travel in kilometers per. | The graph shows the water level rising at a steady rate. |  |

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY28-01 | economic | 경제의<br>about money and how it is used | 사회 / 산업 구조와 글로벌 가치 사슬 (Industrial Structure and Global Value Chains)<br>contextual<br>생산 단계별 비용과 부가가치가 기업의 이익과 경제 활동으로 이어짐을 본다 | The economic news pleased every shopkeeper on the street. | Factories add economic value as raw cotton becomes clothing. | International trade drives economic growth and creates new jobs across nations. | Each economic step, from farm to shop, adds a little cost. |  |
| DAY28-02 | budget | 예산<br>a plan for how to spend money | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>경제와 금융 장면에서 '예산'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The class budget covered paint, brushes, and new paper. | Today, the class budget covered paint, brushes, and new paper. | The city council approved an annual budget for public library expansions. | Our class budget allows five thousand won for craft paper. |  |
| DAY28-03 | possible | 가능한<br>able to happen or be done | fallback<br>word_fallback<br>한 장면에서 '가능한'의 확정 뜻이 다른 뜻으로 오해되지 않게 직접 보여 준다 | It is possible to grow carrots in small pots. | Today, it is possible to grow carrots in small pots. | Technological innovation makes clean renewable energy possible at much lower costs. | It is possible to finish this poster before lunch. |  |
| DAY28-04 | income | 수입<br>money a person earns from working | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>경제와 금융 장면에서 '수입'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The farmer's income came from apples and fresh honey. | Today, the farmer's income came from apples and fresh honey. | Households save a portion of their monthly income for future emergencies. | The bakery's monthly income grew after the new bus stop opened. |  |
| DAY28-05 | crisis | 위기<br>a difficult time needing quick help | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>경제와 금융 장면에서 '위기'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The garden crisis ended when rain finally arrived. | When rain finally arrived, the garden crisis ended. | Coordinated government intervention helped stabilize the national economic banking crisis swiftly. | The water crisis ended when the town repaired the main pipe. |  |
| DAY28-06 | trade | 무역<br>the buying and selling of goods | 사회 / 자원 수출과 상호의존 (Resource Exports and Interdependence)<br>direct<br>자원 수출국과 수입국이 무역을 통해 서로 의존하는 관계를 표시한다 | Ships carried salt and cloth in the old trade. | Two countries trade oil and grain, depending on each other. | Maritime trade routes facilitate exchange of raw materials between world continents. | Trade brings coffee here and sends our machines to other countries. |  |
| DAY28-07 | offer | 제안하다<br>to hold something out for someone | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>경제와 금융 장면에서 '제안하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The baker will offer warm rolls to early customers. | Today, the baker will offer warm rolls to early customers. | Local businesses offer special discounts to students carrying school identification cards. | Shopkeepers offer a smaller size to customers with less money. |  |
| DAY28-08 | specific | 구체적인<br>one exact thing, not a general one | fallback<br>word_fallback<br>한 장면에서 '구체적인'의 확정 뜻이 다른 뜻으로 오해되지 않게 직접 보여 준다 | She wanted a specific blue button for the coat. | A student wanted a specific blue button for the coat. | Please give a specific example to support your written essay argument. | We need one specific size, not just any large box. |  |
| DAY28-09 | several | 여러 개의<br>more than two but not many | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>경제와 금융 장면에서 '여러 개의'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Several kites floated above the wide green hillside. | Today, several kites floated above the wide green hillside. | The university library holds several rare manuscripts from the fourteenth century. | Several coins rolled under the counter during the busy morning. |  |
| DAY28-10 | severe | 심한<br>very strong and hard to bear | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>경제와 금융 장면에서 '심한'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Severe cold kept the little birds inside the barn. | Severe cold kept the small birds within the barn. | The severe winter blizzard caused widespread electrical power outages across districts. | Severe cold last winter raised the heating cost sharply. |  |

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |

| DAY28-12 | wealth | 부<br>a large amount of money or goods | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>경제와 금융 장면에서 '부'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The kingdom's wealth was stored in golden grain barns. | The kingdom's wealth was stored in yellow grain barns. | Wise investments and careful savings generate long-term family financial wealth securely. | The old story says the farmer shared wealth with neighbors. |  |

| DAY28-14 | merchant | 상인<br>a person who buys and sells goods | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>경제와 금융 장면에서 '상인'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | A traveling merchant sold spices from a colorful cart. | One traveling merchant sold spices from a colorful cart. | The spice merchant traveled across ancient trading routes to sell goods. | One merchant sold silk here two hundred years ago. |  |
| DAY28-15 | finance | 자금을 대다<br>to give money for a big plan | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>경제와 금융 장면에서 '자금을 대다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The town will finance a new bridge this year. | Today, the town will finance a new bridge this year. | Banks finance major infrastructure projects through long-term construction loans safely. | Parents will finance the new library shelves with a small sale. |  |
| DAY28-16 | derive | 얻다<br>to get something from another thing | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>경제와 금융 장면에서 '얻다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | We derive sweet syrup from tall green maple trees. | The class derive sweet syrup from tall green maple trees. | Plants derive essential metabolic energy directly from absorbed sunlight during photosynthesis. | We derive most cooking oil from beans grown nearby. |  |
| DAY28-17 | credit | 신용, 칭찬, 학점<br>praise or approval given to someone for an achievement | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>경제와 금융 장면에서 '신용, 칭찬, 학점'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The teacher gave him credit for his creative science poster idea. | The teacher gives Mia credit for solving the difficult puzzle. | The diligent student deserves full credit for her outstanding science project. | The whole team received credit for finishing the mural early. |  |
| DAY28-18 | substitute | 대체하다<br>to use one thing instead of another | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>경제와 금융 장면에서 '대체하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | You can substitute honey for sugar in this recipe. | Today, you can substitute honey for sugar in this recipe. | Chefs substitute honey for refined sugar in healthy dessert recipes naturally. | Bakers substitute honey for sugar in this simple bread. |  |
| DAY28-19 | debt | 빚<br>money that you must pay back | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>경제와 금융 장면에서 '빚'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The small debt was repaid with two baskets of eggs. | Today, the small debt was repaid with two baskets of eggs. | Responsible borrowers pay off their bank debt on schedule each month. | I paid my small debt to the club treasurer today. |  |
| DAY28-20 | bill | 청구서<br>a paper showing money you must pay | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>경제와 금융 장면에서 '청구서'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The water bill increased a lot during the hot summer. | Today, the water bill increased a lot during the hot summer. | The electricity bill shows our household energy consumption for last month. | Our electricity bill arrived showing last month's exact usage. |  |

## DAY 28 · set 3 · 사회 / 경제와 금융

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY28-21 | cancel | 취소하다<br>to stop a plan that was made | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>경제와 금융 장면에서 '취소하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Heavy fog made the sailors cancel the morning trip. | Today, heavy fog made the sailors cancel the morning trip. | Bad weather forced the airline to cancel all outgoing flights today. | Heavy snow made the market cancel Saturday's outdoor sale. |  |
| DAY28-22 | replace | 교체하다<br>to put a new thing in place | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>경제와 금융 장면에서 '교체하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The carpenter will replace the cracked wooden step today. | Now, the carpenter will replace the cracked wooden step today. | Technicians replace worn mechanical gears with durable stainless steel components carefully. | Workers replace the broken cash drawer with a new one. |  |
| DAY28-23 | property | 재산<br>things that belong to a person | 사회 / 공법, 사법과 사회법 (Public, Private, and Social Law)<br>direct<br>국가와 개인 또는 개인 사이 관계를 보고 적용할 법 영역을 분류한다 | The old bicycle was her favorite property of all. | A property dispute between neighbors belongs to private law. | Civil law protects private property rights and regulates commercial business agreements. | This low fence marks where one neighbor's property clearly ends. |  |

| DAY28-33 | overall | 전반적인<br>counting everything all together | fallback<br>word_fallback<br>한 장면에서 '전반적인'의 확정 뜻이 다른 뜻으로 오해되지 않게 직접 보여 준다 | The overall plan for the festival looked truly wonderful. | Today, the overall plan for the festival looked truly wonderful. | The overall performance of our school sports team improved significantly this. | The overall cost includes paper, ink, and delivery together. |  |

| DAY29-04 | convey | 전달하다<br>to carry or share something with others | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>산업과 경영 장면에서 '전달하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Long belts convey boxes across the busy packing room. | Today, long belts convey boxes across the busy packing room. | Poets use vivid imagery to convey deep emotional thoughts to readers. | Long belts convey boxes from the packing room to trucks. |  |
| DAY29-05 | international | 국제적인<br>between different countries of the world | 사회 / 세계화와 글로컬라이제이션 (Globalization and Glocalization)<br>contextual<br>서로 다른 나라 사이의 상품·사람·문화 이동이 지역 생활을 바꾸는 사례를 본다 | The international fair showed foods from many faraway lands. | An international market brings foreign foods to local neighborhoods. | Global supply chains facilitate international trade of consumer electronics across continents. | This international shop sells Korean tea beside Indian spices. |  |

| DAY29-28 | combine | 결합하다<br>to put two things together as one | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>산업과 경영 장면에서 '결합하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Cooks combine flour and water to make soft dough. | Today, cooks combine flour and water to make soft dough. | Chefs combine fresh basil, pine nuts, and olive oil for sauce. | Cooks combine rice flour and water to make soft dough. |  |
| DAY29-29 | compete | 경쟁하다<br>to try to win against others | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>산업과 경영 장면에서 '경쟁하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Six friendly teams compete in the summer boat race. | Today, six friendly teams compete in the summer boat race. | Athletes compete with great determination during the annual statewide track championship. | Three bakeries compete by offering fresher bread each morning. |  |
| DAY29-30 | complete | 완성하다<br>to finish something all the way | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>산업과 경영 장면에서 '완성하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The class will complete the big mural this week. | The class will complete the large mural this week. | Students must complete their assigned homework tasks before playing video games. | Workers complete the last shelf before the shop opens Friday. |  |

## DAY 29 · set 4 · 사회 / 산업과 경영

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY29-31 | advance | 전진하다<br>to move forward toward something | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>산업과 경영 장면에서 '전진하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The hikers advance slowly up the steep rocky path. | The hikers advance gently up the steep rocky path. | Troops advance cautiously across the rugged terrain during scheduled military exercises. | The parade will advance slowly toward the market square. |  |
| DAY29-32 | imply | 암시하다<br>to show something without saying it directly | 국어 / 추론 (Inference)<br>direct<br>글에 나온 단서와 배경지식을 연결해 드러나지 않은 뜻을 추론한다 | Dark clouds imply that rain will arrive very soon. | Today, dark clouds imply that rain will arrive very soon. | Her subtle facial expressions imply subtle disagreement with the proposed plan. | Empty shelves imply that this bread sold out very early. |  |
| DAY29-33 | guarantee | 보장하다<br>to promise that something will surely happen | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>산업과 경영 장면에서 '보장하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The shop will guarantee this umbrella for two years. | Today, the shop will guarantee this umbrella for two years. | Product warranties guarantee free repairs if defects occur within one year. | This shop will guarantee the umbrella for one whole year. |  |
| DAY29-34 | chief | 주요한<br>most important of all | fallback<br>word_fallback<br>한 장면에서 '주요한'의 확정 뜻이 다른 뜻으로 오해되지 않게 직접 보여 준다 | The chief reason for the trip was seeing whales. | Today, the chief reason for the trip was seeing whales. | Rice cultivation is the chief source of agricultural income in village. | The chief reason for the delay was heavy morning traffic. |  |
| DAY29-35 | thrive | 번성하다<br>to grow very well and be healthy | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>산업과 경영 장면에서 '번성하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Herbs thrive on a sunny warm kitchen windowsill. | Today, herbs thrive on a sunny warm kitchen windowsill. | Hardy desert cactus plants thrive under intense sunlight with minimal watering. | Small tea shops thrive near the busy train station. |  |
| DAY29-36 | gain | 얻다<br>to get more of something good | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>산업과 경영 장면에서 '얻다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Runners gain strength by practicing a little daily. | Today, runners gain strength by practicing a little daily. | Dedicated study helps students gain valuable knowledge and deep conceptual understanding. | Careful workers gain more skill after each training week. |  |
| DAY29-37 | overlook | 간과하다<br>to fail to notice something | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>산업과 경영 장면에서 '간과하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Do not overlook the importance of clear communication in the team. | Today, do not overlook the importance of clear communication in the team. | Careful proofreaders never overlook minor spelling errors in published textbook editions. | Do not overlook the small crack inside this wooden box. |  |
| DAY29-38 | disappear | 사라지다<br>to go away and not be seen | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>산업과 경영 장면에서 '사라지다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Morning mist will disappear once the sun rises higher. | Today, morning mist will disappear once the sun rises higher. | Puddle water droplets will disappear through evaporation under the warm sun. | Morning fog will disappear once sunlight reaches the valley floor. |  |
| DAY29-39 | initial | 처음의<br>happening at the very beginning | fallback<br>word_fallback<br>한 장면에서 '처음의'의 확정 뜻이 다른 뜻으로 오해되지 않게 직접 보여 준다 | The initial plan was simply planting six small trees. | Today, the initial plan was simply planting six small trees. | Her initial test score was low, but subsequent scores improved dramatically. | Our initial plan used ten boxes, but twelve arrived instead. |  |
| DAY29-40 | domestic | 가정의<br>about the home or your own country | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>산업과 경영 장면에서 '가정의'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Cats and dogs are common domestic animals in homes. | Parents share domestic tasks such as cooking and cleaning. | Washing dishes and sweeping floors are common domestic chores in households. | These domestic tools include a broom, a mop, and buckets. |  |

## DAY 30 · set 1 · 사회 / 언론과 뉴스

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY30-01 | magazine | 잡지<br>a thin book with pictures and stories | 국어 / 매체 문해력 (Media literacy)<br>direct<br>뉴스의 출처·표현·빠진 정보를 확인해 메시지를 비판적으로 읽는다 | A garden magazine sat open on the wooden table. | Readers check the magazine author, evidence, and missing viewpoints. | She subscribed to a monthly science magazine filled with colorful photographs. | This magazine lists its writers and sources on every page. |  |
| DAY30-02 | article | 기사<br>a piece of writing about one subject | 국어 / 정보 전달 글쓰기 (Informative writing)<br>direct<br>독자가 절차나 사실을 이해하도록 정확한 정보를 순서 있게 쓴다 | The article told how bees make golden sweet honey. | The article explains each beekeeping step in a clear order. | The journalist published an informative article about regional wildlife conservation efforts. | The article explains each rescue step in the correct order. |  |
| DAY30-03 | press | 누르다, 언론<br>to apply firm, steady weight or force against something | 과학 / 힘과 알짜힘 (Force and Net Force)<br>direct<br>물체에 작용하는 힘의 크기와 방향을 화살표로 합성한다 | Please press the green button to start the elevator motor. | Students press opposite sides of a cart to compare forces. | Use your palm to press the dough flat on the board. | We press down on the scale and watch the number rise. |  |
| DAY30-04 | advertise | 광고하다<br>to tell people about something you sell | 사회 / 미디어 리터러시 (Media and Information Literacy)<br>direct<br>뉴스의 출처와 근거를 확인하고 거짓·편향 정보를 사실과 구별한다 | Colorful posters advertise the fresh fruit at the market. | Posters advertise fruit, but shoppers verify each health claim. | Companies advertise their latest electronic products through online video streaming platforms. | Shops advertise sales, so we check whether the prices really dropped. |  |
| DAY30-05 | recent | 최근의<br>happening a short time ago | 국어 / 자료 신뢰성 (Source credibility)<br>contextual<br>자료의 게시 날짜가 최근인지 확인해 현재 주장에 쓸 수 있는지 판단한다 | The recent rain filled every barrel in the yard. | Students check whether a recent article uses current evidence. | Check the recent publication date to ensure the source is current. | We used the most recent chart because older numbers changed. |  |
| DAY30-06 | diverse | 다양한<br>of many different kinds | 국어 / 재현과 공정성 (Representation and fairness)<br>contextual<br>매체에 서로 다른 집단과 관점이 편견 없이 다양하게 나타나는지 확인한다 | A diverse group of birds visited the garden feeder. | The film shows diverse families without unfair stereotypes. | Media broadcasts should include diverse cultural perspectives without unfair stereotyping prejudice. | This news program shows diverse voices from young and older residents. |  |
| DAY30-07 | document | 문서<br>an important paper with information on it | 국어 / 정보 전달 글쓰기 (Informative writing)<br>direct<br>독자가 절차나 사실을 이해하도록 정확한 정보를 순서 있게 쓴다 | The old document showed a map of the village. | The document presents facts and steps in a clear order. | The official government document was signed by all five international delegates. | One document lists every safety rule in numbered order. |  |
| DAY30-08 | expert | 전문가<br>a person who knows a subject very well | 국어 / 자료 신뢰성 (Source credibility)<br>contextual<br>자료의 작성자·날짜·근거를 확인하고 다른 출처와 대조한다 | A bird expert explained the calls of forest owls. | Students confirm the expert has experience and reliable evidence. | A medical expert verified the scientific accuracy of the health report. | The article quotes a weather expert and names the research center. |  |
| DAY30-09 | explore | 탐험하다<br>to travel around a new place | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>언론과 뉴스 장면에서 '탐험하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The children explore the cave with bright yellow lamps. | Several children explore the cave with bright yellow lamps. | Underwater robotic vehicles explore deep oceanic trenches beneath miles of water. | Reporters explore the old cave and describe what they find. |  |
| DAY30-10 | explode | 폭발하다<br>to burst open suddenly with a bang | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>언론과 뉴스 장면에서 '폭발하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Popcorn kernels explode into fluffy white puffs quickly. | Popcorn kernels explode into fluffy white puffs rapidly. | Volatile gas leaks can explode if exposed to open candle flames. | Popcorn kernels explode into white puffs inside the hot pot. |  |

## DAY 30 · set 2 · 사회 / 언론과 뉴스

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY30-11 | handle | 다루다<br>to deal with something carefully | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>언론과 뉴스 장면에서 '다루다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Please handle these eggs gently on the way home. | Kindly handle these eggs gently on the way home. | Laboratory assistants handle fragile glass test tubes with utmost delicate care. | Please handle these old photographs with clean dry hands. |  |
| DAY30-12 | phrase | 구절<br>a small group of words together | 국어 / 문장 구조 (Sentence structure)<br>direct<br>낱말과 구절을 주어와 서술어 관계가 분명한 문장으로 배열한다 | The parrot learned one funny phrase from the sailor. | Students place each phrase beside a clear subject and verb. | The teacher underlined an important prepositional phrase in the sample sentence. | We moved this phrase so the subject sits before the verb. |  |
| DAY30-13 | annoy | 짜증나게 하다<br>to bother someone in a small way | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>언론과 뉴스 장면에서 '짜증나게 하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Buzzing flies annoy the cows in the warm field. | Today, buzzing flies annoy the cows in the warm field. | Loud background chatter can annoy students trying to study in library. | Repeated pop-up boxes annoy readers of this news site. |  |
| DAY30-14 | obvious | 분명한<br>very easy to see or understand | fallback<br>word_fallback<br>한 장면에서 '분명한'의 확정 뜻이 다른 뜻으로 오해되지 않게 직접 보여 준다 | It was obvious that the puppy wanted a walk. | Today, it was obvious that the puppy wanted a walk. | It was obvious that the wet grass resulted from morning dew. | The missing date made the mistake obvious to every reader. |  |
| DAY30-15 | mention | 언급하다<br>to say something briefly while talking | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>언론과 뉴스 장면에서 '언급하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Did the guide mention the old bridge near town? | Today, did the guide mention the old bridge near town? | Did the speaker mention the scheduled date for our class trip? | The reporter did mention two names during the short interview. |  |
| DAY30-16 | entertain | 즐겁게 하다<br>to make people enjoy their time | 국어 / 복합양식 매체 제작 (Multimodal media production)<br>contextual<br>대본·영상·소리를 조합해 청중이 즐길 수 있는 매체 작품을 만든다 | Puppets entertain young children at the library every weekend. | Actors combine script, music, and video to entertain the audience. | The amusing puppet show managed to entertain children throughout the afternoon. | Our short film mixes music and jokes to entertain classmates. |  |
| DAY30-17 | detail | 세부 사항<br>a small part of something bigger | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>언론과 뉴스 장면에서 '세부 사항'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Every tiny detail of the model ship looked perfect. | Today, every tiny detail of the model ship looked perfect. | The architect explained every architectural detail of the new school building. | One small detail in the photo showed the correct season. |  |
| DAY30-18 | conflict | 갈등<br>a disagreement between two people or sides | 사회 / 사회 갈등과 차별 (Social Conflict and Discrimination)<br>direct<br>집단 간 갈등과 차별의 원인을 찾고 평등한 해결 방안을 비교한다 | A small conflict about turns ended with a handshake. | Students find the conflict's cause and choose an equal solution. | Constructive dialogue helps resolve social conflict between opposing community interest groups. | The conflict began over water use, then both villages shared fairly. |  |
| DAY30-19 | thus | 따라서<br>for that reason | fallback<br>word_fallback<br>한 장면에서 '따라서'의 확정 뜻이 다른 뜻으로 오해되지 않게 직접 보여 준다 | Rain fell all night; thus, the river rose higher. | Today, rain fell all night; thus, the river rose higher. | The experiment conditions were identical; thus, the test results were consistent. | Rain stopped early; thus, the outdoor interview started on time. |  |
| DAY30-20 | medium | 매체<br>a way of sharing news or art | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>언론과 뉴스 장면에서 '매체'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Radio was the favorite medium in the small village. | Today, radio was the favorite medium in the small village. | Digital video is an interactive medium for sharing educational science lessons. | Radio is still the fastest medium in this mountain village. |  |

## DAY 30 · set 3 · 사회 / 언론과 뉴스

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY30-21 | depress | 우울하게 하다<br>to make someone feel sad | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>언론과 뉴스 장면에서 '우울하게 하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Bad exam results usually depress students, but warm pizza fixes everything quickly. | Bad exam results usually depress students, but warm pizza fixes everything rapidly. | Gloomy rainy weather can depress your mood during long winter weeks. | Sad news can depress readers, so editors add helpful information. |  |
| DAY30-22 | demand | 요구하다<br>to ask for something very firmly | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>언론과 뉴스 장면에서 '요구하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The fat sheep will demand fresh orange carrots after chewing its cud. | Residents demand safer crossings during the town meeting. | Consumers demand high quality and safety standards from food manufacturing companies. | Readers demand a correction when a story lists wrong numbers. |  |
| DAY30-23 | identify | 확인하다<br>to say exactly what something is | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>언론과 뉴스 장면에서 '확인하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Can you identify this bird by its blue feathers? | Today, can you identify this bird by its blue feathers? | Botanists use microscopic features to identify unknown wild flower plant species. | Editors identify each photograph before printing tomorrow's morning paper. |  |
| DAY30-24 | award | 상<br>a prize given for doing well | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>언론과 뉴스 장면에서 '상'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The young painter received an award for her mural. | Today, the young painter received an award for her mural. | The young author received a prestigious literary award for her novel. | Our school paper won an award for its clear writing. |  |
| DAY30-25 | regret | 후회하다<br>to feel sorry about something you did | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>언론과 뉴스 장면에서 '후회하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The rabbit may regret eating the whole carrot cake. | The small rabbit may regret eating the whole carrot cake. | He expressed deep regret for speaking harsh words to his classmate. | Writers regret printing the wrong name in yesterday's short notice. |  |
| DAY30-26 | regard | 여기다<br>to think about someone in a certain way | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>언론과 뉴스 장면에서 '여기다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Villagers regard the old oak as a special friend. | Today, villagers regard the old oak as a special friend. | Many historians regard the printing press as a monumental human invention. | Many readers regard this reporter as careful and very fair. |  |
| DAY30-27 | inquire | 문의하다<br>to ask politely about something | 국어 / 면담 (Interview)<br>contextual<br>목적에 맞는 질문을 하고 답을 기록한 뒤 추가 질문으로 확인한다 | Visitors inquire about the museum hours at the desk. | Interviewers inquire about one answer, then record a follow-up. | Prospective students inquire about application deadlines at the university admissions office. | We inquire about the schedule, then ask one follow-up question. |  |
| DAY30-28 | script | 대본<br>the written words for a play or show | 국어 / 복합양식 매체 제작 (Multimodal media production)<br>direct<br>대본·영상·소리 자료를 역할에 맞게 묶어 하나의 매체 자료를 만든다 | The actors practiced the script inside the small theater. | The script guides actors, sound, and pictures in one video. | Actors practiced their assigned dialogue lines from the theater play script. | Our script tells each speaker when the background music starts. |  |
| DAY30-29 | decline | 감소하다<br>to become smaller or fewer | 수학 / 그래프 해석 (Interpreting Graphs)<br>contextual<br>그래프의 축과 눈금을 읽고 값이 증가·감소하는 구간과 변화 속도를 설명한다 | Bee numbers decline when wildflowers disappear from the meadow. | Students mark where values decline fastest on the graph. | Line graphs indicate that birth rates decline across several developed countries. | On this line graph, temperatures decline steeply after seven o'clock. |  |
| DAY30-30 | declare | 선언하다<br>to say something loudly and clearly | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>언론과 뉴스 장면에서 '선언하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The judges will declare the winner after one hour. | After one hour, the judges will declare the winner. | Officials declare the opening of the international youth sports competition games. | The mayor will declare the new library open next Saturday. |  |

## DAY 30 · set 4 · 사회 / 언론과 뉴스

| ID | word | 확정 뜻·정의 | 배정 개념·직접 단서 | baseline | codex | antigravity | claude | 선택 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| DAY30-31 | deceive | 속이다<br>to make someone believe something untrue | 사회 / 미디어 리터러시 (Media and Information Literacy)<br>contextual<br>사람을 속이는 정보의 출처와 근거를 확인해 사실과 구별한다 | The magic trick can deceive even the sharpest eyes. | False headlines deceive readers who never check their sources. | Scammers create fake websites to deceive consumers into revealing bank passwords. | Fake photos can deceive readers, so we check the original source. |  |
| DAY30-32 | external | 외부의<br>on the outside of something | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>언론과 뉴스 장면에서 '외부의'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The external walls of the cottage were painted white. | Today, the external walls of the cottage were painted white. | The external brick facade of the building was cleaned thoroughly yesterday. | The external cover of this camera has small rubber grips. |  |
| DAY30-33 | instantly | 즉시<br>right away, with no waiting | fallback<br>word_fallback<br>한 장면에서 '즉시'의 확정 뜻이 다른 뜻으로 오해되지 않게 직접 보여 준다 | The puppy instantly noticed the treat behind her back. | The young dog instantly noticed the treat behind her back. | Smartphones deliver emergency weather notifications instantly without any noticeable transmission delay. | The printer starts instantly when we press the green button. |  |
| DAY30-34 | norm | 표준<br>the usual way things are done | 사회 / 사회화와 사회적 정체성 (Socialization and Social Identity)<br>direct<br>가족·학교·친구 관계에서 규범과 정체성을 배우는 모습을 찾는다 | Sharing snacks became the norm in this friendly class. | Friends teach the class norm by taking turns and sharing. | Following shared ethical rules is an established cultural norm in society. | Greeting elders first is a norm students learn at home. |  |
| DAY30-35 | analyze | 분석하다<br>to study something carefully part by part | 사회 / 미디어 리터러시 (Media and Information Literacy)<br>contextual<br>뉴스의 출처·근거·표현을 부분별로 분석해 신뢰성을 판단한다 | Scientists analyze the soil before planting new young trees. | Students analyze a news source, evidence, wording, and omissions. | Media researchers analyze news articles to detect subtle political bias framing. | We analyze the headline, the source, and the missing numbers separately. |  |
| DAY30-36 | clarify | 명확히 하다<br>to make something easier to understand | 국어 / 설명과 논증 (Explanation and argument)<br>direct<br>원인과 결과 또는 주장과 근거의 연결을 분명히 설명한다 | The teacher will clarify the rules once more today. | The writer uses evidence to clarify why the river changed. | The teacher used simple diagrams to clarify the complex mathematical concept. | One added sentence will clarify why the road closed yesterday. |  |
| DAY30-37 | defend | 방어하다<br>to keep something safe from trouble | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>언론과 뉴스 장면에서 '방어하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Tall hedges defend the garden from cold winter winds. | Today, tall hedges defend the garden from cold winter winds. | Castle walls were built to defend the ancient city against invaders. | Thick covers defend these old newspapers from sunlight and dust. |  |
| DAY30-38 | reputation | 평판<br>what people generally think about someone | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>언론과 뉴스 장면에서 '평판'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The bakery has a fine reputation for soft bread. | Today, the bakery has a fine reputation for soft bread. | The hospital earned an excellent reputation for compassionate patient medical care. | This bakery earned a good reputation for fresh morning bread. |  |
| DAY30-39 | exclude | 제외하다<br>to leave something or someone out | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>언론과 뉴스 장면에서 '제외하다'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | Please do not exclude anyone from the team game. | Kindly do not exclude anyone from the team game. | Statistical studies exclude flawed data samples to maintain accurate research findings. | Editors exclude any photograph without a clear date and place. |  |
| DAY30-40 | net | 그물<br>a loose cloth made of knotted string | fallback<br>day_fallback<br>언론과 뉴스 장면에서 '그물'의 뜻이 행동·사물·상태로 바로 보이게 한다 | The fishers pulled a wide net onto the boat. | Today, the fishers pulled a wide net onto the boat. | Fishermen cast a large nylon net into the calm ocean water. | Fishers pulled the wide net onto the wooden boat deck. |  |
